`README.md`:

```md
# Iris

A zero-overhead, anti-cheat safe desktop overlay and modular HUD compositor for Windows.

Iris renders high-resolution transparent PNG and WebP overlays directly over borderless and windowed games with near-zero resource consumption. It is engineered as a standalone native utility built with Rust, Tauri 2, and React.

---

## Architectural Principles

Traditional game overlay tools (such as Overwolf, Discord Overlay, or RivaTuner Statistics Server) rely on DLL injection, DirectX/Vulkan swapchain hooking, or process memory inspection to draw graphics inside the game pipeline. This approach introduces input latency, destabilizes frame pacing, and frequently triggers anti-cheat false positives.

Iris operates on an entirely external, non-invasive model:

- **Zero DLL Hooking:** Never injects code into foreign game processes.
- **Zero Memory Reading:** Does not inspect, read, or modify game memory address spaces.
- **Native OS Compositing:** Employs Win32 hardware-accelerated layered topmost windows (`WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT | WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW`).
- **Anti-Cheat Safe:** Inherently compatible with Riot Vanguard, Easy Anti-Cheat, BattlEye, and Ricochet, operating with the same security profile as a native Windows notification or taskbar flyout.
- **Click-Through Transparency:** Mouse and keyboard inputs pass completely through the overlay directly into the game window with zero intercept delay.
- **Streamer Privacy (`WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE`):** Native Windows Display Affinity allows the overlay to remain visible on the player's physical monitor while being entirely omitted from OBS Studio, Discord screen shares, and Game Capture hooks.

---

## Interface Overview

### Vault (Asset & Profile Management)
![Iris Vault Interface](public/images/iris/Vault.png)

The Vault serves as a centralized digital asset repository for all transparent overlays, organized by game category (Overwatch 2, Valorant, Marvel Rivals, Apex Legends, Dead by Daylight, and Custom titles).
- Instant multi-category filtering, keyword search, and favorites pinning.
- Per-item persistent calibration profiles (X/Y coordinates, scale, opacity, hue, brightness, and contrast).
- Double-click to instantly engage an overlay into live play.

---

### Layering Studio (Modular HUD Compositor)
![Iris Layering Studio Interface](public/images/iris/Layering.png)

A multi-layer composition workspace for stacking, transforming, and synchronizing complex HUD packages.
- Visual positioning canvas with edge and center snapping guides.
- Layer stack management: reordering, grouping by HUD roles (reticles, minimaps, status frames, ult meters), duplication, and visibility toggling.
- Reference schematic backdrops to calibrate layout alignments against game UI proportions.
- Single-click deployment of the entire composite stack to the live overlay canvas.

---

### Display Controller (Targeting & Compositing)
![Iris Display Controller Interface](public/images/iris/Display.png)

Direct control over monitor selection, scaling modes, and active overlay state.
- Physical pixel coordinate mapping (`1/devicePixelRatio`) ensuring exact 1:1 pixel rendering at 1080p, 1440p, and 4K resolutions.
- Real-time global opacity slider and topmost window layer verification.
- Active game process detection and automated profile switching.

---

### Settings & Streamer Configuration
![Iris Settings Interface](public/images/iris/Settings.png)

Low-level application parameters and execution toggles.
- Global system hotkeys: instant overlay show/hide toggle (default F10), previous/next asset cycling, and role-based layer activation.
- Streamer Mode toggle utilizing `SetWindowDisplayAffinity`.
- Hardware acceleration toggle passing `--disable-gpu` flags to the underlying WebView2 compositing instance when minimizing GPU usage is paramount.
- Low-Resource Mode reducing background process polling frequency.

---

## Technical Specifications

| Parameter | Specification |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Operating System** | Windows 10 (Build 19041+) / Windows 11 (64-bit) |
| **Runtime Core** | Rust 1.77+ with Tauri 2 Framework |
| **Frontend UI** | React 18, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS, Vite |
| **Binary Footprint** | ~5.1 MB standalone portable executable (`Iris.exe`) |
| **Privileges Required** | Standard User (Does NOT require Run as Administrator) |
| **Supported Formats** | 32-bit Transparent PNG, Animated WebP, Static WebP with alpha channel |
| **Display Modes** | Borderless Windowed, Windowed, Fullscreen (FSO / DirectFlip) |
| **Stream Hiding** | Win32 `WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE` (Windows 10 Version 2004+) |

---

## Codebase Structure

```
Iris/
├── index.html                  Control UI entry point
├── overlay.html                Frameless transparent click-through window entry point
├── src/
│   ├── App.tsx                 Application state coordinator and tab navigation
│   ├── api.ts                  Type-safe Tauri IPC bridge invokes
│   ├── layers.ts               Composite stack algorithms and transform math
│   ├── overlay.tsx             High-performance single-canvas composition renderer
│   ├── OverlayLayer.tsx        DOM preview renderer for single asset inspector
│   ├── types.ts                Shared TypeScript data contracts and manifests
│   ├── util.ts                 Search indexers, sorting routines, and file helpers
│   ├── styles.css              Dark matte Venus Visuals design tokens
│   └── components/
│       ├── Display.tsx         Display targeting and monitor configuration view
│       ├── Layering.tsx        Multi-track HUD layering studio canvas
│       ├── LayerDrawer.tsx     Drawer selector for adding assets into stack
│       ├── LayerPanel.tsx      Layer properties, z-index, and transform sliders
│       ├── SceneView.tsx       Interactive viewport with drag-to-position anchors
│       ├── Settings.tsx        Global hotkeys, streamer mode, and storage management
│       ├── TopBar.tsx          App header, active game badge, and tab switcher
│       └── Vault.tsx           Digital asset library grid, filters, and card actions
└── src-tauri/
    ├── Cargo.toml              Rust dependencies and optimization profile
    ├── tauri.conf.json         Window declarations and security permissions
    └── src/
        ├── main.rs             Application bootstrap, WebView2 flags, lifecycle events
        ├── win32.rs            Win32 API bindings: styles, placement, affinity, refresh rates
        ├── overlay.rs          Overlay window lifecycle, click-through, and canvas sync
        ├── vault.rs            Atomic JSON manifest persistence and asset caching
        ├── system.rs           Global keyboard hooks, process monitors, display enumeration
        └── model.rs            Rust data models matching frontend TypeScript types
```

---

## Local Development & Compilation

### Prerequisites
1. **Rust:** Current stable toolchain (`rustup default stable`)
2. **Node.js:** Node.js 18 or higher with `npm`
3. **WebView2 Runtime:** Installed natively on Windows 10/11
4. **C++ Build Tools:** Visual Studio C++ Build Tools or MSVC toolchain

### Development Mode
```powershell
npm install
npm run tauri dev
```

### Production Build
```powershell
npm run tauri build
```
The optimized portable binary will be generated at `src-tauri/target/release/Iris.exe`. Release profiles are configured with Link-Time Optimization (`lto = true`), symbol stripping (`strip = true`), single codegen units, and abort-on-panic to minimize binary size and latency.

---

## Data Storage

Application data is stored atomically in `%APPDATA%\art.veevisuals.iris\`:
- `vault_manifest.json`: Asset metadata, folder hierarchies, and layer presets.
- `settings.json`: User keybindings, monitor selections, and display preferences.
- `vault_assets/`: Scaled transparent graphics stored locally.
- `vault_thumbs/`: Pre-rendered high-performance UI thumbnails.

---

## License & Credits

Engineered for [Venus Visuals](https://veevisuals.art). Distributed under the MIT License.

</html>

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <title>Iris Overlay</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="/src/styles.css" />
    <style>
      @keyframes irisRainbow {
        0% { filter: hue-rotate(0deg); }
        100% { filter: hue-rotate(360deg); }
      }
      html, body, #root { margin: 0; padding: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; background: transparent !important; overflow: hidden; cursor: none; user-select: none; }
    </style>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="root"></div>
    <script type="module" src="/src/overlay.tsx"></script>
  </body>
</html>

```

[package]
name = "iris"
version = "0.1.0"
description = "Iris - lightweight in-game HUD & overlay utility"
edition = "2021"

[build-dependencies]
tauri-build = { version = "2", features = [] }

[dependencies]
tauri = { version = "2", features = ["tray-icon", "protocol-asset"] }
tauri-plugin-global-shortcut = "2"
serde = { version = "1", features = ["derive"] }
serde_json = "1"
tauri-plugin-single-instance = "2"
tauri-plugin-updater = "2"
tauri-plugin-process = "2"

[target.'cfg(windows)'.dependencies]
windows = { version = "0.61", features = [
  "Win32_Foundation",
  "Win32_Graphics_Gdi",
  "Win32_System_Diagnostics_ToolHelp",
  "Win32_UI_WindowsAndMessaging",
] }

[profile.release]
opt-level = "s"
lto = true
codegen-units = 1
panic = "abort"
strip = true

`src-tauri/src/main.rs`:

```rs
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

mod model;
mod overlay;
mod system;
mod updater;
mod vault;
mod win32;

use model::{AppState, Settings};
use tauri::{Emitter, Manager, WindowEvent};

fn main() {
    let root = model::default_root();
    let settings: Settings = model::read_json(&root.join("settings.json")).unwrap_or_default();

    // WebView2 flags must be set before any webview is created.
    let mut browser_args = String::from(
        "--disable-background-timer-throttling --disable-backgrounding-occluded-windows --disable-renderer-backgrounding",
    );
    if !settings.hardware_acceleration {
        browser_args.push_str(" --disable-gpu");
    }
    std::env::set_var("WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS", browser_args);

    tauri::Builder::default()
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_global_shortcut::Builder::new().build())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_single_instance::init(|app, argv, _cwd| {
            system::show_main(app);
            for arg in argv {
                if arg.starts_with("iris://") {
                    let _ = app.emit_to("main", "iris-protocol-url", arg);
                }
            }
        }))
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_process::init())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_updater::Builder::new().build())
        .manage(AppState::new(root, settings))
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
            vault::vault_load,
            vault::import_asset,
            vault::save_thumbnail,
            vault::vault_update_item,
            vault::vault_delete_item,
            vault::vault_create_folder,
            vault::vault_delete_folder,
            vault::open_vault_dir,
            vault::preset_save,
            vault::preset_delete,
            overlay::overlay_apply,
            overlay::overlay_get,
            system::list_monitors,
            system::settings_get,
            system::settings_save,
            updater::updater_check,
            updater::updater_download_and_install,
            updater::updater_restart,
            updater::updater_get_status,
        ])
        .setup(|app| {
            let handle = app.handle().clone();
            overlay::create_window(&handle)?;
            system::build_tray(&handle)?;
            system::register_hotkeys(&handle);
            system::register_url_protocol();
            system::spawn_game_watcher(handle.clone());
            updater::check_on_startup(handle.clone());
            if !std::env::args().any(|a| a == "--minimized") {
                system::show_main(&handle);
            }
            // If opened via iris:// custom URL protocol, dispatch to UI
            for arg in std::env::args() {
                if arg.starts_with("iris://") {
                    let h = handle.clone();
                    std::thread::spawn(move || {
                        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(800));
                        let _ = h.emit_to("main", "iris-protocol-url", arg);
                    });
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        })
        .on_window_event(|window, event| {
            if let WindowEvent::CloseRequested { api, .. } = event {
                if window.label() == "main" {
                    let app = window.app_handle();
                    let to_tray = app.state::<AppState>().settings.lock().unwrap().close_to_tray;
                    if to_tray {
                        api.prevent_close();
                        let _ = window.hide();
                    } else {
                        app.exit(0);
                    }
                }
            }
        })
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running Iris");
}

```

`src-tauri/src/model.rs`:

```rs
//! Shared data model + persistence helpers (vault_manifest.json, settings.json).

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    fs,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    sync::{
        atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64},
        Mutex,
    },
};

pub const IDENTIFIER: &str = "art.veevisuals.iris";

/// `%APPDATA%\art.veevisuals.iris` - identical to Tauri's `app_data_dir`, but
/// resolvable before the app is built (needed for early WebView2 flags).
pub fn default_root() -> PathBuf {
    let base = std::env::var_os("APPDATA")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(std::env::temp_dir);
    base.join(IDENTIFIER)
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", default)]
pub struct Transform {
    pub x: f64,
    pub y: f64,
    /// percent, 100 = native pixel match
    pub scale: f64,
    /// percent
    pub opacity: f64,
    /// degrees
    pub hue: f64,
    /// percent
    pub brightness: f64,
    /// percent
    pub contrast: f64,
    /// continuous animated rainbow hue-shift
    pub chroma_cycle: bool,
    /// cycle duration in seconds (e.g. 6.0)
    pub chroma_speed: f64,
}

impl Default for Transform {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            x: 0.0,
            y: 0.0,
            scale: 100.0,
            opacity: 100.0,
            hue: 0.0,
            brightness: 100.0,
            contrast: 100.0,
            chroma_cycle: false,
            chroma_speed: 6.0,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct VaultItem {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub game: String,
    pub folder: Option<String>,
    pub file: String,
    pub thumb: Option<String>,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub created_at: u64,
    pub transform: Option<Transform>,
    /// element tag: "ult" | "map" | "health" | "hud" | "reticle"
    #[serde(default)]
    pub tag: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub favorite: bool,
}

/// One modular element inside a composite stack.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PresetLayer {
    pub id: String,
    pub vault_item_id: String,
    pub name: String,
    /// "base" | "ult" | "map" | "ability" | "crosshair" | "custom"
    pub role: String,
    /// higher = rendered in front
    pub z_index: i32,
    pub visible: bool,
    pub locked: bool,
    pub x: f64,
    pub y: f64,
    pub scale: f64,
    pub opacity: f64,
    pub hue: f64,
    /// non-destructive crop / flip / blend / link-group
    #[serde(default)]
    pub fx: LayerFx,
}

/// Non-destructive "Photoshop-style" layer effects.
/// Crop values are percentages of the source image trimmed from each edge.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, Default)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", default)]
pub struct LayerFx {
    pub crop_t: f64,
    pub crop_r: f64,
    pub crop_b: f64,
    pub crop_l: f64,
    pub flip_x: bool,
    pub flip_y: bool,
    /// "" / "normal" | "screen" | "color-dodge" | "overlay" | "soft-light" | "lighten" | "multiply"
    pub blend: String,
    /// layers sharing a group id move together
    pub group: Option<String>,
}

/// A saved multi-layer "Loadout".
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct CompositePreset {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub game: Option<String>,
    pub layers: Vec<PresetLayer>,
    /// monitor size the stack was authored on (thumbnail aspect)
    pub ref_w: u32,
    pub ref_h: u32,
    /// shown in the Vault tab with a STACK badge
    pub in_vault: bool,
    pub created_at: u64,
}

/// Resolved, ready-to-draw layer pushed to the overlay window.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SceneLayer {
    pub path: String,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub transform: Transform,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub fx: LayerFx,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct Manifest {
    pub version: u32,
    pub items: Vec<VaultItem>,
    pub folders: Vec<String>,
    pub presets: Vec<CompositePreset>,
}

impl Default for Manifest {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self { version: 1, items: vec![], folders: vec![], presets: vec![] }
    }
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", default)]
pub struct Settings {
    pub hotkey_toggle: String,
    pub hotkey_next: String,
    pub hotkey_prev: String,
    /// true => WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE (invisible to OBS / screenshots)
    pub hide_from_capture: bool,
    /// "topmost" | "game"
    pub target_mode: String,
    pub target_exe: String,
    pub auto_game_switch: bool,
    pub auto_hide_unfocused: bool,
    pub game_profiles: HashMap<String, String>,
    pub start_with_windows: bool,
    pub close_to_tray: bool,
    pub hardware_acceleration: bool,
    pub low_resource: bool,
    /// layer role -> global toggle hotkey
    pub layer_hotkeys: HashMap<String, String>,
    pub check_updates_on_startup: bool,
}

impl Default for Settings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            hotkey_toggle: "F10".into(),
            hotkey_next: "Ctrl+Alt+Right".into(),
            hotkey_prev: "Ctrl+Alt+Left".into(),
            hide_from_capture: false,
            target_mode: "topmost".into(),
            target_exe: "Overwatch.exe".into(),
            auto_game_switch: true,
            auto_hide_unfocused: false,
            game_profiles: HashMap::new(),
            start_with_windows: false,
            close_to_tray: false,
            hardware_acceleration: true,
            low_resource: false,
            layer_hotkeys: HashMap::new(),
            check_updates_on_startup: true,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, Default)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", default)]
pub struct OverlayState {
    pub enabled: bool,
    pub item_id: Option<String>,
    /// absolute path of the PNG/WebP in vault_assets
    pub path: Option<String>,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub transform: Transform,
    pub monitor: usize,
    /// when non-empty, the composite stack is drawn instead of `path`
    pub scene: Vec<SceneLayer>,
}

impl OverlayState {
    pub fn has_content(&self) -> bool {
        self.path.is_some() || !self.scene.is_empty()
    }
}

pub struct AppState {
    pub root: PathBuf,
    pub manifest: Mutex<Manifest>,
    pub settings: Mutex<Settings>,
    pub overlay: Mutex<OverlayState>,
    pub game_running: AtomicBool,
    pub last_rect: Mutex<Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)>>,
    pub counter: AtomicU64,
}

impl AppState {
    pub fn new(root: PathBuf, settings: Settings) -> Self {
        let _ = fs::create_dir_all(root.join("vault_assets"));
        let _ = fs::create_dir_all(root.join("vault_thumbs"));
        let manifest: Manifest = read_json(&root.join("vault_manifest.json")).unwrap_or_default();
        Self {
            root,
            manifest: Mutex::new(manifest),
            settings: Mutex::new(settings),
            overlay: Mutex::new(OverlayState::default()),
            game_running: AtomicBool::new(false),
            last_rect: Mutex::new(None),
            counter: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }
    pub fn assets_dir(&self) -> PathBuf { self.root.join("vault_assets") }
    pub fn thumbs_dir(&self) -> PathBuf { self.root.join("vault_thumbs") }
    pub fn manifest_path(&self) -> PathBuf { self.root.join("vault_manifest.json") }
    pub fn settings_path(&self) -> PathBuf { self.root.join("settings.json") }
}

pub fn read_json<T: for<'de> Deserialize<'de>>(path: &Path) -> Option<T> {
    serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(path).ok()?).ok()
}

/// Write via temp file + rename so a crash never leaves a half-written manifest.
pub fn write_json_atomic<T: Serialize>(path: &Path, value: &T) -> Result<(), String> {
    let data = serde_json::to_vec_pretty(value).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let tmp = path.with_extension("json.tmp");
    fs::write(&tmp, data).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    fs::rename(&tmp, path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

`src-tauri/src/overlay.rs`:

```rs
//! Overlay window lifecycle: a single frameless, transparent, click-through
//! window per app that only renders the active PNG.

use crate::{model::*, win32};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, Manager, State, WebviewUrl, WebviewWindow, WebviewWindowBuilder};

pub const LABEL: &str = "overlay";

#[derive(Serialize, Clone)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct OverlayPayload {
    pub state: OverlayState,
    pub visible: bool,
    pub monitor_w: i32,
    pub monitor_h: i32,
    pub low_res: bool,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Clone)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct OverlayStatus {
    pub enabled: bool,
    pub visible: bool,
    pub game_running: bool,
}

/// Must be called on the main thread (setup). The window starts hidden.
pub fn create_window(app: &AppHandle) -> tauri::Result<()> {
    let win = WebviewWindowBuilder::new(app, LABEL, WebviewUrl::App("overlay.html".into()))
        .title("Iris Overlay")
        .transparent(true)
        .decorations(false)
        .shadow(false)
        .resizable(false)
        .always_on_top(true)
        .skip_taskbar(true)
        .focused(false)
        .visible(false)
        .build()?;
    let _ = win.set_ignore_cursor_events(true);
    if let Ok(h) = win.hwnd() {
        win32::apply_overlay_styles(h.0 as isize);
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn monitor_rect(app: &AppHandle, idx: usize) -> (i32, i32, i32, i32) {
    let mons = app.available_monitors().unwrap_or_default();
    match mons.get(idx).or(mons.first()) {
        Some(m) => {
            let (p, s) = (m.position(), m.size());
            (p.x, p.y, s.width as i32, s.height as i32)
        }
        None => (0, 0, 1920, 1080),
    }
}

/// Recompute visibility/placement from stored state and push to both windows.
pub fn refresh(app: &AppHandle) {
    let st = app.state::<AppState>();
    let o = st.overlay.lock().unwrap().clone();
    let s = st.settings.lock().unwrap().clone();
    let game = st.game_running.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
    let (mx, my, mw, mh) = monitor_rect(app, o.monitor);
    let visible = o.enabled && o.has_content() && (s.target_mode != "game" || game);

    if let Some(win) = app.get_webview_window(LABEL) {
        place_and_show(&win, &st, (mx, my, mw, mh), visible, s.hide_from_capture);
    }
    let _ = app.emit_to(
        LABEL,
        "overlay-state",
        OverlayPayload { state: o.clone(), visible, monitor_w: mw, monitor_h: mh, low_res: s.low_resource },
    );
    let _ = app.emit_to("main", "overlay-status", OverlayStatus { enabled: o.enabled, visible, game_running: game });
}

fn place_and_show(win: &WebviewWindow, st: &AppState, rect: (i32, i32, i32, i32), visible: bool, excl: bool) {
    let Ok(h) = win.hwnd() else { return };
    let raw = h.0 as isize;
    {
        let mut last = st.last_rect.lock().unwrap();
        if *last != Some(rect) {
            win32::place(raw, rect.0, rect.1, rect.2, rect.3);
            *last = Some(rect);
        }
    }
    win32::set_capture_excluded(raw, excl);
    if visible {
        win32::show(raw);
    } else {
        win32::hide(raw);
    }
}

pub fn toggle(app: &AppHandle) {
    {
        let st = app.state::<AppState>();
        let mut o = st.overlay.lock().unwrap();
        o.enabled = !o.enabled;
    }
    refresh(app);
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn overlay_apply(app: AppHandle, state: State<AppState>, overlay: OverlayState) {
    *state.overlay.lock().unwrap() = overlay;
    refresh(&app);
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn overlay_get(app: AppHandle, state: State<AppState>) -> OverlayPayload {
    let o = state.overlay.lock().unwrap().clone();
    let s = state.settings.lock().unwrap().clone();
    let (_, _, mw, mh) = monitor_rect(&app, o.monitor);
    let game = state.game_running.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
    let visible = o.enabled && o.has_content() && (s.target_mode != "game" || game);
    OverlayPayload { state: o, visible, monitor_w: mw, monitor_h: mh, low_res: s.low_resource }
}

`src-tauri/src/system.rs`:

```rs
//! System integration: global hotkeys, tray, autostart, monitors, game watcher.

use crate::{model::*, overlay, win32};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::{os::windows::process::CommandExt, sync::atomic::Ordering, time::Duration};
use tauri::{
    menu::{Menu, MenuItem},
    tray::{MouseButton, MouseButtonState, TrayIconBuilder, TrayIconEvent},
    AppHandle, Emitter, Manager, State,
};
use tauri_plugin_global_shortcut::{GlobalShortcutExt, ShortcutState};

#[derive(Clone)]
enum Action {
    Toggle,
    Next,
    Prev,
    /// toggle every layer with this role in the Layering stack
    Role(String),
}

fn handle(app: &AppHandle, action: Action) {
    match action {
        Action::Toggle => overlay::toggle(app),
        Action::Next => {
            let _ = app.emit_to("main", "cycle-overlay", 1);
        }
        Action::Prev => {
            let _ = app.emit_to("main", "cycle-overlay", -1);
        }
        Action::Role(role) => {
            let _ = app.emit_to("main", "toggle-layer-role", role);
        }
    }
}

pub fn register_hotkeys(app: &AppHandle) {
    let gs = app.global_shortcut();
    let _ = gs.unregister_all();
    let s = app.state::<AppState>().settings.lock().unwrap().clone();
    let mut binds = vec![
        (s.hotkey_toggle, Action::Toggle),
        (s.hotkey_next, Action::Next),
        (s.hotkey_prev, Action::Prev),
    ];
    for (role, keys) in s.layer_hotkeys {
        binds.push((keys, Action::Role(role)));
    }
    for (keys, action) in binds {
        if keys.trim().is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let h = app.clone();
        let res = gs.on_shortcut(keys.as_str(), move |_app, _sc, ev| {
            if ev.state() == ShortcutState::Pressed {
                handle(&h, action.clone());
            }
        });
        if let Err(e) = res {
            eprintln!("hotkey '{keys}' not registered: {e}");
        }
    }
}

pub fn build_tray(app: &AppHandle) -> tauri::Result<()> {
    let show = MenuItem::with_id(app, "show", "Show Iris", true, None::<&str>)?;
    let toggle = MenuItem::with_id(app, "toggle", "Toggle Overlay", true, None::<&str>)?;
    let check_update = MenuItem::with_id(app, "check_update", "Check for Updates...", true, None::<&str>)?;
    let quit = MenuItem::with_id(app, "quit", "Quit", true, None::<&str>)?;
    let menu = Menu::with_items(app, &[&show, &toggle, &check_update, &quit])?;
    TrayIconBuilder::new()
        .icon(app.default_window_icon().unwrap().clone())
        .tooltip("Iris")
        .menu(&menu)
        .show_menu_on_left_click(false)
        .on_menu_event(|app, ev| match ev.id.as_ref() {
            "show" => show_main(app),
            "toggle" => overlay::toggle(app),
            "check_update" => crate::updater::handle_tray_action(app),
            "quit" => app.exit(0),
            _ => {}
        })
        .on_tray_icon_event(|tray, ev| {
            if let TrayIconEvent::Click { button: MouseButton::Left, button_state: MouseButtonState::Up, .. } = ev {
                show_main(tray.app_handle());
            }
        })
        .build(app)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub fn show_main(app: &AppHandle) {
    if let Some(w) = app.get_webview_window("main") {
        let _ = w.show();
        let _ = w.unminimize();
        let _ = w.set_focus();
    }
}

/// Polls for the target game process, emits foreground window changes,
/// and continuously keeps overlay on top of borderless/fullscreen games.
pub fn spawn_game_watcher(app: AppHandle) {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let mut last_fg_exe: Option<String> = None;
        loop {
            let (mode, exe, low, enabled, _auto_switch, auto_hide) = {
                let st = app.state::<AppState>();
                let s = st.settings.lock().unwrap().clone();
                let o = st.overlay.lock().unwrap().clone();
                (s.target_mode, s.target_exe, s.low_resource, o.enabled, s.auto_game_switch, s.auto_hide_unfocused)
            };

            // Detect active foreground window process
            let fg = win32::get_foreground_process_name();
            if fg != last_fg_exe {
                let _ = app.emit_to("main", "foreground-process-changed", &fg);
                last_fg_exe = fg.clone();
            }

            let st = app.state::<AppState>();
            let running = mode == "game" && win32::process_running(exe.trim());
            if st.game_running.swap(running, Ordering::Relaxed) != running {
                overlay::refresh(&app);
            } else if enabled {
                // Auto-hide only suppresses if specifically in game mode and tabbed to non-game apps
                let is_unfocused_non_game = auto_hide && mode == "game" && fg.as_deref().map_or(false, |f| {
                    let f_lower = f.to_ascii_lowercase();
                    f_lower != "iris.exe" && (
                        f_lower.contains("discord")
                            || f_lower.contains("chrome")
                            || f_lower.contains("edge")
                            || f_lower.contains("firefox")
                            || f_lower.contains("explorer")
                            || f_lower.contains("code")
                            || f_lower.contains("spotify")
                    )
                });

                let is_visible_mode = mode != "game" || running;
                if let Some(w) = app.get_webview_window(overlay::LABEL) {
                    if let Ok(h) = w.hwnd() {
                        if is_visible_mode && !is_unfocused_non_game {
                            win32::show(h.0 as isize);
                            win32::assert_topmost(h.0 as isize);
                        } else {
                            win32::hide(h.0 as isize);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(if low { 1500 } else { 600 }));
        }
    });
}

/// Registers the `iris://` URL protocol in HKCU (no admin required) so clicking
/// "Send to Iris" on veevisuals.art or Aether Deck opens Iris and imports overlays directly.
pub fn register_url_protocol() {
    let exe = std::env::current_exe().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    if exe.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let _ = std::process::Command::new("reg")
        .args(["add", r"HKCU\Software\Classes\iris", "/ve", "/t", "REG_SZ", "/d", "URL:Iris Protocol", "/f"])
        .creation_flags(0x0800_0000)
        .output();
    let _ = std::process::Command::new("reg")
        .args(["add", r"HKCU\Software\Classes\iris", "/v", "URL Protocol", "/t", "REG_SZ", "/d", "", "/f"])
        .creation_flags(0x0800_0000)
        .output();
    let _ = std::process::Command::new("reg")
        .args(["add", r"HKCU\Software\Classes\iris\shell\open\command", "/ve", "/t", "REG_SZ", "/d", &format!("\"{exe}\" \"%1\""), "/f"])
        .creation_flags(0x0800_0000)
        .output();
}

pub fn set_autostart(enabled: bool) {
    const KEY: &str = r"HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run";
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("reg");
    if enabled {
        let exe = std::env::current_exe().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
        cmd.args(["add", KEY, "/v", "Iris", "/t", "REG_SZ", "/d", &format!("\"{exe}\" --minimized"), "/f"]);
    } else {
        cmd.args(["delete", KEY, "/v", "Iris", "/f"]);
    }
    let _ = cmd.creation_flags(0x0800_0000).output(); // CREATE_NO_WINDOW
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct MonitorInfo {
    index: usize,
    name: String,
    width: u32,
    height: u32,
    x: i32,
    y: i32,
    scale: f64,
    refresh_hz: u32,
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn list_monitors(app: AppHandle) -> Vec<MonitorInfo> {
    app.available_monitors()
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .map(|(index, m)| {
            let name = m.name().cloned().unwrap_or_default();
            MonitorInfo {
                index,
                refresh_hz: win32::refresh_rate(&name),
                name,
                width: m.size().width,
                height: m.size().height,
                x: m.position().x,
                y: m.position().y,
                scale: m.scale_factor(),
            }
        })
        .collect()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn settings_get(state: State<AppState>) -> Settings {
    state.settings.lock().unwrap().clone()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn settings_save(app: AppHandle, state: State<AppState>, settings: Settings) -> Result<Settings, String> {
    let prev_autostart = state.settings.lock().unwrap().start_with_windows;
    write_json_atomic(&state.settings_path(), &settings)?;
    *state.settings.lock().unwrap() = settings.clone();
    register_hotkeys(&app);
    if prev_autostart != settings.start_with_windows {
        set_autostart(settings.start_with_windows);
    }
    overlay::refresh(&app);
    Ok(settings)
}

`src-tauri/src/updater.rs`:

```rs
//! Automatic update management wrapping tauri-plugin-updater with overlay focus protection.

use crate::model::AppState;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::{
    atomic::Ordering,
    Mutex,
};
use std::time::Duration;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, Manager};
use tauri_plugin_updater::UpdaterExt;

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", tag = "status")]
pub enum UpdateStatus {
    Idle,
    Checking,
    UpToDate {
        current_version: String,
    },
    Available {
        version: String,
        current_version: String,
        body: Option<String>,
        date: Option<String>,
    },
    Downloading {
        version: String,
        progress: f64,
        downloaded: u64,
        total: Option<u64>,
    },
    Downloaded {
        version: String,
    },
    Error {
        message: String,
    },
}

static STATUS: Mutex<UpdateStatus> = Mutex::new(UpdateStatus::Idle);
static PENDING_UPDATE: Mutex<Option<tauri_plugin_updater::Update>> = Mutex::new(None);

/// Launches a background check on startup after a short delay so normal app startup
/// is never blocked or slowed down.
pub fn check_on_startup(app: AppHandle) {
    tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
        // Allow app initialization and window rendering to complete smoothly
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3));

        let should_check = {
            let st = app.state::<AppState>();
            let s = st.settings.lock().unwrap();
            s.check_updates_on_startup
        };

        if !should_check {
            return;
        }

        // Silent check on startup - offline or unreachable errors are logged and ignored
        match run_check(&app).await {
            Ok(Some(update)) => {
                let (overlay_active, game_running) = {
                    let st = app.state::<AppState>();
                    let ov_enabled = st.overlay.lock().unwrap().enabled;
                    let gm_running = st.game_running.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
                    (ov_enabled, gm_running)
                };

                let version = update.version.clone();
                let current_version = update.current_version.clone();
                let body = update.body.clone();
                let date = update.date.map(|d| d.to_string());

                *PENDING_UPDATE.lock().unwrap() = Some(update);
                let new_status = UpdateStatus::Available {
                    version: version.clone(),
                    current_version,
                    body,
                    date,
                };
                *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = new_status.clone();
                let _ = app.emit("updater-status", &new_status);

                // Focus Safety Rule: Never steal window focus or display popups while
                // an in-game overlay is active or a game process is running.
                if !overlay_active && !game_running {
                    let _ = app.emit("updater-available-toast", &version);
                }
            }
            Ok(None) => {
                // Up to date; keep idle on startup to avoid cluttering UI
                let current_version = app.package_info().version.to_string();
                *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = UpdateStatus::UpToDate { current_version };
            }
            Err(e) => {
                // Fail silently on startup
                eprintln!("[Iris Updater] Startup update check skipped or failed: {e}");
            }
        }
    });
}

/// Core updater check routine.
async fn run_check(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<Option<tauri_plugin_updater::Update>, String> {
    let updater = app.updater().map_err(|e| {
        let msg = e.to_string();
        if msg.contains("pubkey") || msg.contains("placeholder") {
            "Minisign public key is not configured or using placeholder. Generate your keypair and update tauri.conf.json.".to_string()
        } else {
            msg
        }
    })?;

    updater.check().await.map_err(|e| {
        let msg = e.to_string();
        if msg.contains("pubkey") || msg.contains("placeholder") {
            "Minisign public key is not configured or using placeholder. Generate your keypair and update tauri.conf.json.".to_string()
        } else {
            msg
        }
    })
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn updater_check(app: AppHandle) -> Result<UpdateStatus, String> {
    *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = UpdateStatus::Checking;
    let _ = app.emit("updater-status", UpdateStatus::Checking);

    match run_check(&app).await {
        Ok(Some(update)) => {
            let version = update.version.clone();
            let current_version = update.current_version.clone();
            let body = update.body.clone();
            let date = update.date.map(|d| d.to_string());

            *PENDING_UPDATE.lock().unwrap() = Some(update);
            let status = UpdateStatus::Available {
                version,
                current_version,
                body,
                date,
            };
            *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = status.clone();
            let _ = app.emit("updater-status", &status);
            Ok(status)
        }
        Ok(None) => {
            *PENDING_UPDATE.lock().unwrap() = None;
            let current_version = app.package_info().version.to_string();
            let status = UpdateStatus::UpToDate { current_version };
            *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = status.clone();
            let _ = app.emit("updater-status", &status);
            Ok(status)
        }
        Err(e) => {
            let status = UpdateStatus::Error { message: e.clone() };
            *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = status.clone();
            let _ = app.emit("updater-status", &status);
            Err(e)
        }
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn updater_download_and_install(app: AppHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
    let update = {
        let mut guard = PENDING_UPDATE.lock().unwrap();
        guard.take()
    };

    let update = match update {
        Some(u) => u,
        None => {
            // If no pending update is cached, try checking first
            match run_check(&app).await? {
                Some(u) => u,
                None => return Err("No update available to download".into()),
            }
        }
    };

    let version = update.version.clone();
    let version_for_cb = version.clone();
    let app_for_cb = app.clone();
    let mut downloaded_bytes: u64 = 0;

    let download_result = update
        .download_and_install(
            move |chunk_length, content_length| {
                downloaded_bytes += chunk_length as u64;
                let progress = if let Some(total) = content_length {
                    if total > 0 {
                        (downloaded_bytes as f64 / total as f64) * 100.0
                    } else {
                        0.0
                    }
                } else {
                    0.0
                };

                let st = UpdateStatus::Downloading {
                    version: version_for_cb.clone(),
                    progress,
                    downloaded: downloaded_bytes,
                    total: content_length,
                };
                *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = st.clone();
                let _ = app_for_cb.emit("updater-status", &st);
            },
            || {
                eprintln!("[Iris Updater] Update downloaded successfully; staging installation.");
            },
        )
        .await;

    match download_result {
        Ok(_) => {
            let st = UpdateStatus::Downloaded { version: version.clone() };
            *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = st.clone();
            let _ = app.emit("updater-status", &st);
            Ok(())
        }
        Err(e) => {
            let err_msg = e.to_string();
            let st = UpdateStatus::Error { message: err_msg.clone() };
            *STATUS.lock().unwrap() = st.clone();
            let _ = app.emit("updater-status", &st);
            Err(err_msg)
        }
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn updater_restart(app: AppHandle) {
    app.restart();
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn updater_get_status(_app: AppHandle) -> UpdateStatus {
    STATUS.lock().unwrap().clone()
}

/// Handles click on tray item.
pub fn handle_tray_action(app: &AppHandle) {
    let current_status = STATUS.lock().unwrap().clone();
    match current_status {
        UpdateStatus::Downloaded { .. } => {
            app.restart();
        }
        _ => {
            crate::system::show_main(app);
            let _ = app.emit_to("main", "navigate-tab", "settings");
            let app_h = app.clone();
            tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
                let _ = updater_check(app_h).await;
            });
        }
    }
}

```

`src-tauri/src/vault.rs`:

```rs
//! Vault commands: import / edit / delete assets, folders, persisted in
//! `vault_manifest.json` next to the managed `vault_assets/` directory.

use crate::model::*;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::{
    fs,
    sync::atomic::Ordering,
    time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH},
};
use tauri::{
    ipc::{InvokeBody, Request},
    State,
};

#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct VaultPayload {
    items: Vec<VaultItem>,
    folders: Vec<String>,
    presets: Vec<CompositePreset>,
    root_dir: String,
    assets_dir: String,
    thumbs_dir: String,
}

fn snapshot(st: &AppState) -> VaultPayload {
    let m = st.manifest.lock().unwrap();
    VaultPayload {
        items: m.items.clone(),
        folders: m.folders.clone(),
        presets: m.presets.clone(),
        root_dir: st.root.to_string_lossy().into(),
        assets_dir: st.assets_dir().to_string_lossy().into(),
        thumbs_dir: st.thumbs_dir().to_string_lossy().into(),
    }
}

fn persist(st: &AppState) -> Result<(), String> {
    let m = st.manifest.lock().unwrap();
    write_json_atomic(&st.manifest_path(), &*m)
}

fn percent_decode(s: &str) -> String {
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(b.len());
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < b.len() {
        if b[i] == b'%' && i + 2 < b.len() {
            if let Some(v) = std::str::from_utf8(&b[i + 1..i + 3]).ok().and_then(|h| u8::from_str_radix(h, 16).ok()) {
                out.push(v);
                i += 3;
                continue;
            }
        }
        out.push(b[i]);
        i += 1;
    }
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&out).into_owned()
}

fn header(req: &Request<'_>, key: &str) -> String {
    req.headers()
        .get(key)
        .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
        .map(percent_decode)
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

fn raw_body<'a>(req: &'a Request<'_>) -> Result<&'a Vec<u8>, String> {
    match req.body() {
        InvokeBody::Raw(b) => Ok(b),
        _ => Err("expected raw binary body".into()),
    }
}

fn new_id(st: &AppState) -> String {
    let nanos = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_nanos()).unwrap_or(0);
    format!("{:x}{:x}", nanos, st.counter.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn vault_load(state: State<AppState>) -> VaultPayload {
    snapshot(&state)
}

/// Raw-body command: body = file bytes, metadata in (percent-encoded) headers.
#[tauri::command]
pub fn import_asset(state: State<AppState>, request: Request<'_>) -> Result<VaultItem, String> {
    let bytes = raw_body(&request)?;
    let is_png = bytes.starts_with(&[0x89, b'P', b'N', b'G']);
    let is_webp = bytes.len() > 12 && &bytes[0..4] == b"RIFF" && &bytes[8..12] == b"WEBP";
    if !is_png && !is_webp {
        return Err("Only PNG and WebP images are supported".into());
    }
    let game = header(&request, "x-game");
    if game.is_empty() {
        return Err("A game category is required".into());
    }
    let id = new_id(&state);
    let file = format!("{}.{}", id, if is_png { "png" } else { "webp" });
    fs::write(state.assets_dir().join(&file), bytes).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

    let name = header(&request, "x-name");
    let folder = header(&request, "x-folder");
    let item = VaultItem {
        id,
        name: if name.trim().is_empty() { "Untitled".into() } else { name.trim().into() },
        game,
        folder: if folder.is_empty() { None } else { Some(folder) },
        file,
        thumb: None,
        width: header(&request, "x-width").parse().unwrap_or(0),
        height: header(&request, "x-height").parse().unwrap_or(0),
        created_at: SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_millis() as u64).unwrap_or(0),
        transform: None,
        tag: Some(header(&request, "x-tag")).filter(|t| !t.is_empty()),
        favorite: header(&request, "x-favorite") == "true",
    };
    state.manifest.lock().unwrap().items.push(item.clone());
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(item)
}

/// Raw-body command: PNG thumbnail generated by the frontend; header `x-id`.
#[tauri::command]
pub fn save_thumbnail(state: State<AppState>, request: Request<'_>) -> Result<VaultPayload, String> {
    let bytes = raw_body(&request)?;
    let id = header(&request, "x-id");
    let thumb = format!("{}.png", id);
    {
        let mut m = state.manifest.lock().unwrap();
        let item = m.items.iter_mut().find(|i| i.id == id).ok_or("unknown item")?;
        fs::write(state.thumbs_dir().join(&thumb), bytes).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        item.thumb = Some(thumb);
    }
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(snapshot(&state))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ItemPatch {
    name: Option<String>,
    game: Option<String>,
    /// empty string clears the custom folder
    folder: Option<String>,
    transform: Option<Transform>,
    /// empty string clears the element tag
    tag: Option<String>,
    favorite: Option<bool>,
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn vault_update_item(state: State<AppState>, id: String, patch: ItemPatch) -> Result<VaultPayload, String> {
    {
        let mut m = state.manifest.lock().unwrap();
        let item = m.items.iter_mut().find(|i| i.id == id).ok_or("unknown item")?;
        if let Some(n) = patch.name.filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty()) {
            item.name = n.trim().into();
        }
        if let Some(g) = patch.game {
            item.game = g;
        }
        if let Some(f) = patch.folder {
            item.folder = if f.is_empty() { None } else { Some(f) };
        }
        if let Some(t) = patch.transform {
            item.transform = Some(t);
        }
        if let Some(t) = patch.tag {
            item.tag = if t.is_empty() { None } else { Some(t) };
        }
        if let Some(fav) = patch.favorite {
            item.favorite = fav;
        }
    }
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(snapshot(&state))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn vault_delete_item(state: State<AppState>, id: String) -> Result<VaultPayload, String> {
    {
        let mut m = state.manifest.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(pos) = m.items.iter().position(|i| i.id == id) {
            let item = m.items.remove(pos);
            let _ = fs::remove_file(state.assets_dir().join(&item.file));
            if let Some(t) = item.thumb {
                let _ = fs::remove_file(state.thumbs_dir().join(t));
            }
        }
    }
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(snapshot(&state))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn vault_create_folder(state: State<AppState>, name: String) -> Result<VaultPayload, String> {
    let name = name.trim().to_string();
    if name.is_empty() {
        return Err("Folder name is empty".into());
    }
    {
        let mut m = state.manifest.lock().unwrap();
        if m.folders.iter().any(|f| f.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&name)) {
            return Err("Folder already exists".into());
        }
        m.folders.push(name);
    }
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(snapshot(&state))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn vault_delete_folder(state: State<AppState>, name: String) -> Result<VaultPayload, String> {
    {
        let mut m = state.manifest.lock().unwrap();
        m.folders.retain(|f| f != &name);
        for i in m.items.iter_mut().filter(|i| i.folder.as_deref() == Some(name.as_str())) {
            i.folder = None;
        }
    }
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(snapshot(&state))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn open_vault_dir(state: State<AppState>) {
    let _ = std::process::Command::new("explorer").arg(&state.root).spawn();
}

/// Insert or replace (by id) a composite preset.
#[tauri::command]
pub fn preset_save(state: State<AppState>, preset: CompositePreset) -> Result<VaultPayload, String> {
    {
        let mut m = state.manifest.lock().unwrap();
        match m.presets.iter_mut().find(|p| p.id == preset.id) {
            Some(p) => *p = preset,
            None => m.presets.push(preset),
        }
    }
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(snapshot(&state))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn preset_delete(state: State<AppState>, id: String) -> Result<VaultPayload, String> {
    state.manifest.lock().unwrap().presets.retain(|p| p.id != id);
    persist(&state)?;
    Ok(snapshot(&state))
}

`src-tauri/src/win32.rs`:

```rs
//! Thin native Win32 layer: click-through overlay styles, placement,
//! capture exclusion, display refresh rate and process lookup.
//! All functions take the raw HWND value (`isize`) so they are independent of
//! the `windows` crate version Tauri links internally.

use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::mem::size_of;
use windows::core::PCWSTR;
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, HWND};
use windows::Win32::Graphics::Gdi::{EnumDisplaySettingsW, DEVMODEW, ENUM_CURRENT_SETTINGS};
use windows::Win32::System::Diagnostics::ToolHelp::{
    CreateToolhelp32Snapshot, Process32FirstW, Process32NextW, PROCESSENTRY32W, TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS,
};
use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::*;

fn hwnd(raw: isize) -> HWND {
    HWND(raw as *mut c_void)
}

/// WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT (click-through, zero input latency)
/// | WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW (no Alt+Tab / taskbar entry)
/// | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE (never steals focus from the game).
pub fn apply_overlay_styles(raw: isize) {
    unsafe {
        let h = hwnd(raw);
        let mut ex = GetWindowLongPtrW(h, GWL_EXSTYLE) as u32;
        ex |= WS_EX_LAYERED.0
            | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT.0
            | WS_EX_TOPMOST.0
            | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW.0
            | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE.0;
        ex &= !WS_EX_APPWINDOW.0;
        SetWindowLongPtrW(h, GWL_EXSTYLE, ex as isize);
        let _ = SetWindowPos(
            h,
            Some(HWND_TOPMOST),
            0,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOACTIVATE | SWP_FRAMECHANGED,
        );
    }
}

/// Cover the given physical-pixel rectangle (borderless "fullscreen").
pub fn place(raw: isize, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32) {
    unsafe {
        let _ = SetWindowPos(hwnd(raw), Some(HWND_TOPMOST), x, y, w, h, SWP_NOACTIVATE);
    }
}

pub fn show(raw: isize) {
    unsafe {
        let _ = ShowWindow(hwnd(raw), SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
        let _ = SetWindowPos(
            hwnd(raw),
            Some(HWND_TOPMOST),
            0,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOACTIVATE,
        );
    }
}

/// Reassert HWND_TOPMOST without stealing focus or moving the window.
/// Keeps overlay visible over borderless, windowed, and fullscreen (FSO) games.
pub fn assert_topmost(raw: isize) {
    unsafe {
        let _ = SetWindowPos(
            hwnd(raw),
            Some(HWND_TOPMOST),
            0,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOACTIVATE,
        );
    }
}

pub fn hide(raw: isize) {
    unsafe {
        let _ = ShowWindow(hwnd(raw), SW_HIDE);
    }
}

/// `true` => WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE (Win10 2004+): hidden from OBS/screenshots.
pub fn set_capture_excluded(raw: isize, excluded: bool) {
    unsafe {
        let _ = SetWindowDisplayAffinity(
            hwnd(raw),
            if excluded { WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE } else { WDA_NONE },
        );
    }
}

/// Current refresh rate of a display by its GDI device name (`\\.\DISPLAY1`).
pub fn refresh_rate(device_name: &str) -> u32 {
    unsafe {
        let wide: Vec<u16> = device_name.encode_utf16().chain(Some(0)).collect();
        let mut dm = DEVMODEW { dmSize: size_of::<DEVMODEW>() as u16, ..Default::default() };
        if EnumDisplaySettingsW(PCWSTR(wide.as_ptr()), ENUM_CURRENT_SETTINGS, &mut dm).as_bool() {
            dm.dmDisplayFrequency
        } else {
            0
        }
    }
}

/// Is a process with this exe name (e.g. `Overwatch.exe`) running?
pub fn process_running(exe: &str) -> bool {
    unsafe {
        let Ok(snap) = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0) else { return false };
        let mut entry = PROCESSENTRY32W { dwSize: size_of::<PROCESSENTRY32W>() as u32, ..Default::default() };
        let mut found = false;
        if Process32FirstW(snap, &mut entry).is_ok() {
            loop {
                let len = entry.szExeFile.iter().position(|&c| c == 0).unwrap_or(entry.szExeFile.len());
                if String::from_utf16_lossy(&entry.szExeFile[..len]).eq_ignore_ascii_case(exe) {
                    found = true;
                    break;
                }
                if Process32NextW(snap, &mut entry).is_err() {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        let _ = CloseHandle(snap);
        found
    }
}

/// Returns the executable name of the currently focused foreground window (e.g. `Overwatch.exe`).
pub fn get_foreground_process_name() -> Option<String> {
    unsafe {
        let h = GetForegroundWindow();
        if h.0.is_null() {
            return None;
        }
        let mut pid = 0u32;
        let _ = GetWindowThreadProcessId(h, Some(&mut pid));
        if pid == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        let Ok(snap) = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0) else { return None };
        let mut entry = PROCESSENTRY32W { dwSize: size_of::<PROCESSENTRY32W>() as u32, ..Default::default() };
        let mut result = None;
        if Process32FirstW(snap, &mut entry).is_ok() {
            loop {
                if entry.th32ProcessID == pid {
                    let len = entry.szExeFile.iter().position(|&c| c == 0).unwrap_or(entry.szExeFile.len());
                    result = Some(String::from_utf16_lossy(&entry.szExeFile[..len]));
                    break;
                }
                if Process32NextW(snap, &mut entry).is_err() {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        let _ = CloseHandle(snap);
        result
    }
}

`src/App.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { listen } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
import { api } from "./api";
import {
  DEFAULT_OVERLAY,
  DEFAULT_TRANSFORM,
  KNOWN_GAMES,
  fxOf,
  type CanvasRes,
  type CompositePreset,
  type Layer,
  type MonitorInfo,
  type OverlayState,
  type OverlayStatus,
  type Settings,
  type Tab,
  type UpdateStatus,
  type VaultData,
  type VaultItem,
} from "./types";
import { filterItems } from "./util";
import { fromPreset, newLayer, toScene, uid } from "./layers";
import { loadUsage, saveUsage, type UsageStats } from "./usage";
import { TopBar } from "./components/TopBar";
import { Vault } from "./components/Vault";
import { Display } from "./components/Display";
import { Layering } from "./components/Layering";
import { SettingsView } from "./components/Settings";

export default function App() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("vault");
  const [vault, setVault] = useState<VaultData | null>(null);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<Settings | null>(null);
  const [monitors, setMonitors] = useState<MonitorInfo[]>([]);
  const [overlay, setOverlay] = useState<OverlayState>(DEFAULT_OVERLAY);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("All");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<OverlayStatus>({ enabled: false, visible: false, gameRunning: false });
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [currentForegroundExe, setCurrentForegroundExe] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [updateStatus, setUpdateStatus] = useState<UpdateStatus>({ status: "idle" });
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<{
    title: string;
    sub: string;
    actionLabel?: string;
    onAction?: () => void;
  } | null>(null);

  const vaultRef = useRef(vault);
  vaultRef.current = vault;
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;
  const lastAutoGame = useRef<string | null>(null);

  // Usage Telemetry (time, swaps, uploads, favorite overlay)
  const usageStatsRef = useRef<UsageStats>(loadUsage());
  const [usageStats, setUsageStats] = useState<UsageStats>(() => usageStatsRef.current);
  const lastActiveOverlayIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const [telemetryPaused, setTelemetryPaused] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem("iris_telemetry_paused") === "true";
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  });

  const toggleTelemetry = useCallback(() => {
    setTelemetryPaused((prev) => {
      const next = !prev;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("iris_telemetry_paused", String(next));
      } catch {
        /* ignore */
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const resetUsageStats = useCallback(() => {
    const v = vaultRef.current;
    const resetStats: UsageStats = {
      totalAppTimeSeconds: 0,
      totalSwaps: 0,
      totalUploads: v?.items.length ?? 0,
      overlayTime: {},
    };
    usageStatsRef.current = resetStats;
    saveUsage(resetStats);
    setUsageStats(resetStats);
  }, []);

  const recordSwap = useCallback((newId: string | null) => {
    if (!newId || telemetryPaused) return;
    if (lastActiveOverlayIdRef.current && lastActiveOverlayIdRef.current !== newId) {
      const next: UsageStats = {
        ...usageStatsRef.current,
        totalSwaps: usageStatsRef.current.totalSwaps + 1,
      };
      usageStatsRef.current = next;
      saveUsage(next);
      setUsageStats(next);
    }
    lastActiveOverlayIdRef.current = newId;
  }, [telemetryPaused]);

  const recordUpload = useCallback(() => {
    if (telemetryPaused) return;
    const next: UsageStats = {
      ...usageStatsRef.current,
      totalUploads: usageStatsRef.current.totalUploads + 1,
    };
    usageStatsRef.current = next;
    saveUsage(next);
    setUsageStats(next);
  }, [telemetryPaused]);

  // Layering studio state (kept here so Vault actions and tab switches preserve it)
  const [layers, setLayers] = useState<Layer[]>([]);
  const [live, setLive] = useState(false); // composite is what the overlay shows
  const [activePresetId, setActivePresetId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  // authoring resolution for the Layering canvas (null = match the monitor)
  const [canvasRes, setCanvasResState] = useState<CanvasRes | null>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem("iris_canvas_res");
      return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CanvasRes) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });
  const setCanvasRes = useCallback((r: CanvasRes | null) => {
    setCanvasResState(r);
    try {
      if (r) localStorage.setItem("iris_canvas_res", JSON.stringify(r));
      else localStorage.removeItem("iris_canvas_res");
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }, []);

  // Live app and active overlay telemetry timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (telemetryPaused) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const cur = usageStatsRef.current;
      const nextAppTime = cur.totalAppTimeSeconds + 1;
      const nextOverlayTime = { ...cur.overlayTime };

      if (overlay.enabled) {
        const activeKey = live && activePresetId ? `preset:${activePresetId}` : overlay.itemId;
        if (activeKey) {
          nextOverlayTime[activeKey] = (nextOverlayTime[activeKey] || 0) + 1;
        }
      }

      const nextStats: UsageStats = {
        ...cur,
        totalAppTimeSeconds: nextAppTime,
        overlayTime: nextOverlayTime,
      };
      usageStatsRef.current = nextStats;

      if (nextAppTime % 5 === 0) {
        saveUsage(nextStats);
      }

      if (tab === "settings" || nextAppTime % 10 === 0) {
        setUsageStats(nextStats);
      }
    }, 1000);

    return () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      saveUsage(usageStatsRef.current);
    };
  }, [overlay.enabled, overlay.itemId, live, activePresetId, tab, telemetryPaused]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab === "settings") {
      setUsageStats({ ...usageStatsRef.current });
    }
  }, [tab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([api.vaultLoad(), api.settingsGet(), api.listMonitors(), api.updaterGetStatus()]).then(([v, s, m, u]) => {
      setVault(v);
      if (v && v.items.length > usageStatsRef.current.totalUploads) {
        const next = { ...usageStatsRef.current, totalUploads: v.items.length };
        usageStatsRef.current = next;
        saveUsage(next);
        setUsageStats(next);
      }
      setSettings(s);
      setMonitors(m);
      if (u) setUpdateStatus(u);
      setReady(true);
    }).catch(() => {
      // Fail gracefully if any initial call fails
      setReady(true);
    });
    const un1 = listen<OverlayStatus>("overlay-status", (e) => {
      setStatus(e.payload);
      setOverlay((o) => (o.enabled === e.payload.enabled ? o : { ...o, enabled: e.payload.enabled }));
    });
    // global per-role hotkey: toggle every layer of that role
    const un2 = listen<string>("toggle-layer-role", (e) =>
      setLayers((ls) => {
        const same = ls.filter((l) => l.role === e.payload);
        if (!same.length) return ls;
        const show = !same.some((l) => l.visible);
        return ls.map((l) => (l.role === e.payload ? { ...l, visible: show } : l));
      }),
    );
    // updater live status updates
    const un3 = listen<UpdateStatus>("updater-status", (e) => {
      setUpdateStatus(e.payload);
    });
    // tray navigation event
    const un4 = listen<string>("navigate-tab", (e) => {
      setTab(e.payload as Tab);
    });
    // subtle non-intrusive toast when an update is available
    const un5 = listen<string>("updater-available-toast", (e) => {
      setToast({
        title: `✨ Iris Update v${e.payload} Available`,
        sub: "Review release notes & download in Settings.",
        actionLabel: "View in Settings",
        onAction: () => setTab("settings"),
      });
    });
    return () => {
      un1.then((f) => f());
      un2.then((f) => f());
      un3.then((f) => f());
      un4.then((f) => f());
      un5.then((f) => f());
    };
  }, []);

  // Push to the native window: either the single overlay or the resolved composite.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready || !vault) return;
    // a canvas authored at another resolution is scaled to fit the real monitor
    const m = monitors[overlay.monitor] ?? monitors[0];
    const factor = canvasRes && m ? Math.min(m.width / canvasRes.w, m.height / canvasRes.h) : 1;
    const state: OverlayState = live
      ? { ...overlay, path: null, scene: toScene(layers, vault, factor) }
      : { ...overlay, scene: [] };
    api.overlayApply(state).catch(console.error);
  }, [overlay, layers, live, vault, ready, canvasRes, monitors]);

  // if the active item is deleted, clear the overlay
  useEffect(() => {
    if (vault && overlay.itemId && !vault.items.some((i) => i.id === overlay.itemId)) {
      setOverlay((o) => ({ ...o, itemId: null, path: null, enabled: false }));
    }
  }, [vault, overlay.itemId]);

  const saveSettings = useCallback(async (patch: Partial<Settings>) => {
    setSettings((s) => (s ? { ...s, ...patch } : s));
    const cur = await api.settingsGet();
    setSettings(await api.settingsSave({ ...cur, ...patch }));
  }, []);

  const loadItem = useCallback(
    (item: VaultItem, goToDisplay = true) => {
      if (!vault) return;
      recordSwap(item.id);
      setLive(false);
      setOverlay((o) => ({
        ...o,
        enabled: true,
        itemId: item.id,
        path: `${vault.assetsDir}\\${item.file}`,
        width: item.width,
        height: item.height,
        transform: item.transform ?? DEFAULT_TRANSFORM,
      }));
      if (goToDisplay) setTab("display");
    },
    [vault, recordSwap],
  );

  /** Append a Vault item on top of the stack (never replaces existing layers). */
  const addLayer = useCallback((item: VaultItem, at?: { x: number; y: number }) => {
    const layer = newLayer(item, at);
    setLayers((ls) => [layer, ...ls]);
    return layer.id;
  }, []);

  const sendToStack = useCallback(
    (item: VaultItem) => {
      addLayer(item);
      setTab("layering");
    },
    [addLayer],
  );

  const loadPreset = useCallback(
    (p: CompositePreset, pushLive: boolean, goToLayering = false) => {
      recordSwap(`preset:${p.id}`);
      setLayers(fromPreset(p.layers));
      setActivePresetId(p.id);
      if (p.refW && p.refH) setCanvasRes({ w: p.refW, h: p.refH });
      if (pushLive) {
        setLive(true);
        setOverlay((o) => ({ ...o, enabled: true }));
      }
      if (goToLayering) setTab("layering");
    },
    [recordSwap, setCanvasRes],
  );

  const pushComposite = useCallback(() => {
    recordSwap(activePresetId ? `preset:${activePresetId}` : "composite_live");
    setLive(true);
    setOverlay((o) => ({ ...o, enabled: true }));
    if (settingsRef.current?.targetMode === "game" && !status.gameRunning) {
      saveSettings({ targetMode: "topmost" });
    }
  }, [activePresetId, recordSwap, status.gameRunning, saveSettings]);

  // Auto-Game Detection & Profile Auto-Switching
  useEffect(() => {
    const un = listen<string | null>("foreground-process-changed", (e) => {
      const fg = e.payload;
      setCurrentForegroundExe(fg);
      if (!fg) return;
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const v = vaultRef.current;
      if (!s || s.autoGameSwitch === false || !v) return;

      const matchedGame = KNOWN_GAMES.find((g) =>
        g.exes.some((exe) => exe.toLowerCase() === fg.toLowerCase())
      );
      if (!matchedGame) {
        lastAutoGame.current = null;
        return;
      }

      if (lastAutoGame.current === matchedGame.name) return;
      lastAutoGame.current = matchedGame.name;

      // Check user-configured explicit profile mapping
      const assigned = s.gameProfiles?.[matchedGame.name];
      if (assigned) {
        if (assigned.startsWith("preset:")) {
          const pid = assigned.slice("preset:".length);
          const p = v.presets.find((x) => x.id === pid);
          if (p) {
            loadPreset(p, true, false);
            setToast({
              title: `● Auto-Switched to ${matchedGame.label}`,
              sub: `Loaded loadout "${p.name}"`,
            });
            return;
          }
        } else if (assigned.startsWith("item:")) {
          const iid = assigned.slice("item:".length);
          const it = v.items.find((x) => x.id === iid);
          if (it) {
            loadItem(it, false);
            setToast({
              title: `● Auto-Switched to ${matchedGame.label}`,
              sub: `Loaded overlay "${it.name}"`,
            });
            return;
          }
        }
      }

      // Default auto lookup: matching game preset or favorite overlay
      const matchingPreset = v.presets.find((p) => p.game === matchedGame.name);
      if (matchingPreset) {
        loadPreset(matchingPreset, true, false);
        setToast({
          title: `● Auto-Switched to ${matchedGame.label}`,
          sub: `Loaded loadout "${matchingPreset.name}"`,
        });
        return;
      }

      const matchingItem =
        v.items.find((i) => i.game === matchedGame.name && i.favorite) ||
        v.items.find((i) => i.game === matchedGame.name);
      if (matchingItem) {
        loadItem(matchingItem, false);
        setToast({
          title: `● Auto-Switched to ${matchedGame.label}`,
          sub: `Loaded overlay "${matchingItem.name}"`,
        });
      }
    });

    return () => {
      un.then((f) => f());
    };
  }, [loadPreset, loadItem]);

  // 1-Click "Install to Iris" Web Protocol (iris://)
  useEffect(() => {
    const un = listen<string>("iris-protocol-url", async (e) => {
      const raw = e.payload.trim();
      if (!raw.startsWith("iris://")) return;
      try {
        const parsedUrl = new URL(raw.replace(/^iris:\/\//i, "https://iris/"));
        const action = parsedUrl.pathname.replace(/^\//, "");

        if (action === "import") {
          const downloadUrl = parsedUrl.searchParams.get("url");
          const name = parsedUrl.searchParams.get("name") || "Imported Design";
          const game = parsedUrl.searchParams.get("game") || "Other";
          if (!downloadUrl) return;

          setToast({ title: "Downloading Pack from veevisuals.art…", sub: name });
          const res = await fetch(downloadUrl);
          const buf = await res.arrayBuffer();
          const blob = new Blob([buf]);
          const bmp = await createImageBitmap(blob);
          const importedItem = await api.importAsset(new Uint8Array(buf), {
            name,
            game,
            folder: "",
            tag: "",
            width: bmp.width,
            height: bmp.height,
          });
          recordUpload();
          const updatedVault = await api.vaultLoad();
          setVault(updatedVault);
          setToast({
            title: "✓ Installed from veevisuals.art!",
            sub: `${name} (${game}) is ready in your Vault`,
            actionLabel: "Equip Now",
            onAction: () => loadItem(importedItem, true),
          });
        } else if (action === "preset") {
          const data = parsedUrl.searchParams.get("data") || "";
          let json: any = null;
          let trimmed = data.trim();
          if (trimmed.startsWith("IRIS-PRESET:")) trimmed = trimmed.slice("IRIS-PRESET:".length);
          try {
            json = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(trimmed))));
          } catch {
            json = JSON.parse(atob(trimmed));
          }

          if (json && Array.isArray(json.layers)) {
            const v = vaultRef.current;
            const items = v?.items || [];
            const reconstructed: Layer[] = json.layers.map((l: any, idx: number) => {
              let matched = items.find((i) => i.id === l.vaultItemId);
              if (!matched && l.assetFile) matched = items.find((i) => i.file === l.assetFile);
              if (!matched && l.assetName) matched = items.find((i) => i.name.toLowerCase() === l.assetName.toLowerCase());
              if (!matched) matched = items[0];
              return {
                id: uid(),
                vaultItemId: matched?.id ?? (items[0]?.id || "item_1"),
                name: l.name || matched?.name || `Layer ${idx + 1}`,
                role: l.role || "custom",
                zIndex: typeof l.zIndex === "number" ? l.zIndex : idx,
                visible: l.visible !== false,
                locked: !!l.locked,
                x: Number(l.x) || 0,
                y: Number(l.y) || 0,
                scale: Number(l.scale) || 100,
                opacity: typeof l.opacity === "number" ? l.opacity : 100,
                hue: Number(l.hue) || 0,
                fx: fxOf({ fx: l.fx }),
              };
            });
            setLayers(() => reconstructed);
            setActivePresetId(null);
            setTab("layering");
            setToast({
              title: "✓ Loadout Loaded from veevisuals.art",
              sub: `${json.name || "Custom Preset"} (${reconstructed.length} layers)`,
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to process iris protocol:", err);
      }
    });

    return () => {
      un.then((f) => f());
    };
  }, [loadItem]);

  // Toast Auto-Dismiss
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 5000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [toast]);

  // global hotkeys Next/Previous: cycle through the overlays of the current Vault filter
  const cycleRef = useRef<(dir: number) => void>(() => {});
  cycleRef.current = (dir) => {
    if (!vault) return;
    const list = filterItems(vault.items, filter);
    if (!list.length) return;
    const idx = list.findIndex((i) => i.id === overlay.itemId);
    const next = list[(((idx === -1 ? (dir > 0 ? -1 : 0) : idx) + dir) % list.length + list.length) % list.length];
    loadItem(next, false);
  };
  useEffect(() => {
    const un = listen<number>("cycle-overlay", (e) => cycleRef.current(e.payload));
    return () => {
      un.then((f) => f());
    };
  }, []);

  if (!vault || !settings) return <div className="boot">INITIALIZING IRIS…</div>;
  const activeItem = vault.items.find((i) => i.id === overlay.itemId) ?? null;

  const handleCheckUpdate = async () => {
    try {
      await api.updaterCheck();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to check for updates:", e);
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadUpdate = async () => {
    try {
      await api.updaterDownloadAndInstall();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to download update:", e);
    }
  };

  const handleRestartUpdate = async () => {
    try {
      await api.updaterRestart();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to restart for update:", e);
    }
  };

  const hasUpdate = updateStatus.status === "available" || updateStatus.status === "downloaded";

  return (
    <div className="app">
      <TopBar
        tab={tab}
        setTab={setTab}
        active={status.visible}
        waiting={overlay.enabled && !status.visible}
        onForceShow={() => saveSettings({ targetMode: "topmost" })}
        hasUpdate={hasUpdate}
      />
      <main>
        {tab === "vault" && (
          <Vault
            vault={vault}
            setVault={setVault}
            filter={filter}
            setFilter={setFilter}
            activeId={overlay.itemId}
            onLoad={loadItem}
            onSendToStack={sendToStack}
            onLoadPreset={(p) => loadPreset(p, true, true)}
            onUpload={recordUpload}
          />
        )}
        {tab === "display" && (
          <Display
            vault={vault}
            setVault={setVault}
            item={activeItem}
            overlay={overlay}
            setOverlay={setOverlay}
            monitors={monitors}
            status={status}
            goVault={() => setTab("vault")}
            compositeLive={live}
            stopComposite={() => setLive(false)}
            settings={settings}
            saveSettings={saveSettings}
          />
        )}
        {tab === "layering" && (
          <Layering
            vault={vault}
            setVault={setVault}
            layers={layers}
            setLayers={setLayers}
            addLayer={addLayer}
            live={live}
            setLive={setLive}
            push={pushComposite}
            monitors={monitors}
            monitor={overlay.monitor}
            setMonitor={(i) => setOverlay((o) => ({ ...o, monitor: i }))}
            settings={settings}
            activePresetId={activePresetId}
            setActivePresetId={setActivePresetId}
            loadPreset={(p, push) => loadPreset(p, push)}
            canvasRes={canvasRes}
            setCanvasRes={setCanvasRes}
          />
        )}
        {tab === "settings" && (
          <SettingsView
            settings={settings}
            save={saveSettings}
            currentForegroundExe={currentForegroundExe}
            vault={vault}
            usageStats={usageStats}
            activeOverlayItemId={overlay.itemId}
            isTelemetryPaused={telemetryPaused}
            onToggleTelemetry={toggleTelemetry}
            onResetTelemetry={resetUsageStats}
            onEquipItem={(item) => {
              loadItem(item, true);
              setToast({
                title: "● Overlay Equipped",
                sub: `Equipped "${item.name}" to display`,
              });
            }}
            onEquipPreset={(p) => {
              loadPreset(p, true, true);
              setToast({
                title: "● Loadout Equipped",
                sub: `Equipped loadout "${p.name}"`,
              });
            }}
            updateStatus={updateStatus}
            onCheckUpdate={handleCheckUpdate}
            onDownloadUpdate={handleDownloadUpdate}
            onRestartUpdate={handleRestartUpdate}
          />
        )}
      </main>

      {toast && (
        <div className="toast-banner">
          <div className="toast-body">
            <div className="toast-title">{toast.title}</div>
            <div className="toast-sub">{toast.sub}</div>
          </div>
          {toast.actionLabel && toast.onAction && (
            <button
              className="btn primary small"
              onClick={() => {
                toast.onAction!();
                setToast(null);
              }}
            >
              {toast.actionLabel}
            </button>
          )}
          <button className="btn outline small num-btn" onClick={() => setToast(null)}>✕</button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

```

`src/OverlayLayer.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { CSSProperties } from "react";
import type { Transform } from "./types";

interface Props {
  src: string;
  imgW: number;
  imgH: number;
  t: Transform;
  /** monitor size in physical pixels */
  monitorW: number;
