```tsx
  monitorH: number;
  /** css px per monitor px */
  k: number;
  lowRes?: boolean;
  /** extra decorations (preview only) */
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

/**
 * Shared renderer used by BOTH the live preview and the real overlay window, so
 * what you see in Display is exactly what is projected. Everything is laid out
 * in physical monitor pixels, then uniformly scaled by `k`.
 */
export function OverlayLayer({ src, imgW, imgH, t, monitorW, monitorH, k, lowRes, children }: Props) {
  const filters: string[] = [];
  if (!lowRes) {
    if (t.hue !== 0) filters.push(`hue-rotate(${t.hue}deg)`);
    if (t.brightness !== 100) filters.push(`brightness(${t.brightness}%)`);
    if (t.contrast !== 100) filters.push(`contrast(${t.contrast}%)`);
  }
  const w = (imgW * t.scale) / 100;
  const h = (imgH * t.scale) / 100;
  const img: CSSProperties = {
    position: "absolute",
    left: monitorW / 2 + t.x - w / 2,
    top: monitorH / 2 + t.y - h / 2,
    width: w,
    height: h,
    opacity: t.opacity / 100,
    filter: filters.length ? filters.join(" ") : undefined,
    animation: t.chromaCycle ? `irisRainbow ${t.chromaSpeed || 6}s linear infinite` : undefined,
    pointerEvents: "none",
    userSelect: "none",
  };
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "absolute",
        left: 0,
        top: 0,
        width: monitorW,
        height: monitorH,
        transform: `scale(${k})`,
        transformOrigin: "0 0",
        overflow: "hidden",
      }}
    >
      <img src={src} draggable={false} style={img} alt="" />
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

```

`src/api.ts`:

```ts
import { invoke, convertFileSrc } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import type {
  CompositePreset,
  MonitorInfo,
  OverlayPayload,
  OverlayState,
  Settings,
  Transform,
  UpdateStatus,
  VaultData,
  VaultItem,
} from "./types";

export const api = {
  vaultLoad: () => invoke<VaultData>("vault_load"),
  importAsset: (
    bytes: Uint8Array,
    meta: { name: string; game: string; folder: string; tag: string; width: number; height: number },
  ) =>
    invoke<VaultItem>("import_asset", bytes, {
      headers: {
        "x-name": encodeURIComponent(meta.name),
        "x-game": encodeURIComponent(meta.game),
        "x-folder": encodeURIComponent(meta.folder),
        "x-tag": encodeURIComponent(meta.tag),
        "x-width": String(meta.width),
        "x-height": String(meta.height),
      },
    }),
  saveThumbnail: (id: string, bytes: Uint8Array) =>
    invoke<VaultData>("save_thumbnail", bytes, { headers: { "x-id": id } }),
  updateItem: (
    id: string,
    patch: { name?: string; game?: string; folder?: string; transform?: Transform; tag?: string; favorite?: boolean },
  ) => invoke<VaultData>("vault_update_item", { id, patch }),
  deleteItem: (id: string) => invoke<VaultData>("vault_delete_item", { id }),
  createFolder: (name: string) => invoke<VaultData>("vault_create_folder", { name }),
  deleteFolder: (name: string) => invoke<VaultData>("vault_delete_folder", { name }),
  savePreset: (preset: CompositePreset) => invoke<VaultData>("preset_save", { preset }),
  deletePreset: (id: string) => invoke<VaultData>("preset_delete", { id }),
  openVaultDir: () => invoke<void>("open_vault_dir"),
  overlayApply: (overlay: OverlayState) => invoke<void>("overlay_apply", { overlay }),
  overlayGet: () => invoke<OverlayPayload>("overlay_get"),
  listMonitors: () => invoke<MonitorInfo[]>("list_monitors"),
  settingsGet: () => invoke<Settings>("settings_get"),
  settingsSave: (settings: Settings) => invoke<Settings>("settings_save", { settings }),
  updaterCheck: () => invoke<UpdateStatus>("updater_check"),
  updaterDownloadAndInstall: () => invoke<void>("updater_download_and_install"),
  updaterRestart: () => invoke<void>("updater_restart"),
  updaterGetStatus: () => invoke<UpdateStatus>("updater_get_status"),
};

export const assetUrl = (dir: string, file: string) => convertFileSrc(`${dir}\\${file}`);

```

`src/components/Display.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api, assetUrl } from "../api";
import {
  DEFAULT_TRANSFORM,
  type MonitorInfo,
  type OverlayState,
  type OverlayStatus,
  type Settings,
  type Transform,
  type VaultData,
  type VaultItem,
} from "../types";
import { clamp, monitorLabel, resolutionTag } from "../util";
import { OverlayLayer } from "../OverlayLayer";
import { NumField, SliderRow, Toggle } from "./ui";

interface Props {
  vault: VaultData;
  setVault: (v: VaultData) => void;
  item: VaultItem | null;
  overlay: OverlayState;
  setOverlay: (f: (o: OverlayState) => OverlayState) => void;
  monitors: MonitorInfo[];
  status: OverlayStatus;
  goVault: () => void;
  compositeLive: boolean;
  stopComposite: () => void;
  settings?: Settings;
  saveSettings?: (p: Partial<Settings>) => void;
}

export function Display({
  vault,
  setVault,
  item,
  overlay,
  setOverlay,
  monitors,
  status,
  goVault,
  compositeLive,
  stopComposite,
  settings,
  saveSettings,
}: Props) {
  const [adjusting, setAdjusting] = useState(false);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [snapEnabled, setSnapEnabled] = useState(true);
  const [grid10, setGrid10] = useState(false);
  const [guides, setGuides] = useState<{ gx: number | null; gy: number | null }>({ gx: null, gy: null });
  const [snapBadge, setSnapBadge] = useState("");

  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [boxW, setBoxW] = useState(800);
  const drag = useRef<{ px: number; py: number; x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  const t = overlay.transform;
  const mon = monitors[overlay.monitor] ?? monitors[0];
  const mw = mon?.width ?? 1920;
  const mh = mon?.height ?? 1080;
  const k = boxW / mw;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!box.current) return;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(([e]) => setBoxW(e.contentRect.width));
    ro.observe(box.current);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [item]);

  // show alignment guides while transform values are being changed
  const guideTimer = useRef<number>();
  const flash = useCallback(() => {
    setAdjusting(true);
    window.clearTimeout(guideTimer.current);
    guideTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setAdjusting(false), 1400);
  }, []);

  const patch = useCallback(
    (p: Partial<Transform>) => {
      setOverlay((o) => ({ ...o, transform: { ...o.transform, ...p } }));
      setSaved(false);
      flash();
    },
    [setOverlay, flash],
  );

  if (!item) {
    return (
      <div className="page center">
        <div className="empty card bracket">
          <div className="empty-title">No overlay loaded</div>
          <p>Double-click a design in the Vault to project it onto your screen.</p>
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={goVault}>Open Vault</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const src = assetUrl(vault.assetsDir, item.file);

  // Sizing & Alignment presets
  const fitScreen = () => {
    flash();
    const scale = Math.round(Math.min(mw / item.width, mh / item.height) * 1000) / 10;
    patch({ x: 0, y: 0, scale });
  };

  const fillScreen = () => {
    flash();
    const scale = Math.round(Math.max(mw / item.width, mh / item.height) * 1000) / 10;
    patch({ x: 0, y: 0, scale });
  };

  const native1to1 = () => {
    flash();
    patch({ x: 0, y: 0, scale: 100 });
  };

  const snapCenter = () => {
    flash();
    patch({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  };

  const roundTo10 = () => {
    flash();
    patch({
      x: Math.round(t.x / 10) * 10,
      y: Math.round(t.y / 10) * 10,
    });
  };

  const nudge = (dx: number, dy: number) => patch({ x: t.x + dx * step, y: t.y + dy * step });
  const bw = (item.width * t.scale) / 100;
  const bh = (item.height * t.scale) / 100;

  return (
    <div className="page display">
      <section className="viewport">
        <div className="vp-head">
          <div>
            <div className="vp-title-row">
              <h2>{item.name}</h2>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={`fav-star-btn${item.favorite ? " active" : ""}`}
                title={item.favorite ? "Remove from Favorites" : "Add to Favorites"}
                onClick={async () => {
                  setVault(await api.updateItem(item.id, { favorite: !item.favorite }));
                }}
              >
                {item.favorite ? "\u2605" : "\u2606"}
              </button>
              {t.chromaCycle && (
                <span className="rainbow-badge mono" style={{ fontSize: 10, padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 4 }}>
                  RAINBOW ACTIVE
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="mono muted small">
              {item.width}x{item.height} · {resolutionTag(item.width, item.height)} · {item.game}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="vp-quick-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className={`btn small ${snapEnabled ? "primary" : "outline"}`}
              onClick={() => setSnapEnabled(!snapEnabled)}
              title="Snap to monitor center and edges when dragging near them (Hold Alt to bypass)"
            >
              {snapEnabled ? "✓ Center Snapping" : "Snapping Off"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className={`btn small ${grid10 ? "primary" : "outline"}`}
              onClick={() => setGrid10(!grid10)}
              title="Drag and nudge in 10px increments (or hold Shift while dragging)"
            >
              {grid10 ? "✓ 10px Grid Active" : "10px Grid Snap"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="btn small outline"
              onClick={fitScreen}
              title="Fit overlay entirely inside your monitor"
            >
              Fit Screen
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="btn small outline"
              onClick={native1to1}
              title="Reset overlay scale to 1:1 original native size"
            >
              1:1 Native
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="btn small outline"
              onClick={snapCenter}
              title="Center overlay horizontally and vertically (X:0, Y:0)"
            >
              Center
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          ref={box}
          className="stage bracket bd-grid"
          style={{
            aspectRatio: `${mw} / ${mh}`,
          }}
          onPointerDown={(e) => {
            (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
            drag.current = { px: e.clientX, py: e.clientY, x: t.x, y: t.y };
            flash();
          }}
          onPointerMove={(e) => {
            const d = drag.current;
            if (!d) return;

            let rawX = d.x + (e.clientX - d.px) / k;
            let rawY = d.y + (e.clientY - d.py) / k;

            let gx: number | null = null;
            let gy: number | null = null;
            const labels: string[] = [];

            const useGrid10 = grid10 || e.shiftKey;
            const bypassSnap = e.altKey;

            if (useGrid10) {
              rawX = Math.round(rawX / 10) * 10;
              rawY = Math.round(rawY / 10) * 10;
              labels.push("10px GRID");
            }

            if (snapEnabled && !bypassSnap) {
              const snapDist = 14;

              // Snap X to center (x = 0)
              if (Math.abs(rawX) <= snapDist) {
                rawX = 0;
                gx = mw / 2;
                labels.push("CENTER X");
              } else {
                const leftSnap = -mw / 2 + bw / 2;
                const rightSnap = mw / 2 - bw / 2;
                if (Math.abs(rawX - leftSnap) <= snapDist) {
                  rawX = leftSnap;
                  gx = 0;
                  labels.push("LEFT EDGE");
                } else if (Math.abs(rawX - rightSnap) <= snapDist) {
                  rawX = rightSnap;
                  gx = mw;
                  labels.push("RIGHT EDGE");
                }
              }

              // Snap Y to center (y = 0)
              if (Math.abs(rawY) <= snapDist) {
                rawY = 0;
                gy = mh / 2;
                labels.push("CENTER Y");
              } else {
                const topSnap = -mh / 2 + bh / 2;
                const bottomSnap = mh / 2 - bh / 2;
                if (Math.abs(rawY - topSnap) <= snapDist) {
                  rawY = topSnap;
                  gy = 0;
                  labels.push("TOP EDGE");
                } else if (Math.abs(rawY - bottomSnap) <= snapDist) {
                  rawY = bottomSnap;
                  gy = mh;
                  labels.push("BOTTOM EDGE");
                }
              }
            }

            setGuides({ gx, gy });
            setSnapBadge(labels.join(" · "));
            patch({ x: Math.round(rawX), y: Math.round(rawY) });
          }}
          onPointerUp={() => {
            drag.current = null;
            setGuides({ gx: null, gy: null });
            setSnapBadge("");
          }}
          onPointerCancel={() => {
            drag.current = null;
            setGuides({ gx: null, gy: null });
            setSnapBadge("");
          }}
        >
          {guides.gx !== null && <div className="guide v" style={{ left: `${(guides.gx / mw) * 100}%` }} />}
          {guides.gy !== null && <div className="guide h" style={{ top: `${(guides.gy / mh) * 100}%` }} />}
          {snapBadge && <div className="guide-badge mono">{snapBadge}</div>}

          <OverlayLayer src={src} imgW={item.width} imgH={item.height} t={t} monitorW={mw} monitorH={mh} k={k}>
            {adjusting && (
              <div
                className="bbox"
                style={{ left: mw / 2 + t.x - bw / 2, top: mh / 2 + t.y - bh / 2, width: bw, height: bh, outlineWidth: 1 / k }}
              />
            )}
          </OverlayLayer>
          {adjusting && (
            <>
              <div className="xhair v" />
              <div className="xhair h" />
              <div className="readout mono">
                X {Math.round(t.x)} · Y {Math.round(t.y)} · {t.scale.toFixed(1)}%
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          <div className="stage-tag mono">{mon ? monitorLabel(mon) : "Display"}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="muted small">
          Tip: Drag inside preview to reposition • Hold <span className="mono" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)" }}>Shift</span> for 10px snap • Hold <span className="mono" style={{ color: "var(--pink)" }}>Alt</span> for free drag • Visual guides snap to monitor center &amp; edges.
        </div>
      </section>

      <aside className="inspector card bracket">
        {compositeLive && (
          <div className="note composite-note mono">
            ▣ A layered composite is currently live. <button className="linkbtn" onClick={stopComposite}>Switch to this single overlay</button>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="master">
          <div>
            <div className="master-title">Enable Overlay on Screen</div>
            <div className={`mono small ${status.visible ? "ok" : "muted"}`}>
              {status.visible ? "● PROJECTING" : overlay.enabled ? "○ WAITING FOR GAME" : "○ OFF"}
            </div>
          </div>
          <Toggle
            large
            checked={overlay.enabled}
            onChange={(v) => {
              setOverlay((o) => ({ ...o, enabled: v }));
              // If turning on while in game-mode and target game isn't running, auto-switch to topmost so it shows immediately!
              if (v && settings?.targetMode === "game" && !status.gameRunning) {
                saveSettings?.({ targetMode: "topmost" });
              }
            }}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="display-mode-card" style={{ marginTop: 8, padding: 10, background: "#0c0d18", border: "1px solid var(--border)", borderRadius: 6 }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <span className="mono bold small" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)" }}>Display Target Mode</span>
            <span className={`tag mono ${settings?.targetMode !== "game" ? "ok" : ""}`} style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>
              {settings?.targetMode !== "game" ? "ALWAYS VISIBLE" : "GAME ONLY"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="seg full" style={{ display: "flex", width: "100%" }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className={settings?.targetMode !== "game" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => saveSettings?.({ targetMode: "topmost" })}
              title="Force overlay visible always over any game (Call of Duty, OW2, Apex) or desktop"
            >
              Force on Screen (Any Game / Desktop)
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className={settings?.targetMode === "game" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => saveSettings?.({ targetMode: "game" })}
              title="Overlay only appears when the assigned game process is running"
            >
              Auto-Attach ({settings?.targetExe || "Game"} Only)
            </button>
          </div>
          {settings?.targetMode === "game" && !status.visible && overlay.enabled && (
            <div style={{ marginTop: 8, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8 }}>
              <span className="mono mini warn" style={{ color: "var(--gold)" }}>
                Waiting for {settings?.targetExe || "game"}...
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn primary small"
                style={{ padding: "3px 10px", fontSize: 11 }}
                onClick={() => saveSettings?.({ targetMode: "topmost" })}
                title="Bypass game requirement and force show on screen right now"
              >
                Force Show Now
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <label>Target Monitor</label>
          <select
            className="input full"
            value={overlay.monitor}
            onChange={(e) => setOverlay((o) => ({ ...o, monitor: Number(e.target.value) }))}
          >
            {monitors.map((m) => (
              <option key={m.index} value={m.index}>{monitorLabel(m)}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <h5 className="section">Transform &amp; Alignment</h5>
        <SliderRow
          label="Scale / Size"
          value={t.scale}
          min={10}
          max={200}
          suffix="%"
          onChange={(v) => patch({ scale: clamp(v, 1, 400) })}
          extra={
            <div className="scale-quick-row">
              <button className="btn small outline num-btn" onClick={() => patch({ scale: clamp(t.scale - 1, 1, 400) })} title="Decrease scale by 1%">-</button>
              <button className="btn small outline num-btn" onClick={() => patch({ scale: clamp(t.scale + 1, 1, 400) })} title="Increase scale by 1%">+</button>
              <button className="btn small outline" onClick={native1to1} title="Reset to original 100% native size">1:1 Native</button>
              <button className="btn small outline" onClick={fitScreen} title="Fit to screen dimensions">Fit Screen</button>
            </div>
          }
        />
        <SliderRow label="Opacity" value={t.opacity} min={0} max={100} suffix="%" onChange={(v) => patch({ opacity: clamp(v, 0, 100) })} />

        <div className="field">
          <div className="field-head">
            <label>Position (X / Y offset)</label>
            <select className="input mini" value={step} onChange={(e) => setStep(Number(e.target.value))}>
              <option value={1}>1 px</option>
              <option value={5}>5 px</option>
              <option value={10}>10 px</option>
              <option value={25}>25 px</option>
              <option value={50}>50 px</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="pos-row">
            <div className="xy">
              <span className="mono muted">X</span>
              <NumField value={t.x} min={-8000} max={8000} onChange={(v) => patch({ x: v })} width={84} />
              <span className="mono muted">Y</span>
              <NumField value={t.y} min={-8000} max={8000} onChange={(v) => patch({ y: v })} width={84} />
            </div>
            <div className="dpad">
              <button onClick={() => nudge(0, -1)} style={{ gridArea: "u" }} aria-label="Up">▲</button>
              <button onClick={() => nudge(-1, 0)} style={{ gridArea: "l" }} aria-label="Left">◀</button>
              <button onClick={snapCenter} style={{ gridArea: "c" }} aria-label="Center" title="Snap to center">●</button>
              <button onClick={() => nudge(1, 0)} style={{ gridArea: "r" }} aria-label="Right">▶</button>
              <button onClick={() => nudge(0, 1)} style={{ gridArea: "d" }} aria-label="Down">▼</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="btn-row" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
            <button className="btn small outline" onClick={snapCenter} title="Set X:0, Y:0">Snap Center</button>
            <button className="btn small outline" onClick={roundTo10} title="Round current X & Y to nearest 10px">Round 10px</button>
            <button className="btn small outline" onClick={fitScreen} title="Scale & center to fit monitor">Fit Screen</button>
            <button className="btn small outline" onClick={fillScreen} title="Scale & center to fill monitor without border gaps">Fill Screen</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <h5 className="section">Color &amp; Visual Tweaks</h5>

        {/* Rainbow / Chroma Cycle Card */}
        <div
          className="rainbow-card"
          style={{
            marginBottom: 14,
            padding: 12,
            background: t.chromaCycle ? "rgba(236, 72, 153, 0.08)" : "#0c0d18",
            border: t.chromaCycle ? "1px solid var(--pink)" : "1px solid var(--border)",
            borderRadius: 6,
            boxShadow: t.chromaCycle ? "0 0 16px rgba(236, 72, 153, 0.2)" : undefined,
            transition: "all 0.2s ease",
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>🌈</span>
              <div>
                <div className="bold" style={{ fontSize: 13, color: t.chromaCycle ? "var(--pink)" : "var(--text)" }}>
                  Animated Rainbow / Chroma Cycle
                </div>
                <div className="mono mini muted" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>
                  360° animated spectrum cycling · 0% CPU cost
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Toggle
              checked={Boolean(t.chromaCycle)}
              onChange={(v) => patch({ chromaCycle: v })}
            />
          </div>

          {t.chromaCycle && (
            <div style={{ marginTop: 12, borderTop: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)", paddingTop: 10 }}>
              <SliderRow
                label="Cycle Speed"
                value={t.chromaSpeed || 6}
                min={1}
                max={25}
                suffix="s"
                onChange={(v) => patch({ chromaSpeed: v })}
              />
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, marginTop: 8 }}>
                {[
                  { label: "⚡ Turbo (2s)", sec: 2 },
                  { label: "🌈 Smooth (6s)", sec: 6 },
                  { label: "✨ Ambient (12s)", sec: 12 },
                  { label: "🌌 Slow (20s)", sec: 20 },
                ].map((preset) => (
                  <button
                    key={preset.sec}
                    type="button"
                    className={`btn mini ${(t.chromaSpeed || 6) === preset.sec ? "primary" : "outline"}`}
                    style={{ flex: 1, padding: "3px 4px", fontSize: 10 }}
                    onClick={() => patch({ chromaSpeed: preset.sec })}
                  >
                    {preset.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <SliderRow
          label="Hue Shift / Tint"
          value={t.hue}
          min={-180}
          max={180}
          suffix="°"
          sliderClassName="hue-slider"
          onChange={(v) => patch({ hue: clamp(v, -180, 180) })}
          extra={
            <div className="swatches-row">
              {[
                { label: "Emerald (Matrix)", color: "#10B981", hue: 140 },
                { label: "Celestial Cyan", color: "#06B6D4", hue: 180 },
                { label: "Cyber Purple", color: "#8B5CF6", hue: -95 },
                { label: "Neon Pink", color: "#EC4899", hue: -35 },
                { label: "Solar Amber", color: "#F59E0B", hue: 45 },
                { label: "Crimson Red", color: "#EF4444", hue: 0 },
              ].map((s) => (
                <button
                  key={s.color}
                  type="button"
                  className={`swatch-btn${Math.abs(t.hue - s.hue) < 5 ? " active" : ""}`}
                  style={{ backgroundColor: s.color, color: s.color }}
                  title={`${s.label} (${s.hue}°)`}
                  onClick={() => patch({ hue: s.hue })}
                />
              ))}
              <button
                type="button"
                className="swatch-btn swatch-reset"
                title="Reset color tint to original asset colors"
                onClick={() => patch({ hue: 0, brightness: 100, contrast: 100 })}
              >
                Reset
              </button>
            </div>
          }
        />
        <SliderRow label="Brightness" value={t.brightness} min={50} max={150} suffix="%" onChange={(v) => patch({ brightness: clamp(v, 0, 300) })} />
        <SliderRow label="Contrast" value={t.contrast} min={50} max={150} suffix="%" onChange={(v) => patch({ contrast: clamp(v, 0, 300) })} />

        <div className="btn-row stick">
          <button
            className="btn primary grow"
            onClick={async () => {
              setVault(await api.updateItem(item.id, { transform: t }));
              setSaved(true);
            }}
          >
            {saved ? "✓ Profile Saved" : "Save Profile to Vault Item"}
          </button>
          <button
            className="btn outline"
            title="Reset to saved calibration (or defaults)"
            onClick={() => patch({ ...(item.transform ?? DEFAULT_TRANSFORM) })}
          >
            Reset
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

```

`src/components/LayerDrawer.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { assetUrl } from "../api";
import { ELEMENT_TAGS, type VaultData, type VaultItem } from "../types";
import { gameClass, resolutionTag } from "../util";

const GAME_OPTIONS = ["All", "Overwatch", "Valorant", "Marvel Rivals", "Apex Legends", "Fortnite", "Dead by Daylight", "Other"];

/** Left panel: compact Vault browser that appends elements to the stack. */
export function LayerDrawer({ vault, onAdd }: { vault: VaultData; onAdd: (item: VaultItem) => void }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [game, setGame] = useState("All");
  const [tag, setTag] = useState("all");

  const items = useMemo(() => {
    const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    return vault.items
      .filter((i) => (game === "All" || i.game === game) && (tag === "all" || i.tag === tag) && (!s || i.name.toLowerCase().includes(s)))
      .sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
  }, [vault.items, q, game, tag]);

  return (
    <aside className="drawer card bracket">
      <div className="panel-title">
        <span>Vault Drawer</span>
        <span className="mono muted small">{items.length}</span>
      </div>
      <input className="input full" placeholder="Search elements..." value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} />
      <div className="drawer-filters">
        <select className="input" value={game} onChange={(e) => setGame(e.target.value)}>
          {GAME_OPTIONS.map((g) => <option key={g}>{g}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select className="input" value={tag} onChange={(e) => setTag(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">All elements</option>
          {ELEMENT_TAGS.map((t) => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.label}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div className="drawer-grid">
        {items.map((item) => (
          <div
            key={item.id}
            className="mini"
            draggable
            onDragStart={(e) => {
              e.dataTransfer.setData("application/x-iris-item", item.id);
              e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "copy";
            }}
            onDoubleClick={() => onAdd(item)}
            title="Drag onto the canvas, or double-click to add"
          >
            <div className="checker mini-thumb">
              <img draggable={false} loading="lazy" src={assetUrl(item.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir, item.thumb ?? item.file)} alt="" />
              <span className="mono mini-res">{resolutionTag(item.width, item.height)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="mini-name">{item.name}</div>
            <div className="mini-row">
              <span className={`chip ${gameClass(item.game)}`}>{item.game}</span>
              {item.tag && <span className="chip folder">{ELEMENT_TAGS.find((t) => t.id === item.tag)?.label ?? item.tag}</span>}
            </div>
            <button className="btn small outline add" onClick={() => onAdd(item)}>+ Add as Layer</button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {!items.length && <div className="muted small empty-note">No matching elements. Upload PNGs in the Vault and tag them (Ult Icon, Minimap…).</div>}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

```

`src/components/LayerPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { assetUrl } from "../api";
import { BLEND_MODES, LINK_GROUPS, ROLES, fxOf, type Layer, type LayerFx, type Role, type VaultData } from "../types";
import { roleBadge } from "../layers";
import { clamp } from "../util";
import { CopyIcon, EyeIcon, LockIcon, TrashIcon } from "./icons";
import { NumField, SliderRow } from "./ui";

interface Props {
  vault: VaultData;
  layers: Layer[];
  setLayers: (f: (l: Layer[]) => Layer[]) => void;
  selectedId: string | null;
  select: (id: string | null) => void;
  update: (id: string, p: Partial<Layer>) => void;
  updateFx: (id: string, p: Partial<LayerFx>) => void;
  duplicate: (id: string) => void;
  remove: (id: string) => void;
  roleHotkeys: Record<string, string>;
}

/** Right panel: stack manager (top) + selected-layer inspector (bottom). */
export function LayerPanel({ vault, layers, setLayers, selectedId, select, update, updateFx, duplicate, remove, roleHotkeys }: Props) {
  const [dragId, setDragId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [overId, setOverId] = useState<{ id: string; after: boolean } | null>(null);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const items = new Map(vault.items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
  const sel = layers.find((l) => l.id === selectedId) ?? null;

  const drop = () => {
    if (dragId && overId && dragId !== overId.id) {
      setLayers((ls) => {
        const moving = ls.find((l) => l.id === dragId)!;
        const rest = ls.filter((l) => l.id !== dragId);
        const idx = rest.findIndex((l) => l.id === overId.id) + (overId.after ? 1 : 0);
        rest.splice(idx, 0, moving);
        return rest;
      });
    }
    setDragId(null);
    setOverId(null);
  };

  return (
    <aside className="stackpanel">
      <section className="card bracket stack">
        <div className="panel-title">
          <span>Layer Stack</span>
          <span className="mono muted small">{layers.length} layers Â· top = front</span>
        </div>
        <div className="rows">
          {layers.map((l, i) => {
            const it = items.get(l.vaultItemId);
            const z = layers.length - 1 - i;
            return (
              <div
                key={l.id}
                className={`lrow${l.id === selectedId ? " sel" : ""}${!l.visible ? " hidden" : ""}${dragId === l.id ? " dragging" : ""}${overId?.id === l.id ? (overId.after ? " drop-after" : " drop-before") : ""}`}
                onClick={() => select(l.id)}
                onDragOver={(e) => {
                  if (!dragId) return;
                  e.preventDefault();
                  const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
                  setOverId({ id: l.id, after: e.clientY > r.top + r.height / 2 });
                }}
                onDrop={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  drop();
                }}
              >
                <span
                  className="handle"
                  draggable
                  title="Drag to reorder"
                  onDragStart={(e) => {
                    const row = e.currentTarget.closest(".lrow") as HTMLElement;
                    e.dataTransfer.setDragImage(row, 12, 12);
                    e.dataTransfer.setData("application/x-iris-layer", l.id);
                    setDragId(l.id);
                    select(l.id);
                  }}
                  onDragEnd={() => {
                    setDragId(null);
                    setOverId(null);
                  }}
                >
                  â‹®â‹®
                </span>
                <button className={`ibtn${l.visible ? " on" : ""}`} title={l.visible ? "Hide" : "Show"} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); update(l.id, { visible: !l.visible }); }}>
                  <EyeIcon off={!l.visible} />
                </button>
                <button className={`ibtn${l.locked ? " lock" : ""}`} title={l.locked ? "Unlock" : "Lock"} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); update(l.id, { locked: !l.locked }); }}>
                  <LockIcon open={!l.locked} />
                </button>
                <div className="checker lthumb">
                  {it ? <img draggable={false} src={assetUrl(it.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir, it.thumb ?? it.file)} alt="" /> : <span className="mono">?</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="lname">
                  <div className="lname-top">{l.name}</div>
                  <div className="lname-sub">
                    <span className={`rbadge r-${l.role}`}>{roleBadge(l.role)}</span>
                    <span className="mono muted">z{z}</span>
                    {fxOf(l).group && <span className="rbadge link-badge" title="Linked group â€” moves together">ðŸ”—{fxOf(l).group}</span>}
                    {(fxOf(l).cropT || fxOf(l).cropR || fxOf(l).cropB || fxOf(l).cropL) ? <span className="mono muted" title="Cropped">âœ‚</span> : null}
                    {!it && <span className="mono warn">MISSING</span>}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button className="ibtn" title="Duplicate" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); duplicate(l.id); }}><CopyIcon /></button>
                <button className="ibtn del" title="Delete layer" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); remove(l.id); }}><TrashIcon /></button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {!layers.length && (
            <div className="muted small empty-note">
              The stack is empty. Add elements from the Vault Drawer, drop them on the canvas, or right-click a Vault card â†’ â€œSend to Layering Stackâ€.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="card bracket inspector-l">
        <div className="panel-title">
          <span>Layer Inspector</span>
          {sel && <span className="mono muted small">{items.get(sel.vaultItemId)?.width}Ã—{items.get(sel.vaultItemId)?.height}</span>}
        </div>
        {!sel ? (
          <div className="muted small empty-note">Select a layer to edit its transform.</div>
        ) : (
          <fieldset className="plain" disabled={sel.locked}>
            {sel.locked && <div className="note mono">ðŸ”’ LAYER LOCKED â€” unlock to edit</div>}
            <div className="field">
              <label>Layer Tag / Role</label>
              <select className="input full" value={sel.role} onChange={(e) => update(sel.id, { role: e.target.value as Role })}>
                {ROLES.map((r) => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.label}</option>)}
              </select>
              {roleHotkeys[sel.role] && <div className="mono small muted hk">âŒ¨ toggles with {roleHotkeys[sel.role]}</div>}
            </div>

            <div className="field">
              <div className="field-head">
                <label>Position (X / Y)</label>
                <select className="input mini" value={step} onChange={(e) => setStep(Number(e.target.value))}>
                  <option value={1}>1 px</option><option value={5}>5 px</option><option value={25}>25 px</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="pos-row">
                <div className="xy">
                  <span className="mono muted">X</span>
                  <NumField value={sel.x} min={-8000} max={8000} width={84} onChange={(v) => update(sel.id, { x: v })} />
                  <span className="mono muted">Y</span>
                  <NumField value={sel.y} min={-8000} max={8000} width={84} onChange={(v) => update(sel.id, { y: v })} />
                </div>
                <div className="dpad">
                  <button style={{ gridArea: "u" }} onClick={() => update(sel.id, { y: sel.y - step })}>â–²</button>
                  <button style={{ gridArea: "l" }} onClick={() => update(sel.id, { x: sel.x - step })}>â—€</button>
                  <button style={{ gridArea: "c" }} title="Center" onClick={() => update(sel.id, { x: 0, y: 0 })}>â—</button>
                  <button style={{ gridArea: "r" }} onClick={() => update(sel.id, { x: sel.x + step })}>â–¶</button>
                  <button style={{ gridArea: "d" }} onClick={() => update(sel.id, { y: sel.y + step })}>â–¼</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <SliderRow
              label="Scale"
              value={sel.scale}
              min={10}
              max={200}
              suffix="%"
              onChange={(v) => update(sel.id, { scale: clamp(v, 1, 400) })}
              extra={<button className="btn small outline" onClick={() => update(sel.id, { scale: 100 })}>Reset to 1:1 Native</button>}
            />
            <SliderRow label="Layer Opacity" value={sel.opacity} min={0} max={100} suffix="%" onChange={(v) => update(sel.id, { opacity: clamp(v, 0, 100) })} />
            <SliderRow
              label="Hue / Tint"
              value={sel.hue}
              min={-180}
              max={180}
              suffix="Â°"
              sliderClassName="hue-slider"
              onChange={(v) => update(sel.id, { hue: clamp(v, -180, 180) })}
              extra={
                <div className="swatches-row">
                  {[
                    { label: "Emerald", color: "#10B981", hue: 140 },
                    { label: "Cyan", color: "#06B6D4", hue: 180 },
                    { label: "Purple", color: "#8B5CF6", hue: -95 },
                    { label: "Pink", color: "#EC4899", hue: -35 },
                    { label: "Amber", color: "#F59E0B", hue: 45 },
                    { label: "Red", color: "#EF4444", hue: 0 },
                  ].map((s) => (
                    <button
                      key={s.color}
                      type="button"
                      className={`swatch-btn${Math.abs(sel.hue - s.hue) < 5 ? " active" : ""}`}
                      style={{ backgroundColor: s.color, color: s.color }}
                      title={`${s.label} (${s.hue}Â°)`}
                      onClick={() => update(sel.id, { hue: s.hue })}
                    />
                  ))}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="swatch-btn swatch-reset"
                    title="Reset layer hue"
                    onClick={() => update(sel.id, { hue: 0 })}
                  >
                    Reset
                  </button>
                </div>
              }
            />
            <FxControls layer={sel} updateFx={updateFx} />
          </fieldset>
        )}
      </section>
    </aside>
  );
}


/** Photoshop-style non-destructive tools: blend mode, crop, flip, link group. */
function FxControls({ layer, updateFx }: { layer: Layer; updateFx: (id: string, p: Partial<LayerFx>) => void }) {
  const fx = fxOf(layer);
  const crops = [
    ["cropT", "Top"],
    ["cropB", "Bottom"],
    ["cropL", "Left"],
    ["cropR", "Right"],
  ] as const;
  const anyCrop = fx.cropT || fx.cropR || fx.cropB || fx.cropL;
  return (
    <>
      <h5 className="section">Blend, Crop &amp; Link</h5>
      <div className="field">
        <label>Blend Mode</label>
        <select className="input full" value={fx.blend || "normal"} onChange={(e) => updateFx(layer.id, { blend: e.target.value })}>
          {BLEND_MODES.map((b) => <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>{b.label}</option>)}
        </select>
        <div className="muted small hk">Blends with the layers beneath it in this stack.</div>
      </div>
      <div className="field">
        <div className="field-head">
          <label>Crop (% trimmed)</label>
          <button className="btn small outline" disabled={!anyCrop} onClick={() => updateFx(layer.id, { cropT: 0, cropR: 0, cropB: 0, cropL: 0 })}>Reset Crop</button>
        </div>
        <div className="crop-grid">
          {crops.map(([k, label]) => (
            <div key={k} className="xy">
              <span className="mono muted">{label[0]}</span>
              <NumField
                value={fx[k]}
                min={0}
                max={95}
                width={64}
                onChange={(v) => updateFx(layer.id, { [k]: clamp(v, 0, 95) } as Partial<LayerFx>)}
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="field">
        <div className="field-head">
          <label>Mirror</label>
        </div>
        <div className="btn-row">
          <button className={`btn small ${fx.flipX ? "primary" : "outline"}`} onClick={() => updateFx(layer.id, { flipX: !fx.flipX })}>? Flip Horizontal</button>
          <button className={`btn small ${fx.flipY ? "primary" : "outline"}`} onClick={() => updateFx(layer.id, { flipY: !fx.flipY })}>? Flip Vertical</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="field">
        <label>Link Group</label>
        <select className="input full" value={fx.group ?? ""} onChange={(e) => updateFx(layer.id, { group: e.target.value || null })}>
          <option value="">Not linked</option>
          {LINK_GROUPS.map((g) => <option key={g} value={g}>?? Group {g} — moves together</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

```

`src/components/Layering.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "../api";
import {
  ALL_RES,
  RES_GROUPS,
  fxOf,
  type CanvasRes,
  type CompositePreset,
  type Layer,
  type LayerFx,
  type MonitorInfo,
  type Settings,
  type VaultData,
  type VaultItem,
} from "../types";
import { layerBox, snapBox, uid, withZ, type Box } from "../layers";
import { clamp, monitorLabel } from "../util";
import { LayerDrawer } from "./LayerDrawer";
import { LayerPanel } from "./LayerPanel";
import { REF_OPTIONS, SceneImages, type RefKey } from "./SceneView";
import { Modal } from "./ui";

interface Props {
  vault: VaultData;
  setVault: (v: VaultData) => void;
  layers: Layer[];
  setLayers: (f: (l: Layer[]) => Layer[]) => void;
  addLayer: (item: VaultItem, at?: { x: number; y: number }) => string;
  live: boolean;
  setLive: (v: boolean) => void;
  push: () => void;
  monitors: MonitorInfo[];
  monitor: number;
  setMonitor: (i: number) => void;
  settings: Settings;
  activePresetId: string | null;
  setActivePresetId: (id: string | null) => void;
  loadPreset: (p: CompositePreset, push: boolean) => void;
  /** authoring canvas resolution; null = match the monitor */
  canvasRes: CanvasRes | null;
  setCanvasRes: (r: CanvasRes | null) => void;
}

type Drag =
  | {
      mode: "move";
      id: string;
      px: number;
      py: number;
      starts: { id: string; x: number; y: number }[];
      box: Box;
      others: Box[];
    }
  | { mode: "resize"; id: string; hx: number; ax: number; ay: number; cx: number; cy: number; scale: number; visW: number }
  | { mode: "crop"; id: string; edges: string }
  | null;

const SNAP_PX = 8;

/** Crop in *visual* space (what the user sees after flipping) <-> stored source-space crop. */
const visCrop = (fx: LayerFx) => ({
  L: fx.flipX ? fx.cropR : fx.cropL,
  R: fx.flipX ? fx.cropL : fx.cropR,
  T: fx.flipY ? fx.cropB : fx.cropT,
  B: fx.flipY ? fx.cropT : fx.cropB,
});
const fromVis = (fx: LayerFx, v: { L: number; R: number; T: number; B: number }): Partial<LayerFx> => ({
  cropL: fx.flipX ? v.R : v.L,
  cropR: fx.flipX ? v.L : v.R,
  cropT: fx.flipY ? v.B : v.T,
  cropB: fx.flipY ? v.T : v.B,
});

const resKey = (r: CanvasRes | null) => (r ? `${r.w}x${r.h}` : "auto");
const aspectTag = (w: number, h: number) => {
  const a = w / h;
  if (Math.abs(a - 16 / 9) < 0.03) return "16:9";
  if (Math.abs(a - 21 / 9) < 0.12) return "21:9";
  if (Math.abs(a - 16 / 10) < 0.03) return "16:10";
  if (Math.abs(a - 4 / 3) < 0.03) return "4:3";
  return `${a.toFixed(2)}:1`;
};

export function Layering(p: Props) {
  const { vault, layers, setLayers, monitors, canvasRes } = p;
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState<"fit" | 50 | 100>("fit");
  const [ref, setRef] = useState<RefKey>("grid");
  const [customShot, setCustomShot] = useState<string | null>(() => localStorage.getItem("iris_custom_ref_shot"));
  const [guides, setGuides] = useState<{ xs: number[]; ys: number[] }>({ xs: [], ys: [] });
  const [saveOpen, setSaveOpen] = useState(false);
  const [exportOpen, setExportOpen] = useState(false);
  const [importOpen, setImportOpen] = useState(false);
  const [tool, setTool] = useState<"move" | "crop">("move");
  const [snapOn, setSnapOn] = useState(true);
  const [grid10, setGrid10] = useState(false);
  const [soloId, setSoloId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [chopPct, setChopPct] = useState(50);
  const stage = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const drag = useRef<Drag>(null);
  const customInput = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const mon = monitors[p.monitor] ?? monitors[0];
  const W = canvasRes?.w ?? mon?.width ?? 1920;
  const H = canvasRes?.h ?? mon?.height ?? 1080;
  const items = useMemo(() => new Map(vault.items.map((i) => [i.id, i])), [vault.items]);
  const sel = layers.find((l) => l.id === selectedId) ?? null;
  const activePreset = vault.presets.find((x) => x.id === p.activePresetId) ?? null;

  const update = useCallback(
    (id: string, patch: Partial<Layer>) => setLayers((ls) => ls.map((l) => (l.id === id ? { ...l, ...patch } : l))),
    [setLayers],
  );
  const updateFx = useCallback(
    (id: string, patch: Partial<LayerFx>) =>
      setLayers((ls) => ls.map((l) => (l.id === id ? { ...l, fx: { ...fxOf(l), ...patch } } : l))),
    [setLayers],
  );

  /** Move a layer plus every unlocked layer linked to it by the same group. */
  const shiftLayers = useCallback(
    (id: string, dx: number, dy: number) =>
      setLayers((ls) => {
        const src = ls.find((l) => l.id === id);
        const g = src ? fxOf(src).group : null;
        return ls.map((l) =>
          (l.id === id || (g && fxOf(l).group === g)) && !l.locked ? { ...l, x: Math.round(l.x + dx), y: Math.round(l.y + dy) } : l,
        );
      }),
    [setLayers],
  );

  const duplicate = (id: string) => {
    const src = layers.find((l) => l.id === id);
    if (!src) return;
    const copy: Layer = {
      ...src,
      id: uid(),
      name: src.name + " copy",
      x: src.x + 20,
      y: src.y + 20,
      locked: false,
      fx: { ...fxOf(src), group: null },
    };
    setLayers((ls) => {
      const i = ls.findIndex((l) => l.id === id);
      return i < 0 ? ls : [...ls.slice(0, i), copy, ...ls.slice(i)];
    });
    setSelectedId(copy.id);
  };
  const remove = (id: string) => {
    setLayers((ls) => ls.filter((l) => l.id !== id));
    if (selectedId === id) setSelectedId(null);
    if (soloId === id) setSoloId(null);
  };

  const boxOf = (l: Layer): Box | null => {
    const it = items.get(l.vaultItemId);
    return it ? layerBox(l, it.width, it.height) : null;
  };
  const toMonitor = (cx: number, cy: number) => {
    const r = stage.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
    const k = r.width / W;
    return { mx: (cx - r.left) / k, my: (cy - r.top) / k, k };
  };

  /** Photoshop-style chop: split the selected layer into two non-destructive crops. */
  function chop(axis: "v" | "h") {
    if (!sel || sel.locked) return;
    const fx = fxOf(sel);
    const v = visCrop(fx);
    const f = clamp(chopPct, 5, 95) / 100;
    const a = { ...v };
    const b = { ...v };
    let nameA: string;
    let nameB: string;
    if (axis === "v") {
      const m = v.L + (100 - v.L - v.R) * f;
      a.R = Math.round((100 - m) * 10) / 10;
      b.L = Math.round(m * 10) / 10;
      nameA = " (left)";
      nameB = " (right)";
    } else {
      const m = v.T + (100 - v.T - v.B) * f;
      a.B = Math.round((100 - m) * 10) / 10;
      b.T = Math.round(m * 10) / 10;
      nameA = " (top)";
      nameB = " (bottom)";
    }
    const base = sel.name.replace(/ \((left|right|top|bottom)\)$/, "");
    const copy: Layer = { ...sel, id: uid(), name: base + nameB, fx: { ...fx, ...fromVis(fx, b) } };
    setLayers((ls) => {
      const i = ls.findIndex((l) => l.id === sel.id);
      if (i < 0) return ls;
      const first: Layer = { ...ls[i], name: base + nameA, fx: { ...fx, ...fromVis(fx, a) } };
      return [...ls.slice(0, i), copy, first, ...ls.slice(i + 1)];
    });
    setSelectedId(copy.id);
  }

  function align(kind: "l" | "cx" | "r" | "t" | "cy" | "b") {
    if (!sel || sel.locked) return;
    const b = boxOf(sel);
    if (!b) return;
    let dx = 0;
    let dy = 0;
    if (kind === "l") dx = -W / 2 - b.l;
    if (kind === "cx") dx = -(b.l + b.w / 2);
    if (kind === "r") dx = W / 2 - (b.l + b.w);
    if (kind === "t") dy = -H / 2 - b.t;
    if (kind === "cy") dy = -(b.t + b.h / 2);
    if (kind === "b") dy = H / 2 - (b.t + b.h);
    shiftLayers(sel.id, dx, dy);
  }

  function onPointerDown(e: React.PointerEvent) {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;
    const { mx, my } = toMonitor(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
    const handle = target.dataset.handle;
    const cropEdges = target.dataset.crop;
    if (cropEdges && sel && !sel.locked) {
      stage.current!.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      drag.current = { mode: "crop", id: sel.id, edges: cropEdges };
      return;
    }
    if (handle && sel && !sel.locked) {
      const b = boxOf(sel)!;
      const [hx] = handle.split(",").map(Number);
      stage.current!.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      const [, hy] = handle.split(",").map(Number);
      drag.current = {
        mode: "resize",
        id: sel.id,
        hx,
        ax: hx < 0 ? b.l + b.w : b.l,
        ay: hy < 0 ? b.t + b.h : b.t,
        cx: sel.x,
        cy: sel.y,
        scale: sel.scale,
        visW: b.w,
      };
      return;
    }
    const hit = layers.find((l) => {
      const b = boxOf(l);
      if (!l.visible || !b) return false;
      const cx = mx - W / 2;
      const cy = my - H / 2;
      return cx >= b.l && cx <= b.l + b.w && cy >= b.t && cy <= b.t + b.h;
    });
    setSelectedId(hit?.id ?? null);
    if (hit && !hit.locked) {
      const g = fxOf(hit).group;
      const members = layers.filter((l) => l.id === hit.id || (g && fxOf(l).group === g)).filter((l) => !l.locked);
      const moving = new Set(members.map((m) => m.id));
      stage.current!.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      drag.current = {
        mode: "move",
        id: hit.id,
        px: mx,
        py: my,
        starts: members.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, x: m.x, y: m.y })),
        box: boxOf(hit)!,
        others: layers.filter((l) => l.visible && !moving.has(l.id)).map(boxOf).filter((b): b is Box => !!b),
      };
    }
  }

  function onPointerMove(e: React.PointerEvent) {
    const d = drag.current;
    if (!d) return;
    const { mx, my, k } = toMonitor(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    if (d.mode === "move") {
      let dx = mx - d.px;
      let dy = my - d.py;
      let xs: number[] = [];
      let ys: number[] = [];
      const useGrid = grid10 || e.shiftKey;
      if (!e.altKey && (snapOn || useGrid)) {
        const moved: Box = { ...d.box, l: d.box.l + dx, t: d.box.t + dy };
        const s = snapBox(moved, W, H, snapOn ? d.others : [], snapOn ? SNAP_PX / k : 0, useGrid ? 10 : 0);
        dx += s.dx;
        dy += s.dy;
        xs = s.xs;
        ys = s.ys;
      }
      setGuides({ xs, ys });
      const byId = new Map(d.starts.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
      setLayers((ls) =>
        ls.map((l) => {
          const s = byId.get(l.id);
          return s ? { ...l, x: Math.round(s.x + dx), y: Math.round(s.y + dy) } : l;
        }),
      );
    } else if (d.mode === "resize") {
      const newW = (mx - W / 2 - d.ax) * d.hx;
      const scale = clamp(d.scale * (newW / d.visW), 5, 400);
      const r = scale / d.scale;
      update(d.id, {
        scale: Math.round(scale * 10) / 10,
        x: Math.round(d.ax + (d.cx - d.ax) * r),
        y: Math.round(d.ay + (d.cy - d.ay) * r),
      });
    } else {
      const l = layers.find((x) => x.id === d.id);
      const it = l && items.get(l.vaultItemId);
      if (!l || !it) return;
      const fx = fxOf(l);
      const lw = (it.width * l.scale) / 100;
      const lh = (it.height * l.scale) / 100;
      const px = ((mx - (W / 2 + l.x - lw / 2)) / lw) * 100;
      const py = ((my - (H / 2 + l.y - lh / 2)) / lh) * 100;
      const v = visCrop(fx);
      const rd = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 10) / 10;
      if (d.edges.includes("l")) v.L = rd(clamp(px, 0, 98 - v.R));
      if (d.edges.includes("r")) v.R = rd(clamp(100 - px, 0, 98 - v.L));
      if (d.edges.includes("t")) v.T = rd(clamp(py, 0, 98 - v.B));
      if (d.edges.includes("b")) v.B = rd(clamp(100 - py, 0, 98 - v.T));
      updateFx(l.id, fromVis(fx, v));
    }
  }
  const endDrag = () => {
    drag.current = null;
    setGuides({ xs: [], ys: [] });
  };

  // Keyboard: arrows nudge (Shift = 10px), Ctrl+D duplicate, Delete removes, C toggles crop tool.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const t = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (t && (t.tagName === "INPUT" || t.tagName === "TEXTAREA" || t.tagName === "SELECT" || t.isContentEditable)) return;
      if (!sel || saveOpen || exportOpen || importOpen) return;
      const n = e.shiftKey ? 10 : 1;
      const arrows: Record<string, [number, number]> = {
        ArrowLeft: [-n, 0],
        ArrowRight: [n, 0],
        ArrowUp: [0, -n],
        ArrowDown: [0, n],
      };
      if (arrows[e.key]) {
        e.preventDefault();
        shiftLayers(sel.id, arrows[e.key][0], arrows[e.key][1]);
      } else if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "d") {
        e.preventDefault();
        duplicate(sel.id);
      } else if (e.key === "Delete" && !sel.locked) {
        remove(sel.id);
      } else if (e.key.toLowerCase() === "c" && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
        setTool((t0) => (t0 === "crop" ? "move" : "crop"));
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [sel, layers, saveOpen, exportOpen, importOpen]);

  function handleCustomScreenshot(f: File | undefined) {
    if (!f) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (ev) => {
      const res = ev.target?.result as string;
      if (res) {
        setCustomShot(res);
        try {
          localStorage.setItem("iris_custom_ref_shot", res);
        } catch {
          // localStorage quota exceeded, keep in memory
        }
        setRef("custom");
      }
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(f);
  }

  const cycleRes = (dir: 1 | -1) => {
    const list: (CanvasRes | null)[] = [null, ...ALL_RES.map((r) => ({ w: r.w, h: r.h }))];
    const cur = list.findIndex((r) => resKey(r) === resKey(canvasRes));
    const next = list[(((cur < 0 ? 0 : cur) + dir) % list.length + list.length) % list.length];
    p.setCanvasRes(next);
  };

  const stageWidth = zoom === "fit" ? "100%" : `${(W * zoom) / 100}px`;

  const bgImage = ref === "custom" && customShot ? `url(${customShot})` : undefined;

  const previewLayers = soloId ? layers.map((l) => (l.id === soloId ? { ...l, visible: true } : { ...l, visible: false })) : layers;
  const selBox = sel && sel.visible ? boxOf(sel) : null;
  const selFx = sel ? fxOf(sel) : null;
  const cropped = !!selFx && (selFx.cropT || selFx.cropR || selFx.cropB || selFx.cropL);
  const pct = (n: number, total: number) => `${(n / total) * 100}%`;

  return (
    <div className="layering">
      <input
        ref={customInput}
        type="file"
        accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
        hidden
        onChange={(e) => handleCustomScreenshot(e.target.files?.[0])}
      />
      <PresetBar
        {...p}
        onSave={() => setSaveOpen(true)}
        onExport={() => setExportOpen(true)}
        onImport={() => setImportOpen(true)}
        clear={() => { setLayers(() => []); p.setActivePresetId(null); setSelectedId(null); setSoloId(null); }}
      />
      <div className="studio">
        <LayerDrawer vault={vault} onAdd={(it) => setSelectedId(p.addLayer(it))} />

        <section className="center-col">
          <div className="viewport-scroll">
            <div
              ref={stage}
              className={`stage lstage bracket bd-${ref === "grid" ? "grid" : "ref"}`}
              style={{
                width: stageWidth,
                aspectRatio: `${W} / ${H}`,
                backgroundImage: bgImage,
                backgroundSize: "100% 100%",
              }}
              onPointerDown={onPointerDown}
              onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
              onPointerUp={endDrag}
              onPointerCancel={endDrag}
              onDragOver={(e) => e.dataTransfer.types.includes("application/x-iris-item") && e.preventDefault()}
              onDrop={(e) => {
                const id = e.dataTransfer.getData("application/x-iris-item");
                const it = items.get(id);
                if (!it) return;
                e.preventDefault();
                const { mx, my } = toMonitor(e.clientX, e.clientY);
                setSelectedId(p.addLayer(it, { x: Math.round(mx - W / 2), y: Math.round(my - H / 2) }));
              }}
            >
              {ref === "custom" && !customShot && (
                <div className="empty-ref-prompt" onClick={() => customInput.current?.click()}>
                  <div className="prompt-icon">&#x1F4F7;</div>
                  <div className="prompt-title">No in-game screenshot uploaded</div>
                  <div className="muted small">Click to upload a full-screen screenshot (1080p, 1440p, 4K) from Overwatch 2, Valorant, Marvel Rivals, etc.</div>
                  <button type="button" className="btn small primary prompt-btn">Upload Screenshot</button>
                </div>
              )}
              <SceneImages layers={previewLayers} vault={vault} refW={W} refH={H} />

              {guides.xs.map((x) => <div key={`x${x}`} className="guide v" style={{ left: pct(x, W) }} />)}
              {guides.ys.map((y) => <div key={`y${y}`} className="guide h" style={{ top: pct(y, H) }} />)}

              {sel && selBox && (
                <div
                  className={`lbox${sel.locked ? " locked" : ""}${tool === "crop" ? " cropping" : ""}`}
                  style={{
                    left: pct(W / 2 + selBox.l, W),
                    top: pct(H / 2 + selBox.t, H),
                    width: pct(selBox.w, W),
                    height: pct(selBox.h, H),
                  }}
                >
                  {!sel.locked && tool === "move" &&
                    ([[-1, -1], [1, -1], [-1, 1], [1, 1]] as const).map(([hx, hy]) => (
                      <i key={`${hx}${hy}`} className="hnd" data-handle={`${hx},${hy}`} style={{ left: hx < 0 ? 0 : "100%", top: hy < 0 ? 0 : "100%", cursor: hx === hy ? "nwse-resize" : "nesw-resize" }} />
                    ))}
                  {!sel.locked && tool === "crop" &&
                    ([
                      ["tl", "0%", "0%", "nwse-resize"],
                      ["t", "50%", "0%", "ns-resize"],
                      ["tr", "100%", "0%", "nesw-resize"],
                      ["r", "100%", "50%", "ew-resize"],
                      ["br", "100%", "100%", "nwse-resize"],
                      ["b", "50%", "100%", "ns-resize"],
                      ["bl", "0%", "100%", "nesw-resize"],
                      ["l", "0%", "50%", "ew-resize"],
                    ] as const).map(([edges, left, top, cursor]) => (
                      <i key={edges} className="hnd crop-hnd" data-crop={edges} style={{ left, top, cursor }} />
                    ))}
                  <span className="lbox-tag mono">
                    {sel.name} · {Math.round(sel.x)},{Math.round(sel.y)} · {sel.scale.toFixed(0)}%{cropped ? " · ✂ cropped" : ""}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="stage-tag mono">
                {canvasRes ? `Canvas ${W}×${H} (${aspectTag(W, H)})` : mon ? monitorLabel(mon) : "Display"}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="canvas-toolbar card">
            <div className="seg">
              {(["fit", 50, 100] as const).map((z) => (
                <button key={z} className={zoom === z ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setZoom(z)}>{z === "fit" ? "Fit" : `${z}%`}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <select
              className="input"
              value={ref}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const v = e.target.value as RefKey;
                if (v === "custom" && !customShot) customInput.current?.click();
                setRef(v);
              }}
              title="Reference backdrop"
            >
              {REF_OPTIONS.map((o) => <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>{o.label}</option>)}
            </select>
            {ref === "custom" && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn small outline"
                onClick={() => customInput.current?.click()}
                title="Upload or change custom in-game screenshot"
              >
                &#x1F4F7; {customShot ? "Change Screenshot" : "Upload Screenshot"}
              </button>
            )}
            <select className="input mon" value={p.monitor} onChange={(e) => p.setMonitor(Number(e.target.value))} title="Target monitor">
              {monitors.map((m) => <option key={m.index} value={m.index}>{monitorLabel(m)}</option>)}
            </select>
            <div className="res-ctl" title="Authoring canvas resolution — pushed overlays auto-scale to your real monitor">
              <button type="button" className="btn small outline num-btn" onClick={() => cycleRes(-1)} aria-label="Previous resolution">◀</button>
              <select
                className="input res-sel"
                value={resKey(canvasRes)}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  if (e.target.value === "auto") return p.setCanvasRes(null);
                  const [w, h] = e.target.value.split("x").map(Number);
                  p.setCanvasRes({ w, h });
                }}
              >
                <option value="auto">Match Monitor{mon ? ` (${mon.width}×${mon.height})` : ""}</option>
                {canvasRes && !ALL_RES.some((r) => r.w === canvasRes.w && r.h === canvasRes.h) && (
                  <option value={resKey(canvasRes)}>Custom {canvasRes.w}×{canvasRes.h}</option>
                )}
                {RES_GROUPS.map((g) => (
                  <optgroup key={g.label} label={g.label}>
                    {g.items.map((r) => <option key={`${r.w}x${r.h}`} value={`${r.w}x${r.h}`}>{r.label} — {r.w}×{r.h}</option>)}
                  </optgroup>
                ))}
              </select>
              <button type="button" className="btn small outline num-btn" onClick={() => cycleRes(1)} aria-label="Next resolution">▶</button>
              <span className="tag mono res-tag">{aspectTag(W, H)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="spacer" />
            {p.live && <span className="mono ok small live-flag">&#x25CF; LIVE SYNC</span>}
            {p.live && <button className="btn outline small" onClick={() => p.setLive(false)}>Stop</button>}
            <button className="btn primary" disabled={!layers.some((l) => l.visible)} onClick={p.push}>
              {p.live ? "Re-push Composite" : "Push Composite to Live Overlay"}
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="canvas-toolbar tools-bar card">
            <div className="seg" title="Tool (press C to toggle crop)">
              <button className={tool === "move" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setTool("move")}>✥ Move</button>
              <button className={tool === "crop" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setTool("crop")}>✂ Crop</button>
            </div>
            <div className="tool-group" title="Align the selected layer to the canvas">
              {([["l", "⇤", "Align left"], ["cx", "↔", "Center horizontally"], ["r", "⇥", "Align right"], ["t", "⤒", "Align top"], ["cy", "↕", "Center vertically"], ["b", "⤓", "Align bottom"]] as const).map(([k, g, t]) => (
                <button key={k} className="btn small outline num-btn" disabled={!sel || sel.locked} title={t} onClick={() => align(k)}>{g}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="tool-group">
              <button className={`btn small ${selFx?.flipX ? "primary" : "outline"}`} disabled={!sel || sel.locked} title="Flip horizontally" onClick={() => sel && updateFx(sel.id, { flipX: !selFx!.flipX })}>⇋ Flip X</button>
              <button className={`btn small ${selFx?.flipY ? "primary" : "outline"}`} disabled={!sel || sel.locked} title="Flip vertically" onClick={() => sel && updateFx(sel.id, { flipY: !selFx!.flipY })}>⇅ Flip Y</button>
            </div>
            <div className="tool-group" title="Chop the selected layer into two independent pieces (non-destructive)">
              <span className="mono small muted">Chop @</span>
              <input
                className="input chop-pct"
                type="number"
                min={5}
                max={95}
                value={chopPct}
                onChange={(e) => setChopPct(Number(e.target.value) || 50)}
              />
              <span className="mono small muted">%</span>
              <button className="btn small outline" disabled={!sel || sel.locked} onClick={() => chop("v")}>┃ Vertical</button>
              <button className="btn small outline" disabled={!sel || sel.locked} onClick={() => chop("h")}>━ Horizontal</button>
            </div>
            <div className="spacer" />
            <button className={`btn small ${soloId ? "primary" : "outline"}`} disabled={!sel && !soloId} title="Show only the selected layer in the editor" onClick={() => setSoloId(soloId ? null : selectedId)}>
              {soloId ? "◉ Solo On" : "○ Solo"}
            </button>
            <button className={`btn small ${snapOn ? "primary" : "outline"}`} title="Smart guides: snap to canvas and other layers (hold Alt to bypass)" onClick={() => setSnapOn(!snapOn)}>
              {snapOn ? "✓ Smart Snap" : "Snap Off"}
            </button>
            <button className={`btn small ${grid10 ? "primary" : "outline"}`} title="Move in 10px steps (or hold Shift)" onClick={() => setGrid10(!grid10)}>
              {grid10 ? "✓ 10px Grid" : "10px Grid"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="muted small tips">
            Drag to move · corner handles scale · <b>C</b> crop tool · arrows nudge 1px (<b>Shift</b> 10px) · <b>Ctrl+D</b> duplicate · <b>Del</b> delete · hold <b>Alt</b> to bypass snapping · drop Vault items onto the canvas.
          </div>
        </section>

        <LayerPanel
          vault={vault}
          layers={layers}
          setLayers={setLayers}
          selectedId={selectedId}
          select={setSelectedId}
          update={update}
          updateFx={updateFx}
          duplicate={duplicate}
          remove={remove}
          roleHotkeys={p.settings.layerHotkeys}
        />
      </div>

      {saveOpen && (
        <SavePresetModal
          vault={vault}
          layers={layers}
          active={activePreset}
          refW={W}
          refH={H}
          onClose={() => setSaveOpen(false)}
          onSaved={(v, id) => {
            p.setVault(v);
            p.setActivePresetId(id);
            setSaveOpen(false);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {exportOpen && (
        <ExportPresetModal
          layers={layers}
          active={activePreset}
          vault={vault}
          refW={W}
          refH={H}
          onClose={() => setExportOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      {importOpen && (
        <ImportPresetModal
          vault={vault}
          onImport={(importedLayers, meta) => {
            setLayers(() => importedLayers);
            p.setActivePresetId(null);
            setSelectedId(importedLayers[0]?.id ?? null);
            if (meta.refW && meta.refH) p.setCanvasRes({ w: meta.refW, h: meta.refH });
          }}
          onClose={() => setImportOpen(false)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function PresetBar(p: Props & { onSave: () => void; onExport: () => void; onImport: () => void; clear: () => void }) {
  const active = p.vault.presets.find((x) => x.id === p.activePresetId) ?? null;
  return (
    <div className="presetbar card bracket">
      <span className="mono small muted">LOADOUT</span>
      <select
        className="input preset-sel"
        value={p.activePresetId ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const pr = p.vault.presets.find((x) => x.id === e.target.value);
          if (pr) p.loadPreset(pr, false);
          else p.setActivePresetId(null);
        }}
      >
        <option value="">— Unsaved stack —</option>
        {p.vault.presets.map((x) => <option key={x.id} value={x.id}>{x.name}{x.inVault ? "  ▣ STACK" : ""}</option>)}
      </select>
      {active?.inVault && <span className="stack-badge mono">STACK</span>}
      <div className="spacer" />
      <button className="btn outline small" onClick={p.onImport} title="Import a preset share code or .irispack file">📥 Import Preset</button>
      <button className="btn outline small" onClick={p.onExport} disabled={!p.layers.length} title="Export this loadout as a share code or .irispack file">📤 Share Preset</button>
      <button className="btn outline small" onClick={p.clear} disabled={!p.layers.length}>Clear Stack</button>
      {active && (
        <>
          <button
            className="btn outline small"
            onClick={async () => p.setVault(await api.savePreset({ ...active, inVault: !active.inVault }))}
            title="Show this loadout in the Vault tab with a STACK badge"
          >
            {active.inVault ? "Unpin from Vault" : "Pin to Vault"}
          </button>
          <button
            className="btn outline small danger-o"
            onClick={async () => {
              p.setVault(await api.deletePreset(active.id));
              p.setActivePresetId(null);
            }}
          >
            Delete
          </button>
        </>
      )}
      <button className="btn primary small" disabled={!p.layers.length} onClick={p.onSave}>Save Layer Preset</button>
    </div>
  );
}

function SavePresetModal({ vault, layers, active, refW, refH, onClose, onSaved }: {
  vault: VaultData;
  layers: Layer[];
  active: CompositePreset | null;
  refW: number;
  refH: number;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: (v: VaultData, id: string) => void;
}) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(active?.name ?? "");
  const [inVault, setInVault] = useState(active?.inVault ?? true);
  const [asNew, setAsNew] = useState(false);
  const overwrite = !!active && !asNew;

  async function save() {
    const first = vault.items.find((i) => i.id === layers[0]?.vaultItemId);
    const preset: CompositePreset = {
      id: overwrite ? active!.id : uid(),
      name: name.trim(),
      game: first?.game ?? null,
      layers: withZ(layers),
      refW,
      refH,
      inVault,
      createdAt: overwrite ? active!.createdAt : Date.now(),
    };
    onSaved(await api.savePreset(preset), preset.id);
  }

  return (
    <Modal title="Save Layer Preset" onClose={onClose} width={460}>
      <label className="label">Loadout name</label>
      <input className="input full" autoFocus value={name} placeholder='e.g. "Valo Viper Map + HUD"' onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && name.trim() && save()} />
      <label className="check"><input type="checkbox" checked={inVault} onChange={(e) => setInVault(e.target.checked)} /> Show in Vault with a <span className="stack-badge mono">STACK</span> badge</label>
      {active && <label className="check"><input type="checkbox" checked={asNew} onChange={(e) => setAsNew(e.target.checked)} /> Save as a new preset (keep "{active.name}" unchanged)</label>}
      <p className="muted small">Saves {layers.length} layers: references, z-order, position, scale, opacity, hue, visibility and locks.</p>
      <div className="modal-foot">
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
        <button className="btn primary" disabled={!name.trim()} onClick={save}>{overwrite ? "Update Preset" : "Save Preset"}</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ExportPresetModal({
  layers,
  active,
  vault,
  refW,
  refH,
  onClose,
}: {
  layers: Layer[];
  active: CompositePreset | null;
  vault: VaultData;
  refW: number;
  refH: number;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const items = useMemo(() => new Map(vault.items.map((i) => [i.id, i])), [vault.items]);

  const presetData = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      irisVersion: "1.0",
      type: "preset",
      name: active?.name || "Custom Loadout",
      game: active?.game || items.get(layers[0]?.vaultItemId)?.game || "Other",
      refW,
      refH,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      layers: layers.map((l) => ({
        name: l.name,
        role: l.role,
        zIndex: l.zIndex,
        visible: l.visible,
        locked: l.locked,
        x: l.x,
        y: l.y,
        scale: l.scale,
        opacity: l.opacity,
        hue: l.hue,
        fx: fxOf(l),
        vaultItemId: l.vaultItemId,
        assetFile: items.get(l.vaultItemId)?.file,
        assetName: items.get(l.vaultItemId)?.name,
        game: items.get(l.vaultItemId)?.game,
      })),
    };
  }, [layers, active, items, refW, refH]);

  const jsonString = useMemo(() => JSON.stringify(presetData, null, 2), [presetData]);
  const shareCode = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return `IRIS-PRESET:${btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(presetData))))}`;
    } catch {
      return `IRIS-PRESET:${btoa(JSON.stringify(presetData))}`;
    }
  }, [presetData]);

  const copyCode = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareCode);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };

  const downloadFile = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: "application/json" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    const safeName = (presetData.name || "loadout").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "_");
    a.download = `${safeName}.irispack`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <Modal title="Share / Export Preset" onClose={onClose} width={520}>
      <p className="muted small">
        Share your exact multi-element HUD layout with friends, Discord communities, or Venus Visuals.
      </p>
      <label className="label">Preset Share Code (One-Click Copy)</label>
      <textarea
        className="share-code-box"
        readOnly
        value={shareCode}
        onClick={(e) => (e.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).select()}
      />
      <div className="share-actions-row">
        <button className="btn primary grow" onClick={copyCode}>
          {copied ? "✓ Copied to Clipboard!" : "Copy Share Code"}
        </button>
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={downloadFile} title="Download as .irispack file">
          Download .irispack File
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="modal-foot">
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={onClose}>Close</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ImportPresetModal({
  vault,
  onImport,
  onClose,
}: {
  vault: VaultData;
  onImport: (presetLayers: Layer[], presetMeta: { name: string; game: string | null; refW: number; refH: number }) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleProcess = (rawText: string) => {
    setError("");
    let parsed: any = null;
    let trimmed = rawText.trim();
    if (trimmed.startsWith("IRIS-PRESET:")) {
      trimmed = trimmed.slice("IRIS-PRESET:".length).trim();
      try {
        const decoded = decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(trimmed)));
        parsed = JSON.parse(decoded);
      } catch {
        try {
          parsed = JSON.parse(atob(trimmed));
        } catch {
          setError("Invalid IRIS-PRESET share code format.");
          return;
        }
      }
    } else {
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed);
      } catch {
        setError("Invalid JSON or share code format.");
        return;
      }
    }

    if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.layers)) {
      setError("Parsed preset does not contain a valid layers array.");
      return;
    }

    const items = vault.items;
    const reconstructed: Layer[] = parsed.layers.map((l: any, idx: number) => {
      let matched = items.find((i) => i.id === l.vaultItemId);
      if (!matched && l.assetFile) matched = items.find((i) => i.file === l.assetFile);
      if (!matched && l.assetName) matched = items.find((i) => i.name.toLowerCase() === l.assetName.toLowerCase());
      if (!matched) matched = items[0];

      return {
        id: uid(),
        vaultItemId: matched?.id ?? (items[0]?.id || "item_1"),
        name: l.name || matched?.name || `Layer ${idx + 1}`,
        role: l.role || "custom",
        zIndex: typeof l.zIndex === "number" ? l.zIndex : idx,
        visible: l.visible !== false,
        locked: !!l.locked,
        x: Number(l.x) || 0,
        y: Number(l.y) || 0,
        scale: Number(l.scale) || 100,
        opacity: typeof l.opacity === "number" ? l.opacity : 100,
        hue: Number(l.hue) || 0,
        fx: fxOf({ fx: l.fx }),
      };
    });

    onImport(reconstructed, {
      name: parsed.name || "Imported Loadout",
      game: parsed.game || null,
```
