```tsx
      refW: parsed.refW || 1920,
      refH: parsed.refH || 1080,
    });
    onClose();
  };

  const handleFileUpload = (file?: File) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      if (typeof reader.result === "string") handleProcess(reader.result);
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  return (
    <Modal title="Import Layer Preset" onClose={onClose} width={500}>
      <p className="muted small">
        Paste a preset share code (`IRIS-PRESET:...`) or upload a `.irispack` / `.iris` JSON file.
      </p>
      <label className="label">Paste Share Code or JSON</label>
      <textarea
        className="share-code-box"
        placeholder="IRIS-PRESET:eyJ2ZXJzaW9uIjoxLC..."
        value={code}
        onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
      />
      {error && <div className="error">{error}</div>}
      <div className="share-actions-row">
        <label className="btn outline grow" style={{ textAlign: "center", cursor: "pointer" }}>
          📁 Browse .irispack File
          <input
            type="file"
            accept=".irispack,.iris,.json"
            hidden
            onChange={(e) => handleFileUpload(e.target.files?.[0])}
          />
        </label>
        <button className="btn primary grow" disabled={!code.trim()} onClick={() => handleProcess(code)}>
          Load into Workspace
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="modal-foot">
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`src/components/SceneView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { assetUrl } from "../api";
import { fxOf, type Layer, type VaultData } from "../types";

/**
 * Pure-CSS (percent-positioned) stack renderer used for the Layering canvas and
 * STACK thumbnails. The live overlay window uses a single canvas instead.
 */
export function SceneImages({
  layers,
  vault,
  refW,
  refH,
}: {
  layers: Layer[];
  vault: VaultData;
  refW: number;
  refH: number;
}) {
  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map(vault.items.map((i) => [i.id, i])), [vault.items]);
  return (
    <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, isolation: "isolate", pointerEvents: "none" }}>
      {[...layers].reverse().map((l) => {
        const it = byId.get(l.vaultItemId);
        if (!l.visible || !it) return null;
        const fx = fxOf(l);
        const w = (it.width * l.scale) / 100;
        const h = (it.height * l.scale) / 100;
        const cropped = fx.cropT || fx.cropR || fx.cropB || fx.cropL;
        return (
          <img
            key={l.id}
            src={assetUrl(vault.assetsDir, it.file)}
            draggable={false}
            alt=""
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              left: `${((refW / 2 + l.x - w / 2) / refW) * 100}%`,
              top: `${((refH / 2 + l.y - h / 2) / refH) * 100}%`,
              width: `${(w / refW) * 100}%`,
              height: `${(h / refH) * 100}%`,
              opacity: l.opacity / 100,
              filter: l.hue ? `hue-rotate(${l.hue}deg)` : undefined,
              clipPath: cropped ? `inset(${fx.cropT}% ${fx.cropR}% ${fx.cropB}% ${fx.cropL}%)` : undefined,
              transform: fx.flipX || fx.flipY ? `scale(${fx.flipX ? -1 : 1}, ${fx.flipY ? -1 : 1})` : undefined,
              mixBlendMode: fx.blend && fx.blend !== "normal" ? (fx.blend as React.CSSProperties["mixBlendMode"]) : undefined,
              pointerEvents: "none",
            }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
export function StackThumb({ layers, vault, refW, refH }: { layers: Layer[]; vault: VaultData; refW: number; refH: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="stack-thumb" style={{ aspectRatio: `${refW} / ${refH}` }}>
      <SceneImages layers={layers} vault={vault} refW={refW} refH={refH} />
    </div>
  );
}

export type RefKey = "grid" | "custom";

export const REF_OPTIONS: { id: RefKey; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "grid", label: "Dark Grid" },
  { id: "custom", label: "Custom In-Game Screenshot" },
];
```

```tsx
function TargetIcon() {

function keyName(e: KeyboardEvent): string | null {
  const k = e.key;
  if (/^F\d{1,2}$/.test(k)) return k;
  if (/^[a-z0-9]$/i.test(k)) return k.toUpperCase();
  if (k.startsWith("Arrow")) return k.slice(5);
  if (k === " ") return "Space";
  if (["Home", "End", "PageUp", "PageDown", "Insert"].includes(k)) return k;
  return null;
}

function HotkeyInput({ value, onChange }: { value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void }) {
  const [rec, setRec] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!rec) return;
    const h = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (["Control", "Shift", "Alt", "Meta"].includes(e.key)) return;
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setRec(false);
        return;
      }
      if (e.key === "Backspace" || e.key === "Delete") {
        onChange("");
        setRec(false);
        return;
      }
      const k = keyName(e);
      if (!k) return;
      const mods = [e.ctrlKey && "Ctrl", e.altKey && "Alt", e.shiftKey && "Shift", e.metaKey && "Super"].filter(Boolean);
      onChange([...mods, k].join("+"));
      setRec(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", h, true);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", h, true);
  }, [rec, onChange]);

  if (rec) {
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="keycap-rec"
        onClick={() => setRec(false)}
        title="Press your key combination or press Esc to cancel"
      >
        <span className="keycap-rec-dot" />
        Press key combo... (Esc cancel)
      </button>
    );
  }

  if (!value) {
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="keycap-unbound"
        onClick={() => setRec(true)}
        title="Click to record keybind"
      >
        <span className="keycap-plus">+</span> Click to bind
      </button>
    );
  }

  const keys = value.split("+");

  return (
    <div className="keycap-group" onClick={() => setRec(true)} title="Click to re-record keybind">
      <div className="keycaps-wrapper">
        {keys.map((k, idx) => (
          <span key={idx} className="keycap-item">
            <kbd className="keycap">{k}</kbd>
            {idx < keys.length - 1 && <span className="keycap-plus">+</span>}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="keycap-clear-btn"
        title="Clear keybind"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          onChange("");
        }}
      >
        ×
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function SettingsRow({
  title,

        {desc && <div className="settings-row-desc">{desc}</div>}
      </div>
      <div className="settings-row-ctrl">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function SettingsView({
  settings: s,
  save,
  currentForegroundExe,
  vault,
  usageStats,
  activeOverlayItemId,
  isTelemetryPaused,
  onToggleTelemetry,
  onResetTelemetry,
  onEquipItem,
  onEquipPreset,
  updateStatus = { status: "idle" },
  onCheckUpdate,
  onDownloadUpdate,
  onRestartUpdate,
}: {
  settings: Settings;
  save: (p: Partial<Settings>) => void;
  currentForegroundExe?: string | null;
  vault?: VaultData | null;
  usageStats?: UsageStats;
  activeOverlayItemId?: string | null;
  isTelemetryPaused?: boolean;
  onToggleTelemetry?: () => void;
  onResetTelemetry?: () => void;
  onEquipItem?: (item: VaultItem) => void;
  onEquipPreset?: (preset: CompositePreset) => void;
  updateStatus?: UpdateStatus;
  onCheckUpdate?: () => void;
  onDownloadUpdate?: () => void;
  onRestartUpdate?: () => void;
}) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<SettingsTab>("display");
  const [exe, setExe] = useState(s.targetExe);
  const [needsRestart, setNeedsRestart] = useState(false);
  const [confirmReset, setConfirmReset] = useState(false);

  const isGameActive = !!currentForegroundExe && KNOWN_GAMES.some((g) =>
    g.exes.some((e) => e.toLowerCase() === currentForegroundExe.toLowerCase())
  );

  const favOverlay = computeFavoriteOverlay(vault, usageStats, activeOverlayItemId);
  const effectiveUploads = Math.max(usageStats?.totalUploads ?? 0, vault?.items.length ?? 0);
  const vaultItemCount = vault?.items.length ?? 0;
  const estimatedSizeMB = vault && vault.items.length > 0
    ? (vault.items.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.width * it.height * 0.4) / (1024 * 1024), 0)).toFixed(1)
    : "0";

  const handleResetClick = () => {
    if (!confirmReset) {
      setConfirmReset(true);
      setTimeout(() => setConfirmReset(false), 3500);
    } else {
      setConfirmReset(false);
      onResetTelemetry?.();
    }
  };

  const gameBreakdown = useMemo(() => {
    if (!vault) return { segments: [] as Array<{ game: string; percent: number; detail: string; color: string }>, modeLabel: "no data" };

    const times = usageStats?.overlayTime ?? {};
    const gameTimes: Record<string, number> = {};
    let totalTime = 0;

    for (const it of vault.items) {
      const t = times[it.id] ?? 0;
      const g = it.game || "Other";
      if (t > 0) {
        gameTimes[g] = (gameTimes[g] || 0) + t;
        totalTime += t;
      }
    }

    for (const p of vault.presets) {
      const t = times[`preset:${p.id}`] ?? times[p.id] ?? 0;
      const g = p.game || "Other";
      if (t > 0) {
        gameTimes[g] = (gameTimes[g] || 0) + t;
        totalTime += t;
      }
    }

    const GAME_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
      Overwatch: "#38bdf8",
      Valorant: "#f43f5e",
      "Marvel Rivals": "#a855f7",
      "Call of Duty": "#eab308",
      Fortnite: "#10b981",
      "Apex Legends": "#fb923c",
      "Dead by Daylight": "#64748b",
      Other: "#06b6d4",
    };
    const PALETTE = ["#38bdf8", "#a855f7", "#f43f5e", "#10b981", "#eab308", "#fb923c", "#06b6d4", "#64748b"];

    if (totalTime > 0) {
      const sorted = Object.entries(gameTimes).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
      const segments = sorted.map(([game, sec], i) => ({
        game,
        percent: Math.max(1, Math.round((sec / totalTime) * 100)),
        detail: formatDuration(sec),
        color: GAME_COLORS[game] || PALETTE[i % PALETTE.length],
      }));
      return { segments, modeLabel: "by active time" };
    }

    const gameCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
    let totalCount = 0;
    for (const it of vault.items) {
      const g = it.game || "Other";
      gameCounts[g] = (gameCounts[g] || 0) + 1;
      totalCount++;
    }
    for (const p of vault.presets) {
      const g = p.game || "Other";
      gameCounts[g] = (gameCounts[g] || 0) + 1;
      totalCount++;
    }

    if (totalCount > 0) {
      const sorted = Object.entries(gameCounts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
      const segments = sorted.map(([game, count], i) => ({
        game,
        percent: Math.max(1, Math.round((count / totalCount) * 100)),
        detail: `${count} ${count === 1 ? "overlay" : "overlays"}`,
        color: GAME_COLORS[game] || PALETTE[i % PALETTE.length],
      }));
      return { segments, modeLabel: "by library distribution" };
    }

    return { segments: [], modeLabel: "no activity" };
  }, [vault, usageStats]);

  const SETTINGS_NAV: Array<{ id: SettingsTab; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }> = [
    { id: "display", label: "Display & Targeting", icon: <TargetIcon /> },
    { id: "profiles", label: "Game Profiles", icon: <GamepadIcon /> },
    { id: "keybinds", label: "Keybinds", icon: <KeyboardIcon /> },
    { id: "telemetry", label: "Telemetry", icon: <ActivityIcon /> },
    { id: "system", label: "System & Updates", icon: <SystemIcon /> },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="page settings">
      <div className="settings-layout">
        {/* Left Navigation Sidebar */}
        <aside className="settings-sidebar">
          {SETTINGS_NAV.map((item) => (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              type="button"
              className={`settings-nav-btn ${activeTab === item.id ? "active" : ""}`}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab(item.id)}
            >
              <span className="settings-nav-icon">{item.icon}</span>
              <span className="settings-nav-label">{item.label}</span>
            </button>
          ))}

          {/* Pinned Bottom Version & Repo Badge */}
          <div className="settings-sidebar-footer">
            <div className="settings-version-card">
              <div className="settings-version-top">
                <span className="settings-version-title mono">Iris v0.1.0</span>
                <span
                  className={`settings-update-dot ${
                    updateStatus.status === "available"
                      ? "has-update"
                      : updateStatus.status === "upToDate"
                      ? "ok"
                      : ""
                  }`}
                  title={
                    updateStatus.status === "available"
                      ? "Update available!"
                      : updateStatus.status === "upToDate"
                      ? "Up to date"
                      : "Standing by"
                  }
                />
              </div>
              <a
                href="https://github.com/allnighter51/Iris"
                target="_blank"
                rel="noreferrer"
                className="settings-repo-link mono"
              >
                github.com/allnighter51/Iris
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>

        {/* Right Content Pane */}
        <main className="settings-content">
          {/* TAB 1: Display & Targeting */}
          {activeTab === "display" && (
            <div className="settings-panel">
              <div className="settings-panel-head">
                <h3 className="settings-panel-title">Overlay Display &amp; Game Targeting</h3>
                <p className="settings-panel-desc">
                  Configure whether the overlay stays always-on-top or automatically attaches to running game processes.
                </p>
              </div>

              <SettingsRow
                title="Target Mode"
                desc="Always-on-top desktop overlay, or auto-attach (show overlay only while target game is focused)."
              >
                <div className="seg">
                  <button
                    className={s.targetMode === "topmost" ? "active" : ""}
                    onClick={() => save({ targetMode: "topmost" })}
                  >
                    Always on Top
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className={s.targetMode === "game" ? "active" : ""}
                    onClick={() => save({ targetMode: "game" })}
                  >
                    Auto-Attach to Game
                  </button>
                </div>
              </SettingsRow>

              {s.targetMode === "game" && (
                <SettingsRow title="Game Executable" desc="Overlay appears only while this process is running.">
                  <div className="exe">
                    <select
                      className="input"
                      value={GAME_EXES.some((g) => g.exe === s.targetExe) ? s.targetExe : ""}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        if (e.target.value) {
                          setExe(e.target.value);
                          save({ targetExe: e.target.value });
                        }
                      }}
                    >
                      <option value="">Custom...</option>
                      {GAME_EXES.map((g) => (
                        <option key={g.exe} value={g.exe}>
                          {g.label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                    <input
                      className="input mono"
                      value={exe}
                      onChange={(e) => setExe(e.target.value)}
                      onBlur={() => exe.trim() && exe !== s.targetExe && save({ targetExe: exe.trim() })}
                      placeholder="Game.exe"
                    />
                  </div>
                </SettingsRow>
              )}

              <SettingsRow
                title="Hide from Capture (OBS / Stream Mode)"
                desc="ON: Only you see the overlay on your screen (WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE). OFF: Overlay also appears in OBS Game and Display capture."
              >
                <Toggle checked={s.hideFromCapture} onChange={(v) => save({ hideFromCapture: v })} />
              </SettingsRow>

              <SettingsRow
                title="Automatic Game Detection"
                desc="Detects foreground game processes (Overwatch, Valorant, Marvel Rivals) and auto-switches to matching loadouts."
              >
                <Toggle checked={s.autoGameSwitch ?? true} onChange={(v) => save({ autoGameSwitch: v })} />
              </SettingsRow>

              <SettingsRow
                title="Auto-Hide when Tabbed Out"
                desc="Conceals overlay when tabbed into Discord, Chrome, or Desktop; reappears instantly when you focus back on the game."
              >
                <Toggle checked={s.autoHideUnfocused ?? false} onChange={(v) => save({ autoHideUnfocused: v })} />
              </SettingsRow>

              <SettingsRow
                title="Focused Foreground Process"
                desc="Live hardware readout of the currently focused Windows application."
              >
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                  <span className="mono small" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)" }}>
                    {currentForegroundExe || "desktop / idle"}
                  </span>
                  <span className={`tag mono ${isGameActive ? "ok" : ""}`}>
                    {isGameActive ? "● GAME DETECTED" : "○ BACKGROUND"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </SettingsRow>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: Game Profiles */}
          {activeTab === "profiles" && (
            <div className="settings-panel">
              <div className="settings-panel-head">
                <h3 className="settings-panel-title">Game-to-HUD Profile Assignments</h3>
                <p className="settings-panel-desc">
                  Automatically load a specific Vault Overlay or Layering Loadout when a supported game is launched.
                </p>
              </div>

              {vault ? (
                <div className="profile-list-container">
                  {KNOWN_GAMES.map((g) => {
                    const profiles = s.gameProfiles ?? {};
                    const currentVal = profiles[g.name] || "";
                    const isCustom = !!currentVal;

                    return (
                      <div key={g.name} className="profile-row">
                        <div className="profile-row-left">
                          <span className="profile-game-accent" style={{ backgroundColor: g.color }} />
                          <div className="profile-game-info">
                            <div className="profile-game-title-row">
                              <span className="profile-game-name">{g.label}</span>
                              {isCustom && <span className="profile-custom-pill">Custom</span>}
                            </div>
                            <span className="profile-game-exe mono" title={g.exes.join(", ")}>
                              {g.exes[0]}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="profile-row-right">
                          <select
                            className={`input profile-select ${isCustom ? "custom-selected" : "auto-selected"}`}
                            value={currentVal}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              const next = { ...profiles };
                              if (e.target.value) next[g.name] = e.target.value;
                              else delete next[g.name];
                              save({ gameProfiles: next });
                            }}
                          >
                            <option value="">Automatic (First Favorite / Matching HUD)</option>
                            {vault.presets.filter((p) => p.game === g.name || !p.game).length > 0 && (
                              <optgroup label="Layer Presets">
                                {vault.presets.map((p) => (
                                  <option key={`preset:${p.id}`} value={`preset:${p.id}`}>
                                    ★ Loadout: {p.name}
                                  </option>
                                ))}
                              </optgroup>
                            )}
                            <optgroup label="Vault Overlays">
                              {vault.items
                                .filter((it) => it.game === g.name || it.game === "Other")
                                .map((it) => (
                                  <option key={`item:${it.id}`} value={`item:${it.id}`}>
                                    {it.favorite ? "★ " : ""}{it.name}
                                  </option>
                                ))}
                            </optgroup>
                          </select>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="muted small" style={{ padding: 16 }}>
                  Loading Vault profiles...
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: Keybinds */}
          {activeTab === "keybinds" && (
            <>
              <div className="settings-panel">
                <div className="settings-panel-head">
                  <h3 className="settings-panel-title">Global Hotkeys</h3>
                  <p className="settings-panel-desc">
                    System-wide shortcuts active across Windows while in-game, streaming, or on desktop.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <SettingsRow
                  title="Toggle Overlay Visibility"
                  desc="Show or hide the active overlay on screen."
                >
                  <HotkeyInput value={s.hotkeyToggle} onChange={(v) => save({ hotkeyToggle: v })} />
                </SettingsRow>
                <SettingsRow
                  title="Next Overlay"
                  desc="Cycles overlays in the current Vault folder filter."
                >
                  <HotkeyInput value={s.hotkeyNext} onChange={(v) => save({ hotkeyNext: v })} />
                </SettingsRow>
                <SettingsRow
                  title="Previous Overlay"
                  desc="Cycles backward through overlays in the current filter."
                >
                  <HotkeyInput value={s.hotkeyPrev} onChange={(v) => save({ hotkeyPrev: v })} />
                </SettingsRow>
              </div>

              <div className="settings-panel">
                <div className="settings-panel-head">
                  <h3 className="settings-panel-title">Layer Role Hotkeys</h3>
                  <p className="settings-panel-desc">
                    Toggle individual modular HUD parts in the composite stack (e.g. Map Overlay on Ctrl+Alt+M).
                  </p>
                </div>
                {ROLES.map((r) => (
                  <SettingsRow key={r.id} title={r.label} desc={`Toggles all ${r.badge} layers.`}>
                    <HotkeyInput
                      value={s.layerHotkeys?.[r.id] ?? ""}
                      onChange={(v) => {
                        const next = { ...(s.layerHotkeys ?? {}) };
                        if (v) next[r.id] = v;
                        else delete next[r.id];
                        save({ layerHotkeys: next });
                      }}
                    />
                  </SettingsRow>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* TAB 4: Telemetry */}
          {activeTab === "telemetry" && (
            <div className="settings-panel telemetry-section">
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 12 }}>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="settings-panel-title" style={{ margin: 0 }}>Iris Usage &amp; Activity</h3>
                  <p className="settings-panel-desc" style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>
                    Live overlay and session telemetry tracking your Iris usage across games and presets.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="telemetry-header-controls">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className={`telemetry-status-pill ${!isTelemetryPaused ? "live" : "paused"}`}
                    onClick={() => onToggleTelemetry?.()}
                    title={!isTelemetryPaused ? "Click to pause telemetry tracking" : "Click to resume telemetry tracking"}
                  >
                    <span className="telemetry-pulse-dot" />
                    {!isTelemetryPaused ? "TRACKING ACTIVE" : "TRACKING PAUSED"}
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className={`btn small ${confirmReset ? "danger" : "outline"} telemetry-reset-btn`}
                    onClick={handleResetClick}
                    title={confirmReset ? "Click again to confirm resetting all usage telemetry" : "Reset session and usage counters"}
                  >
                    {confirmReset ? "Confirm Reset?" : "↺ Reset"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Bento 2-Column Grid */}
              <div className="telemetry-bento-grid">
                {/* Left Column: Top Equipped Overlay Hero Card */}
                <div className="bento-cell telemetry-hero-card">
                  <div className="telemetry-hero-head">
                    <span className="bento-label">TOP EQUIPPED OVERLAY</span>
                    {favOverlay.isStarred && (
                      <span
                        className="telemetry-badge star"
                        style={{
                          color: "#fbbf24",
                          background: "rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.12)",
                          border: "1px solid rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.35)",
                        }}
                      >
                        ★ FAVORITE
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {favOverlay.item && vault ? (
                    <div className="telemetry-hero-thumb">
                      <div className="telemetry-hero-glow" />
                      <img
                        src={assetUrl(
                          favOverlay.item.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir,
                          favOverlay.item.thumb ?? favOverlay.item.file
                        )}
                        alt={favOverlay.item.name}
                        className="telemetry-hero-img"
                      />
                      <div className="telemetry-equip-overlay">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="btn small primary telemetry-equip-btn"
                          onClick={() => onEquipItem?.(favOverlay.item!)}
                        >
                          ⚡ Equip Now
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : favOverlay.preset && vault ? (
                    <div className="telemetry-hero-thumb">
                      <div className="telemetry-hero-glow" />
                      <div style={{ width: "85%", height: "85%", position: "relative", zIndex: 1 }}>
                        <StackThumb
                          layers={favOverlay.preset.layers}
                          vault={vault}
                          refW={favOverlay.preset.refW}
                          refH={favOverlay.preset.refH}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div className="telemetry-equip-overlay">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="btn small primary telemetry-equip-btn"
                          onClick={() => onEquipPreset?.(favOverlay.preset!)}
                        >
                          ⚡ Equip Now
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="telemetry-hero-thumb empty">
                      <div className="muted small" style={{ textAlign: "center", padding: 16 }}>
                        No overlays in Vault
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="telemetry-hero-details">
                    <div className="telemetry-hero-name-row">
                      <div className="telemetry-hero-name" title={favOverlay.name}>
                        {favOverlay.name}
                      </div>
                      {favOverlay.isRecentFallback || favOverlay.timeSeconds === 0 ? (
                        <span className="telemetry-badge recent">Recently Added</span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="telemetry-badge active-time">⏱ {favOverlay.timeFormatted}</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="telemetry-hero-meta">
                      {favOverlay.sub && (
                        <span className={`tag mono ${gameClass(favOverlay.sub)}`}>{favOverlay.sub}</span>
                      )}
                      {favOverlay.tag && (
                        <span className="tag mono">
                          {ELEMENT_TAGS.find((t) => t.id === favOverlay.tag)?.label || favOverlay.tag}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Right Column: Unified Telemetry Strip + Game Distribution Bar */}
                <div className="telemetry-right-col">
                  {/* Top Row: 3 Compact Stat Cells */}
                  <div className="telemetry-stat-strip">
                    <div className="bento-cell telemetry-stat-cell">
                      <div className="bento-label">VAULT LIBRARY</div>
                      <div className="bento-value">{effectiveUploads}</div>
                      <div className="bento-sub">
                        {vaultItemCount} {vaultItemCount === 1 ? "overlay" : "overlays"}
                        {estimatedSizeMB && estimatedSizeMB !== "0" ? ` · ~${estimatedSizeMB} MB` : ""}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="bento-cell telemetry-stat-cell">
                      <div className="bento-label">OVERLAYS SWAPPED</div>
                      <div className="bento-value">{usageStats?.totalSwaps ?? 0}</div>
                      <div className="bento-sub">Switches &amp; cyclers</div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="bento-cell telemetry-stat-cell">
                      <div className="bento-label">ACTIVE HUD TIME</div>
                      <div className="bento-value">{formatDuration(usageStats?.totalAppTimeSeconds ?? 0)}</div>
                      <div className="bento-sub">Lifetime Iris usage</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Bottom Row: Game Activity Breakdown Bar */}
                  <div className="bento-cell telemetry-breakdown-card">
                    <div className="telemetry-breakdown-head">
                      <span className="bento-label">GAME ACTIVITY BREAKDOWN</span>
                      <span className="mono muted small">{gameBreakdown.modeLabel}</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="telemetry-progress-track">
                      {gameBreakdown.segments.map((seg) => (
                        <div
                          key={seg.game}
                          className="telemetry-segment"
                          style={{ width: `${seg.percent}%`, backgroundColor: seg.color }}
                          title={`${seg.game}: ${seg.percent}% (${seg.detail})`}
                        />
                      ))}
                      {gameBreakdown.segments.length === 0 && (
                        <div className="telemetry-segment-empty" style={{ width: "100%" }} />
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="telemetry-legend">
                      {gameBreakdown.segments.map((seg) => (
                        <div key={seg.game} className="telemetry-legend-item">
                          <span className="telemetry-dot" style={{ backgroundColor: seg.color }} />
                          <span className="telemetry-legend-name">{seg.game}</span>
                          <span className="telemetry-legend-pct mono">{seg.percent}%</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                      {gameBreakdown.segments.length === 0 && (
                        <span className="muted small">No game activity recorded yet</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 5: System & Updates */}
          {activeTab === "system" && (
            <>
              <div className="settings-panel">
                <div className="settings-panel-head">
                  <h3 className="settings-panel-title">System &amp; Storage</h3>
                  <p className="settings-panel-desc">
                    Configure boot behavior, background minimization, GPU compositing, and asset paths.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <SettingsRow
                  title="Start minimized to tray on Windows boot"
                  desc="Registers Iris in HKCU\...\Run with --minimized."
                >
                  <Toggle checked={s.startWithWindows} onChange={(v) => save({ startWithWindows: v })} />
                </SettingsRow>
                <SettingsRow
                  title="Close button minimizes to tray"
                  desc="Keeps the overlay and hotkeys alive. Use the tray icon to quit."
                >
                  <Toggle checked={s.closeToTray} onChange={(v) => save({ closeToTray: v })} />
                </SettingsRow>
                <SettingsRow
                  title="Hardware Acceleration"
                  desc="GPU-composited rendering. Requires application restart."
                >
                  <Toggle
                    checked={s.hardwareAcceleration}
                    onChange={(v) => {
                      setNeedsRestart(true);
                      save({ hardwareAcceleration: v });
                    }}
                  />
                </SettingsRow>
                <SettingsRow
                  title="Low-Resource Mode"
                  desc="Disables color filters on the overlay and slows background process polling."
                >
                  <Toggle checked={s.lowResource} onChange={(v) => save({ lowResource: v })} />
                </SettingsRow>
                {needsRestart && (
                  <div className="note mono" style={{ margin: "10px 0" }}>
                    &#x21BB; Restart Iris to apply the hardware acceleration change.
                  </div>
                )}
                <SettingsRow
                  title="Local Vault Directory"
                  desc="PNG assets, thumbnails, vault_manifest.json and settings.json on your computer."
                >
                  <button className="btn outline" onClick={() => api.openVaultDir()}>
                    Open Vault Directory
                  </button>
                </SettingsRow>
              </div>

              <div className="settings-panel">
                <div className="settings-panel-head">
                  <h3 className="settings-panel-title">Updates &amp; Release Maintenance</h3>
                  <p className="settings-panel-desc">
                    Official release channel, auto-updater telemetry, and GitHub release binaries.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <SettingsRow
                  title="App Version &amp; Repository"
                  desc="Iris Desktop Overlay utility releases are distributed via GitHub Releases."
                >
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
                    <span className="mono bold" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)" }}>
                      Iris v0.1.0
                    </span>
                    <a
                      href="https://github.com/allnighter51/Iris"
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="tag mono"
                      style={{ textDecoration: "none", cursor: "pointer" }}
                    >
                      github.com/allnighter51/Iris
                    </a>
                  </div>
                </SettingsRow>

                <SettingsRow
                  title="Check for updates on app startup"
                  desc="Silently checks GitHub Releases in the background. Does not interrupt or steal focus while playing or streaming."
                >
                  <Toggle
                    checked={s.checkUpdatesOnStartup ?? true}
                    onChange={(v) => save({ checkUpdatesOnStartup: v })}
                  />
                </SettingsRow>

                <SettingsRow title="Update Status" desc="Current status of the official Tauri auto-updater.">
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                    {updateStatus.status === "idle" && <span className="tag mono">○ STANDBY</span>}
                    {updateStatus.status === "checking" && (
                      <span className="tag mono" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)" }}>
                        ⟳ CHECKING GITHUB RELEASES...
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {updateStatus.status === "upToDate" && (
                      <span className="tag mono ok">● UP TO DATE (v{updateStatus.currentVersion})</span>
                    )}
                    {updateStatus.status === "available" && (
                      <span className="tag mono" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)", borderColor: "var(--cyan)" }}>
                        ★ UPDATE AVAILABLE: v{updateStatus.version}
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {updateStatus.status === "downloading" && (
                      <span className="tag mono" style={{ color: "var(--purple)", borderColor: "var(--purple)" }}>
                        ↓ DOWNLOADING {updateStatus.progress.toFixed(0)}%
                        {updateStatus.total
                          ? ` (${(updateStatus.downloaded / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} / ${(
                              updateStatus.total /
                              (1024 * 1024)
                            ).toFixed(1)} MB)`
                          : ""}
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {updateStatus.status === "downloaded" && (
                      <span className="tag mono ok" style={{ borderColor: "var(--emerald)" }}>
                        ✓ UPDATE READY TO INSTALL (v{updateStatus.version})
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {updateStatus.status === "error" && (
                      <span
                        className="tag mono"
                        style={{ color: "#f87171", borderColor: "#f87171" }}
                        title={updateStatus.message}
                      >
                        ⚠{" "}
                        {updateStatus.message.length > 50
                          ? `${updateStatus.message.slice(0, 50)}...`
                          : updateStatus.message}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </SettingsRow>

                {updateStatus.status === "available" && updateStatus.body && (
                  <div
                    style={{
                      margin: "12px 0",
                      padding: "12px",
                      background: "rgba(10, 11, 18, 0.8)",
                      borderRadius: 6,
                      border: "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
                    }}
                  >
                    <div className="mono small bold" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                      Release Notes (v{updateStatus.version})
                    </div>
                    <div className="small muted" style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", maxHeight: 120, overflowY: "auto" }}>
                      {updateStatus.body}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, marginTop: 14 }}>
                  <button
                    className="btn outline"
                    disabled={updateStatus.status === "checking" || updateStatus.status === "downloading"}
                    onClick={onCheckUpdate}
                  >
                    {updateStatus.status === "checking" ? "Checking..." : "Check for Updates"}
                  </button>

                  {updateStatus.status === "available" && (
                    <button className="btn primary" onClick={onDownloadUpdate}>
                      Download in Background
                    </button>
                  )}

                  {updateStatus.status === "downloaded" && (
                    <button className="btn primary" onClick={onRestartUpdate}>
                      Restart &amp; Update Now
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

`src/components/TopBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Tab } from "../types";

const TABS: { id: Tab; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "vault", label: "Vault" },
  { id: "display", label: "Display" },
  { id: "layering", label: "Layering" },
  { id: "settings", label: "Settings" },
];

export function TopBar({
  tab,
  setTab,
  active,
  waiting,
  onForceShow,
  hasUpdate,
}: {
  tab: Tab;
  setTab: (t: Tab) => void;
  active: boolean;
  waiting?: boolean;
  onForceShow?: () => void;
  hasUpdate?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <header className="topbar">
      <div className="brand">
        <span className="brand-mark" />
        <span className="brand-name">Iris</span>
        <span className="brand-script">by venus visuals</span>
      </div>
      <div
        className={`status-pill${active ? " live" : waiting ? " waiting" : ""}`}
        style={waiting && onForceShow ? { cursor: "pointer" } : undefined}
        onClick={waiting && onForceShow ? onForceShow : undefined}
        title={waiting && onForceShow ? "Click to force show overlay on screen now" : undefined}
      >
        {active ? "● OVERLAY ACTIVE" : waiting ? "○ WAITING FOR GAME (CLICK TO FORCE SHOW)" : "○ STANDBY"}
      </div>
      <nav className="tabs">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            className={tab === t.id ? "active" : ""}
            onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            style={{ position: "relative" }}
          >
            {t.label}
            {t.id === "settings" && hasUpdate && (
              <span
                style={{
                  position: "absolute",
                  top: 7,
                  right: 8,
                  width: 7,
                  height: 7,
                  borderRadius: "50%",
                  background: "var(--cyan)",
                  boxShadow: "0 0 8px var(--cyan)",
                }}
                title="Update available"
              />
            )}
          </button>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </header>
  );
}

```

`src/components/UploadModal.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "../api";
import { ELEMENT_TAGS, GAMES, type VaultData } from "../types";
import { analyzeImage, makeThumbnail, resolutionTag } from "../util";
import { Modal } from "./ui";

interface Analysis {
  bmp: ImageBitmap;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  hasAlpha: boolean;
}

interface GameRule {
  game: string;
  aliases: RegExp[];
  heroes: RegExp[];
  stripPrefixes: RegExp[];
}

const GAME_RULES: GameRule[] = [
  {
    game: "Valorant",
    aliases: [/\b(valorant|val)\b/i],
    heroes: [
      /\b(jett|reyna|clove|omen|sage|phoenix|sova|viper|cypher|brimstone|breach|raze|killjoy|skye|yoru|astra|kayo|kay\/o|chamber|neon|fade|harbor|gekko|deadlock|iso|vyse)\b/i,
    ],
    stripPrefixes: [/^(valorant|val)[\s_-]+/i],
  },
  {
    game: "Overwatch",
    aliases: [/\b(overwatch|ow|ow2)\b/i],
    heroes: [
      /\b(kiriko|mercy|genji|dva|d\.va|ana|tracer|widowmaker|reinhardt|zenyatta|hanzo|reaper|cassidy|ashe|soldier|lucio|moira|sigma|sojourn|ramattra|illari|mauga|venture|juno)\b/i,
    ],
    stripPrefixes: [/^(overwatch|ow2|ow)[\s_-]+/i],
  },
  {
    game: "Call of Duty",
    aliases: [/\b(call\s*of\s*duty|warzone|cod|mw|mw2|mw3|bo6|black\s*ops)\b/i],
    heroes: [],
    stripPrefixes: [/^(call[\s_-]*of[\s_-]*duty|warzone|cod|mw[123]?|bo6|black[\s_-]*ops)[\s_-]+/i],
  },
  {
    game: "Fortnite",
    aliases: [/\b(fortnite|fn)\b/i],
    heroes: [],
    stripPrefixes: [/^(fortnite|fn)[\s_-]+/i],
  },
  {
    game: "Dead by Daylight",
    aliases: [/\b(dead\s*by\s*daylight|dbd)\b/i],
    heroes: [],
    stripPrefixes: [/^(dead[\s_-]*by[\s_-]*daylight|dbd)[\s_-]+/i],
  },
  {
    game: "Apex Legends",
    aliases: [/\b(apex\s*legends|apex)\b/i],
    heroes: [],
    stripPrefixes: [/^(apex[\s_-]*legends|apex)[\s_-]+/i],
  },
  {
    game: "Marvel Rivals",
    aliases: [/\b(marvel\s*rivals|rivals|marvel)\b/i],
    heroes: [],
    stripPrefixes: [/^(marvel[\s_-]*rivals|rivals|marvel)[\s_-]+/i],
  },
];

const TAG_RULES: Array<{ id: string; patterns: RegExp[] }> = [
  { id: "reticle", patterns: [/\b(reticle|crosshair|xhair|sight|dot)\b/i] },
  { id: "map", patterns: [/\b(minimap|map|radar)\b/i] },
  { id: "health", patterns: [/\b(healthbar|health|hp|shield|status)\b/i] },
  { id: "ult", patterns: [/\b(ultimate|ult|ability|icon)\b/i] },
  { id: "hud", patterns: [/\b(hud|overlay|full|frame|pack)\b/i] },
];

export function detectGameFromFilename(name: string): string | null {
  const norm = name.replace(/[-_.]+/g, " ");
  // Pass 1: explicit game aliases/titles
  for (const rule of GAME_RULES) {
    for (const re of rule.aliases) {
      if (re.test(norm) || re.test(name)) {
        return rule.game;
      }
    }
  }
  // Pass 2: hero / agent keywords
  for (const rule of GAME_RULES) {
    for (const re of rule.heroes) {
      if (re.test(norm) || re.test(name)) {
        return rule.game;
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function detectTagFromFilename(name: string): string | null {
  const norm = name.replace(/[-_.]+/g, " ");
  for (const rule of TAG_RULES) {
    for (const re of rule.patterns) {
      if (re.test(norm) || re.test(name)) {
        return rule.id;
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function gcd(a: number, b: number): number {
  return b === 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b);
}

export function formatAspectRatio(w: number, h: number): string {
  if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return "—";
  if (w === h) return "1:1";

  const ratio = w / h;
  if (Math.abs(ratio - 16 / 9) < 0.02) return "16:9";
  if (
    Math.abs(ratio - 21 / 9) < 0.05 ||
    Math.abs(ratio - 43 / 18) < 0.05 ||
    (w === 3440 && h === 1440) ||
    (w === 2560 && h === 1080)
  ) {
    return "21:9";
  }
  if (Math.abs(ratio - 32 / 9) < 0.05 || (w === 5120 && h === 1440)) return "32:9";
  if (Math.abs(ratio - 16 / 10) < 0.02) return "16:10";
  if (Math.abs(ratio - 4 / 3) < 0.02) return "4:3";
  if (Math.abs(ratio - 9 / 16) < 0.02) return "9:16";

  const g = gcd(w, h);
  const rw = w / g;
  const rh = h / g;
  if (rw <= 32 && rh <= 32) {
    return `${rw}:${rh}`;
  }
  return `${ratio.toFixed(2)}:1`;
}

export function detectTagFromDimensions(w: number, h: number): string | null {
  if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return null;

  const ratio = w / h;
  const is16by9 = Math.abs(ratio - 16 / 9) < 0.04;
  const is21by9 =
    Math.abs(ratio - 21 / 9) < 0.06 ||
    Math.abs(ratio - 43 / 18) < 0.06 ||
    (w === 3440 && h === 1440) ||
    (w === 2560 && h === 1080);

  // Full HUD: standard 16:9 or 21:9 monitor aspect ratios and >= 1280px wide
  if (w >= 1280 && (is16by9 || is21by9)) {
    return "hud";
  }

  // Health Bar: wide horizontal aspect ratio (width >= 2.5x height) and height <= 300px
  if (w >= 2.5 * h && h <= 300) {
    return "health";
  }

  // Square (1:1) aspect ratio heuristics:
  const isSquare = Math.abs(w - h) <= Math.max(2, Math.round(w * 0.02));
  if (isSquare) {
    const maxDim = Math.max(w, h);
    if (maxDim <= 96) return "reticle";
    if (maxDim >= 97 && maxDim <= 256) return "ult";
    if (maxDim >= 257 && maxDim <= 600) return "map";
  }

  return null;
}

export function cleanOverlayName(rawName: string, detectedGame?: string | null): string {
  let cleaned = rawName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "");

  if (detectedGame) {
    const rule = GAME_RULES.find((r) => r.game === detectedGame);
    if (rule) {
      for (const prefixRe of rule.stripPrefixes) {
        if (prefixRe.test(cleaned)) {
          const stripped = cleaned.replace(prefixRe, "").trim();
          if (stripped.length > 0) {
            cleaned = stripped;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    for (const rule of GAME_RULES) {
      for (const prefixRe of rule.stripPrefixes) {
        if (prefixRe.test(cleaned)) {
          const stripped = cleaned.replace(prefixRe, "").trim();
          if (stripped.length > 0) {
            cleaned = stripped;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  cleaned = cleaned.replace(/[_-]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();

  if (!cleaned) {
    cleaned = rawName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "").replace(/[_-]+/g, " ").trim();
  }

  const acronyms = new Set(["HUD", "HP", "UI", "FPS", "COD", "DBD", "OW", "FN", "MW"]);
  const words = cleaned.split(/\s+/).map((w) => {
    const up = w.toUpperCase();
    if (acronyms.has(up)) return up;
    if (up === "DVA") return "D.Va";
    return w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase();
  });

  return words.join(" ");
}

export function UploadModal({
  vault,
  defaultGame,
  defaultFolder,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
}: {
  vault: VaultData;
  defaultGame: string;
  defaultFolder: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: (v: VaultData) => void;
}) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState("");
  const [info, setInfo] = useState<Analysis | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [game, setGame] = useState(defaultGame);
  const [folder, setFolder] = useState(defaultFolder);
  const [tag, setTag] = useState("");
  const [favorite, setFavorite] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState(false);

  // Auto-detection indicators
  const [autoDetectedName, setAutoDetectedName] = useState(false);
  const [autoDetectedGame, setAutoDetectedGame] = useState(false);
  const [autoDetectedTag, setAutoDetectedTag] = useState(false);

  // Session interaction tracking
  const userEditedNameRef = useRef(false);
  const userClickedGameRef = useRef(false);
  const userClickedTagRef = useRef(false);

  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(preview);
    };
  }, [preview]);

  async function pick(f: File | undefined) {
    if (!f) return;
    setErr("");
    setInfo(null);
    if (!/\.(png|webp)$/i.test(f.name) && !/^image\/(png|webp)$/.test(f.type)) {
      setErr("Only .png and .webp files are accepted.");
      return;
    }
    try {
      const a = await analyzeImage(f);
      setFile(f);
      setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(f));
      setInfo(a);

      const rawName = f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "");
      const detectedGame = detectGameFromFilename(rawName);
      const tagFromFilename = detectTagFromFilename(rawName);
      const tagFromDims = !tagFromFilename ? detectTagFromDimensions(a.width, a.height) : null;
      const detectedTag = tagFromFilename || tagFromDims;

      if (!userEditedNameRef.current) {
        setName(cleanOverlayName(rawName, detectedGame));
        setAutoDetectedName(true);
      }

      if (!userClickedGameRef.current && detectedGame) {
        setGame(detectedGame);
        setAutoDetectedGame(true);
      }

      if (!userClickedTagRef.current && detectedTag) {
        setTag(detectedTag);
        setAutoDetectedTag(true);
      }

      if (!a.hasAlpha) {
        setErr("Warning: No transparency detected. This image has no alpha channel and may cover the screen.");
      }
    } catch {
      setErr("Could not decode this image.");
    }
  }

  const canSave = !!file && !!game && !!name.trim() && !busy;

  async function save() {
    if (!file || !info) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const item = await api.importAsset(bytes, { name, game, folder, tag, width: info.width, height: info.height });
      if (favorite) {
        await api.updateItem(item.id, { favorite: true });
      }
      onSaved(await api.saveThumbnail(item.id, await makeThumbnail(info.bmp)));
    } catch (e) {
      setErr(String(e));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Modal title="Upload New Design" onClose={onClose} width={620}>
      <input ref={input} type="file" accept=".png,.webp,image/png,image/webp" hidden onChange={(e) => pick(e.target.files?.[0])} />

      {preview && info ? (
        <div className="upload-preview-card card bracket checker">
          <div className="upload-preview-screen">
            <img src={preview} alt="Overlay Preview" className="upload-preview-img" />
          </div>
          <div className="upload-preview-overlay-bar">
            <span className="mono tag">
              {info.width}×{info.height} • {formatAspectRatio(info.width, info.height)}
            </span>
            {info.height >= 1080 && (
              <span className="mono tag">{resolutionTag(info.width, info.height)}</span>
            )}
            <span className={`mono tag ${info.hasAlpha ? "ok" : "warn"}`}>
              {info.hasAlpha ? "Alpha OK" : "No Alpha"}
            </span>
            <div className="spacer" />
            <button type="button" className="btn small outline" onClick={() => input.current?.click()}>
              Change File
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div
          className={`dropzone${drag ? " drag" : ""}`}
          onClick={() => input.current?.click()}
          onDragOver={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setDrag(true);
          }}
          onDragLeave={() => setDrag(false)}
          onDrop={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setDrag(false);
            pick(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
          }}
        >
          <div className="dz-icon">^</div>
          <div>Drag &amp; drop a transparent <b>.png</b> or <b>.webp</b></div>
          <div className="muted small">or click to browse from your computer</div>
        </div>
      )}

      <label className="label">
        Overlay name
        {autoDetectedName && <span className="auto-detect-badge">✦ Auto-detected</span>}
      </label>
      <input
        className="input full"
        value={name}
        placeholder="Defaults to filename"
        onChange={(e) => {
          userEditedNameRef.current = true;
          setAutoDetectedName(false);
          setName(e.target.value);
        }}
      />

      <label className="label">
        Game category <span className="req">required</span>
        {autoDetectedGame && <span className="auto-detect-badge">✦ Auto-detected</span>}
      </label>
      <div className="pill-bar wrap">
        {GAMES.map((g) => (
          <button
            key={g}
            type="button"
            className={`pill${game === g ? " active" : ""}${autoDetectedGame && game === g ? " auto-detected" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => {
              userClickedGameRef.current = true;
              setAutoDetectedGame(false);
              setGame(g);
            }}
          >
            {g}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <label className="label">
        Element type <span className="muted small">optional · used by Layering drawer</span>
        {autoDetectedTag && <span className="auto-detect-badge">✦ Auto-detected</span>}
      </label>
      <div className="pill-bar wrap">
        {ELEMENT_TAGS.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            type="button"
            className={`pill${tag === t.id ? " active" : ""}${autoDetectedTag && tag === t.id ? " auto-detected" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => {
              userClickedTagRef.current = true;
              setAutoDetectedTag(false);
              setTag(tag === t.id ? "" : t.id);
            }}
          >
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <label className="label">Custom folder <span className="muted small">optional</span></label>
      <select className="input full" value={folder} onChange={(e) => setFolder(e.target.value)}>
        <option value="">— None —</option>
        {vault.folders.map((f) => <option key={f}>{f}</option>)}
      </select>

      <label className="check">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={favorite} onChange={(e) => setFavorite(e.target.checked)} />
        <span>Add to Favorites *</span>
      </label>

      {err && <div className="error">{err}</div>}
      <div className="modal-foot">
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
        <button className="btn primary" disabled={!canSave} onClick={save}>
          {busy ? "Saving..." : "Save to Vault"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

```

`src/components/Vault.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api, assetUrl } from "../api";
import { ELEMENT_TAGS, FILTER_GAMES, GAMES, type CompositePreset, type VaultData, type VaultItem } from "../types";
import { StackThumb } from "./SceneView";
import { filterItems, gameClass, resolutionTag, type SortKey } from "../util";
import { Modal } from "./ui";
import { UploadModal } from "./UploadModal";

export type VaultViewMode = "grid" | "cards" | "carousel" | "list";

type Dialog =
  | { kind: "upload" }
  | { kind: "folder" }
  | { kind: "rename"; item: VaultItem }
  | { kind: "move"; item: VaultItem }
  | { kind: "delete"; item: VaultItem }
  | { kind: "delPreset"; preset: CompositePreset }
  | { kind: "manageGames" }
  | null;

interface Menu {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  item?: VaultItem;
  preset?: CompositePreset;
}

interface Props {
  vault: VaultData;
  setVault: (v: VaultData) => void;
  filter: string;
  setFilter: (f: string) => void;
  activeId: string | null;
  onLoad: (item: VaultItem) => void;
  onSendToStack: (item: VaultItem) => void;
  onLoadPreset: (p: CompositePreset) => void;
  onUpload?: () => void;
}

export function Vault({ vault, setVault, filter, setFilter, activeId, onLoad, onSendToStack, onLoadPreset, onUpload }: Props) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortKey>("new");
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<Dialog>(null);
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState<Menu | null>(null);

  // View Mode: grid | cards | carousel | list
  const [viewMode, setViewModeState] = useState<VaultViewMode>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem("iris_vault_view_mode") as VaultViewMode;
      if (saved && ["grid", "cards", "carousel", "list"].includes(saved)) return saved;
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
    return "grid";
  });

  const setViewMode = (mode: VaultViewMode) => {
    setViewModeState(mode);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("iris_vault_view_mode", mode);
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  };

  // Hidden game tabs
  const [hiddenGames, setHiddenGamesState] = useState<string[]>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem("iris_hidden_games");
      return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as string[]) : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  });

  const saveHiddenGames = (next: string[]) => {
    setHiddenGamesState(next);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("iris_hidden_games", JSON.stringify(next));
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
    if (next.includes(filter)) {
      setFilter("All");
    }
  };

  const hideGame = (game: string) => {
    if (!hiddenGames.includes(game)) {
      saveHiddenGames([...hiddenGames, game]);
    }
  };

  const toggleGameVisibility = (game: string) => {
    if (hiddenGames.includes(game)) {
      saveHiddenGames(hiddenGames.filter((g) => g !== game));
    } else {
      saveHiddenGames([...hiddenGames, game]);
    }
  };

  // Carousel active index
  const [carouselIdx, setCarouselIdx] = useState(0);
  const filmstripRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menu) return;
    const close = () => setMenu(null);
    const esc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && close();
    window.addEventListener("click", close);
    window.addEventListener("blur", close);
    window.addEventListener("keydown", esc);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("click", close);
      window.removeEventListener("blur", close);
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", esc);
    };
  }, [menu]);

  // STACK presets pinned to the Vault
  const presets = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return vault.presets
      .filter((p) => p.inVault)
      .filter((p) => {
        if (filter === "Favorites") return false; // STACK presets aren't individually favorited
        if (filter === "All") return true;
        return p.game === filter;
      })
      .filter((p) => !q || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      .sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
  }, [vault.presets, filter, query]);

  const items = useMemo(
    () => filterItems(vault.items, filter, query, sort),
    [vault.items, filter, query, sort],
  );

  const visibleGames = FILTER_GAMES.filter((g) => !hiddenGames.includes(g));
  const pills = ["All", "Favorites", ...visibleGames, ...vault.folders];

  // a deleted folder or hidden game must not leave the filter dangling
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pills.includes(filter)) setFilter("All");
  });

  const toggleFavorite = async (item: VaultItem) => {
    setVault(await api.updateItem(item.id, { favorite: !item.favorite }));
  };

  // Combined entries for carousel mode
  const allEntries = useMemo(() => {
    const list: ({ type: "preset"; data: CompositePreset } | { type: "item"; data: VaultItem })[] = [];
    presets.forEach((p) => list.push({ type: "preset", data: p }));
    items.forEach((it) => list.push({ type: "item", data: it }));
    return list;
  }, [presets, items]);

  const safeCarouselIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(carouselIdx, Math.max(0, allEntries.length - 1)));
  const activeEntry = allEntries[safeCarouselIdx] ?? null;

  // Keyboard navigation for Carousel
  useEffect(() => {
    if (viewMode !== "carousel" || allEntries.length <= 1) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return;
      if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setCarouselIdx((i) => (i <= 0 ? allEntries.length - 1 : i - 1));
      } else if (e.key === "ArrowRight") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setCarouselIdx((i) => (i >= allEntries.length - 1 ? 0 : i + 1));
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [viewMode, allEntries.length]);

  return (
    <div className="page vault" onClick={() => setSelected(null)}>
      <div className="vault-actions" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "upload" })}>
          + Upload New Design
        </button>
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "folder" })}>
          + New Folder
        </button>
        <div className="spacer" />

        {/* View Mode Toggle: Grid | Cards | Carousel | List (Left of Search box) */}
        <div className="seg vault-view-mode-seg" title="Vault View Mode">
          <button
            type="button"
            className={viewMode === "grid" ? "active" : ""}
            onClick={() => setViewMode("grid")}
            title="Compact Grid View"
          >
            ⊞ Grid
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={viewMode === "cards" ? "active" : ""}
            onClick={() => setViewMode("cards")}
            title="Detailed Card View"
          >
            🗎 Cards
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={viewMode === "carousel" ? "active" : ""}
            onClick={() => setViewMode("carousel")}
            title="Cinematic Carousel View"
          >
            ❖ Carousel
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={viewMode === "list" ? "active" : ""}
            onClick={() => setViewMode("list")}
            title="Compact List View"
          >
            ☰ List
          </button>
        </div>

        <input
          className="input search"
          placeholder="Search overlays..."
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
        />
        <select className="input" value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as SortKey)}>
          <option value="new">Newest First</option>
          <option value="name">Name</option>
          <option value="game">Game</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="pill-bar" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        {pills.map((p) => {
          const custom = vault.folders.includes(p);
          const isFav = p === "Favorites";
          const isGame = (FILTER_GAMES as readonly string[]).includes(p);
          return (
            <button
              key={p}
              className={`pill${filter === p ? " active" : ""}${custom ? " folder" : ""}${isFav ? " fav-pill" : ""}`}
              onClick={() => setFilter(p)}
            >
              {isFav ? (
                <span>&#x2605; Favorites</span>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {custom && <span className="folder-glyph">&#x25B8;</span>}
                  {p}
                  {custom && filter === p && (
                    <span
                      className="pill-x"
                      title="Delete folder (items are kept)"
                      onClick={async (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        setVault(await api.deleteFolder(p));
                        setFilter("All");
                      }}
                    >
                      &times;
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {isGame && (
                    <span
                      className="pill-hide-btn"
                      title={`Hide "${p}" tab`}
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        hideGame(p);
                      }}
                    >
                      &times;
                    </span>
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}

        {/* Manage Tabs Option */}
        <button
          type="button"
          className="pill manage-tabs-pill"
          onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "manageGames" })}
          title="Customize visible game categories"
        >
          ⚙ Manage Tabs{hiddenGames.length > 0 ? ` (${hiddenGames.length} hidden)` : ""}
        </button>
      </div>

      {items.length === 0 && presets.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty card bracket">
          <div className="empty-title">
            {filter === "Favorites"
              ? "No favorite overlays yet"
              : vault.items.length
                ? "No overlays match"
                : "Your Vault is empty"}
          </div>
          <p>
            {filter === "Favorites"
              ? "Click the star icon on any overlay card to add it to your Favorites."
              : vault.items.length
                ? "Try another folder or search term."
                : "Upload a transparent PNG to get started."}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : viewMode === "grid" ? (
        /* 1. Classic Compact Grid View */
        <div className="grid">
          {presets.map((p) => {
            const isSel = selected === p.id;
            return (
              <article
                key={p.id}
                className={`card bracket vcard stackcard${isSel ? " selected" : ""}`}
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(p.id);
                }}
                onDoubleClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onLoadPreset(p);
                }}
                onContextMenu={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(p.id);
                  setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, preset: p });
                }}
              >
                <div className="thumb checker">
                  <StackThumb layers={p.layers} vault={vault} refW={p.refW} refH={p.refH} />
                  <div className="badges">
                    <span className="stack-badge mono">STACK</span>
                    {p.game && <span className={`tag mono ${gameClass(p.game)}`}>{p.game}</span>}
                  </div>
                  {isSel && (
                    <div className="quick" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} onDoubleClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                      <button onClick={() => onLoadPreset(p)}>Load Stack</button>
                      <button onClick={async () => setVault(await api.savePreset({ ...p, inVault: false }))}>Unpin</button>
                      <button className="danger" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "delPreset", preset: p })}>Delete</button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="meta">
                  <h4>{p.name}</h4>
                  <div className="meta-row">
                    <span className="chip folder">{p.layers.length} layers</span>
                    <span className="dims mono">{p.refW}x{p.refH}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
          {items.map((item) => {
            const isSel = selected === item.id;
            return (
              <article
                key={item.id}
                className={`card bracket vcard${isSel ? " selected" : ""}${activeId === item.id ? " live" : ""}`}
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(item.id);
                }}
                onDoubleClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onLoad(item);
                }}
                onContextMenu={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(item.id);
                  setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, item });
                }}
              >
                <div className="thumb checker">
                  <img
                    loading="lazy"
                    draggable={false}
                    src={assetUrl(item.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir, item.thumb ?? item.file)}
                    alt={item.name}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className={`fav-star-btn${item.favorite ? " active" : ""}`}
                    title={item.favorite ? "Remove from Favorites" : "Add to Favorites"}
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      toggleFavorite(item);
                    }}
                  >
                    {item.favorite ? "\u2605" : "\u2606"}
                  </button>
                  <div className="badges">
                    <span className="tag mono">{resolutionTag(item.width, item.height)}</span>
                    <span className={`tag mono ${gameClass(item.game)}`}>{item.game}</span>
                  </div>
                  {activeId === item.id && <span className="live-dot mono">&#x25CF; ACTIVE</span>}
                  {isSel && (
                    <div className="quick" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} onDoubleClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                      <button onClick={() => toggleFavorite(item)}>
                        {item.favorite ? "Unfavorite" : "Favorite"}
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "rename", item })}>Rename</button>
                      <button onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "move", item })}>Folder</button>
                      <button className="danger" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "delete", item })}>
                        Delete
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="meta">
                  <h4>{item.name}</h4>
                  <div className="meta-row">
                    <span className={`chip ${gameClass(item.game)}`}>{item.game}</span>
                    {item.tag && <span className="chip folder">{ELEMENT_TAGS.find((t) => t.id === item.tag)?.label ?? item.tag}</span>}
                    {item.folder && <span className="chip folder">&#x25B8; {item.folder}</span>}
                    <span className="dims mono">
                      {item.width}x{item.height}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : viewMode === "cards" ? (
        /* 2. Detailed Card View */
        <div className="vault-cards">
          {presets.map((p) => {
            const isSel = selected === p.id;
            return (
              <article
                key={p.id}
                className={`card bracket vshowcase-card${isSel ? " selected" : ""}`}
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(p.id);
                }}
                onDoubleClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onLoadPreset(p);
                }}
                onContextMenu={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(p.id);
                  setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, preset: p });
                }}
              >
                <div className="vshowcase-thumb checker">
                  <StackThumb layers={p.layers} vault={vault} refW={p.refW} refH={p.refH} />
                  <div className="badges">
                    <span className="stack-badge mono">STACK</span>
                    {p.game && <span className={`tag mono ${gameClass(p.game)}`}>{p.game}</span>}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="vshowcase-body">
                  <div className="vshowcase-head">
                    <h4>{p.name}</h4>
                  </div>
                  <div className="vshowcase-chips">
                    <span className="chip folder">{p.layers.length} layers</span>
                    {p.game && <span className={`chip ${gameClass(p.game)}`}>{p.game}</span>}
                    <span className="dims mono">{p.refW} × {p.refH}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="vshowcase-actions">
                    <button className="btn small primary" onClick={() => onLoadPreset(p)}>
                      Load Stack
                    </button>
                    <button
                      className="btn small outline"
                      onClick={async () => setVault(await api.savePreset({ ...p, inVault: false }))}
                    >
                      Unpin
                    </button>
                    <button
                      className="btn small outline icon-btn"
                      title="More options"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        setSelected(p.id);
                        setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, preset: p });
                      }}
                    >
                      ⋯
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
          {items.map((item) => {
            const isSel = selected === item.id;
            return (
              <article
                key={item.id}
                className={`card bracket vshowcase-card${isSel ? " selected" : ""}${activeId === item.id ? " live" : ""}`}
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(item.id);
                }}
                onDoubleClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onLoad(item);
                }}
                onContextMenu={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(item.id);
                  setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, item });
                }}
              >
                <div className="vshowcase-thumb checker">
                  <img
                    loading="lazy"
                    draggable={false}
                    src={assetUrl(item.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir, item.thumb ?? item.file)}
                    alt={item.name}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className={`fav-star-btn${item.favorite ? " active" : ""}`}
                    title={item.favorite ? "Remove from Favorites" : "Add to Favorites"}
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      toggleFavorite(item);
                    }}
                  >
                    {item.favorite ? "★" : "☆"}
                  </button>
                  <div className="badges">
                    <span className="tag mono">{resolutionTag(item.width, item.height)}</span>
                    <span className={`tag mono ${gameClass(item.game)}`}>{item.game}</span>
                  </div>
                  {activeId === item.id && <span className="live-dot mono">● ACTIVE</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="vshowcase-body">
                  <div className="vshowcase-head">
                    <h4>{item.name}</h4>
                  </div>
                  <div className="vshowcase-chips">
```
