```tsx
                    <span className={`chip ${gameClass(item.game)}`}>{item.game}</span>
                    {item.tag && <span className="chip folder">{ELEMENT_TAGS.find((t) => t.id === item.tag)?.label ?? item.tag}</span>}
                    {item.folder && <span className="chip folder">&#x25B8; {item.folder}</span>}
                    <span className="dims mono">{item.width} × {item.height}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="vshowcase-actions">
                    <button className="btn small primary" onClick={() => onLoad(item)}>
                      Equip / Display
                    </button>
                    <button className="btn small outline" onClick={() => onSendToStack(item)}>
                      + Add to Stack
                    </button>
                    <button
                      className="btn small outline icon-btn"
                      title="More actions"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        setSelected(item.id);
                        setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, item });
                      }}
                    >
                      ⋯
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : viewMode === "carousel" ? (
        /* 3. Cinematic Carousel / Hero Stage View */
        <div className="vault-carousel">
          {activeEntry && (
            <div className="carousel-stage-card card bracket">
              <div className="carousel-preview checker">
                {allEntries.length > 1 && (
                  <>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="carousel-nav-btn prev"
                      aria-label="Previous item"
                      onClick={() => setCarouselIdx((i) => (i <= 0 ? allEntries.length - 1 : i - 1))}
                    >
                      ◀
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="carousel-nav-btn next"
                      aria-label="Next item"
                      onClick={() => setCarouselIdx((i) => (i >= allEntries.length - 1 ? 0 : i + 1))}
                    >
                      ▶
                    </button>
                  </>
                )}

                {activeEntry.type === "item" ? (
                  <img
                    draggable={false}
                    src={assetUrl(activeEntry.data.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir, activeEntry.data.thumb ?? activeEntry.data.file)}
                    alt={activeEntry.data.name}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div style={{ width: "80%", height: "80%" }}>
                    <StackThumb
                      layers={activeEntry.data.layers}
                      vault={vault}
                      refW={activeEntry.data.refW}
                      refH={activeEntry.data.refH}
                    />
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="badges">
                  <span className="tag mono" style={{ color: "var(--cyan)", borderColor: "var(--cyan)" }}>
                    {safeCarouselIdx + 1} / {allEntries.length}
                  </span>
                  {activeEntry.type === "item" ? (
                    <>
                      <span className="tag mono">{resolutionTag(activeEntry.data.width, activeEntry.data.height)}</span>
                      <span className={`tag mono ${gameClass(activeEntry.data.game)}`}>{activeEntry.data.game}</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <span className="stack-badge mono">STACK</span>
                      {activeEntry.data.game && <span className={`tag mono ${gameClass(activeEntry.data.game)}`}>{activeEntry.data.game}</span>}
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>

                {activeEntry.type === "item" && activeId === activeEntry.data.id && (
                  <span className="live-dot mono">● ACTIVE ON SCREEN</span>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="carousel-stage-bar">
                <div>
                  <h3 style={{ margin: "0 0 6px", fontSize: 18, color: "var(--text)" }}>
                    {activeEntry.type === "item" && activeEntry.data.favorite ? "★ " : ""}
                    {activeEntry.data.name}
                  </h3>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                    <span className={`chip ${activeEntry.data.game ? gameClass(activeEntry.data.game) : "folder"}`}>
                      {activeEntry.data.game || "Custom"}
                    </span>
                    {activeEntry.type === "item" && activeEntry.data.tag && (
                      <span className="chip folder">{ELEMENT_TAGS.find((t) => t.id === activeEntry.data.tag)?.label ?? activeEntry.data.tag}</span>
                    )}
                    {activeEntry.type === "item" && activeEntry.data.folder && (
                      <span className="chip folder">▸ {activeEntry.data.folder}</span>
                    )}
                    <span className="dims mono">
                      {activeEntry.type === "item" ? `${activeEntry.data.width} × ${activeEntry.data.height}` : `${activeEntry.data.refW} × ${activeEntry.data.refH} (${activeEntry.data.layers.length} layers)`}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "center" }}>
                  {activeEntry.type === "item" ? (
                    <>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className={`btn small ${activeEntry.data.favorite ? "primary" : "outline"}`}
                        onClick={() => toggleFavorite(activeEntry.data)}
                      >
                        {activeEntry.data.favorite ? "★ Favorited" : "☆ Favorite"}
                      </button>
                      <button className="btn small primary" onClick={() => onLoad(activeEntry.data)}>
                        Equip to Screen
                      </button>
                      <button className="btn small outline" onClick={() => onSendToStack(activeEntry.data)}>
                        + Add to Stack
                      </button>
                      <button
                        className="btn small outline icon-btn"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          setSelected(activeEntry.data.id);
                          setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, item: activeEntry.data });
                        }}
                      >
                        ⋯
                      </button>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <button className="btn small primary" onClick={() => onLoadPreset(activeEntry.data)}>
                        Load Stack to Live
                      </button>
                      <button
                        className="btn small outline icon-btn"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          setSelected(activeEntry.data.id);
                          setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, preset: activeEntry.data });
                        }}
                      >
                        ⋯
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Filmstrip Carousel Reel */}
          <div className="carousel-reel-wrap" ref={filmstripRef}>
            {allEntries.map((entry, idx) => {
              const isActive = idx === safeCarouselIdx;
              return (
                <div
                  key={entry.type === "item" ? entry.data.id : entry.data.id}
                  className={`carousel-reel-thumb checker${isActive ? " active" : ""}`}
                  onClick={() => setCarouselIdx(idx)}
                  title={entry.data.name}
                >
                  {entry.type === "item" ? (
                    <img
                      draggable={false}
                      src={assetUrl(entry.data.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir, entry.data.thumb ?? entry.data.file)}
                      alt={entry.data.name}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <StackThumb
                      layers={entry.data.layers}
                      vault={vault}
                      refW={entry.data.refW}
                      refH={entry.data.refH}
                    />
                  )}
                  {entry.type === "item" && activeId === entry.data.id && (
                    <span className="live-dot mono" style={{ fontSize: 9 }}>●</span>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* 4. Dense Tabular List View */
        <div className="vault-list">
          {presets.map((p) => {
            const isSel = selected === p.id;
            return (
              <div
                key={p.id}
                className={`vault-list-row card bracket${isSel ? " selected" : ""}`}
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(p.id);
                }}
                onDoubleClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onLoadPreset(p);
                }}
                onContextMenu={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(p.id);
                  setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, preset: p });
                }}
              >
                <div className="vault-list-thumb checker">
                  <StackThumb layers={p.layers} vault={vault} refW={p.refW} refH={p.refH} />
                </div>
                <span className="stack-badge mono" style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>STACK</span>
                <span className="vault-list-name" title={p.name}>
                  {p.name}
                </span>
                {p.game && <span className={`chip ${gameClass(p.game)}`}>{p.game}</span>}
                <span className="chip folder">{p.layers.length} layers</span>
                <span className="dims mono">{p.refW}×{p.refH}</span>
                <button
                  className="btn small primary"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    onLoadPreset(p);
                  }}
                >
                  Load Stack
                </button>
                <button
                  className="btn small outline icon-btn"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    setSelected(p.id);
                    setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, preset: p });
                  }}
                >
                  ⋯
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {items.map((item) => {
            const isSel = selected === item.id;
            return (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className={`vault-list-row card bracket${isSel ? " selected" : ""}${activeId === item.id ? " live" : ""}`}
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(item.id);
                }}
                onDoubleClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onLoad(item);
                }}
                onContextMenu={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelected(item.id);
                  setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, item });
                }}
              >
                <div className="vault-list-thumb checker">
                  <img
                    loading="lazy"
                    draggable={false}
                    src={assetUrl(item.thumb ? vault.thumbsDir : vault.assetsDir, item.thumb ?? item.file)}
                    alt={item.name}
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={`fav-star-btn${item.favorite ? " active" : ""}`}
                  style={{ position: "static", transform: "none", fontSize: 16 }}
                  title={item.favorite ? "Remove from Favorites" : "Add to Favorites"}
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    toggleFavorite(item);
                  }}
                >
                  {item.favorite ? "★" : "☆"}
                </button>
                <span className="vault-list-name" title={item.name}>
                  {item.name}
                </span>
                <span className={`chip ${gameClass(item.game)}`}>{item.game}</span>
                {item.tag && <span className="chip folder">{ELEMENT_TAGS.find((t) => t.id === item.tag)?.label ?? item.tag}</span>}
                {item.folder && <span className="chip folder">&#x25B8; {item.folder}</span>}
                <span className="dims mono">{item.width}×{item.height}</span>
                {activeId === item.id && <span className="tag mono ok">● ACTIVE</span>}
                <button
                  className="btn small primary"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    onLoad(item);
                  }}
                >
                  Equip
                </button>
                <button
                  className="btn small outline"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    onSendToStack(item);
                  }}
                >
                  + Stack
                </button>
                <button
                  className="btn small outline icon-btn"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    setSelected(item.id);
                    setMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, item });
                  }}
                >
                  ⋯
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {menu && (
        <div
          className="ctxmenu card"
          style={{ left: Math.min(menu.x, window.innerWidth - 220), top: Math.min(menu.y, window.innerHeight - 220) }}
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        >
          {menu.item && (
            <>
              <button onClick={() => { onLoad(menu.item!); setMenu(null); }}>Load in Display</button>
              <button className="accent" onClick={() => { onSendToStack(menu.item!); setMenu(null); }}>Send to Layering Stack</button>
              <button onClick={async () => { await toggleFavorite(menu.item!); setMenu(null); }}>
                {menu.item!.favorite ? "\u2605 Remove from Favorites" : "\u2606 Add to Favorites"}
              </button>
              <hr />
              <button onClick={() => { setDialog({ kind: "rename", item: menu.item! }); setMenu(null); }}>Rename / Tag...</button>
              <button onClick={() => { setDialog({ kind: "move", item: menu.item! }); setMenu(null); }}>Change Folder...</button>
              <button className="danger" onClick={() => { setDialog({ kind: "delete", item: menu.item! }); setMenu(null); }}>Delete</button>
            </>
          )}
          {menu.preset && (
            <>
              <button className="accent" onClick={() => { onLoadPreset(menu.preset!); setMenu(null); }}>Load Stack (Layering + Live)</button>
              <button onClick={async () => { const p = menu.preset!; setMenu(null); setVault(await api.savePreset({ ...p, inVault: false })); }}>Unpin from Vault</button>
              <button className="danger" onClick={() => { setDialog({ kind: "delPreset", preset: menu.preset! }); setMenu(null); }}>Delete Preset</button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {dialog?.kind === "delPreset" && (
        <Modal title="Delete preset" onClose={() => setDialog(null)} width={420}>
          <p className="muted">Delete the STACK preset <b>{dialog.preset.name}</b>? Your individual overlays are not affected.</p>
          <div className="modal-foot">
            <button className="btn outline" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Cancel</button>
            <button className="btn danger-solid" onClick={async () => { setVault(await api.deletePreset(dialog.preset.id)); setSelected(null); setDialog(null); }}>Delete</button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog?.kind === "upload" && (
        <UploadModal
          vault={vault}
          defaultGame={(GAMES as readonly string[]).includes(filter) ? filter : ""}
          defaultFolder={vault.folders.includes(filter) ? filter : ""}
          onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
          onSaved={(v) => {
            setVault(v);
            setDialog(null);
            onUpload?.();
          }}
        />
      )}
      {dialog?.kind === "folder" && (
        <FolderDialog vault={vault} onClose={() => setDialog(null)} onDone={(v, name) => { setVault(v); setFilter(name); setDialog(null); }} />
      )}
      {dialog?.kind === "rename" && (
        <RenameDialog item={dialog.item} onClose={() => setDialog(null)} onDone={(v) => { setVault(v); setDialog(null); }} />
      )}
      {dialog?.kind === "move" && (
        <MoveDialog vault={vault} item={dialog.item} onClose={() => setDialog(null)} onDone={(v) => { setVault(v); setDialog(null); }} />
      )}
      {dialog?.kind === "delete" && (
        <Modal title="Delete overlay" onClose={() => setDialog(null)} width={420}>
          <p className="muted">
            Permanently remove <b>{dialog.item.name}</b> and its saved calibration from the Vault?
          </p>
          <div className="modal-foot">
            <button className="btn outline" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Cancel</button>
            <button
              className="btn danger-solid"
              onClick={async () => {
                setVault(await api.deleteItem(dialog.item.id));
                setSelected(null);
                setDialog(null);
              }}
            >
              Delete
            </button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog?.kind === "manageGames" && (
        <Modal title="Manage Game Tabs" onClose={() => setDialog(null)} width={480}>
          <p className="muted small" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
            Select which game categories appear in your Vault navigation bar. Unselected games will be hidden from the tab bar.
          </p>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10, marginBottom: 20 }}>
            {FILTER_GAMES.map((game) => {
              const isVisible = !hiddenGames.includes(game);
              return (
                <label
                  key={game}
                  className="card bracket"
                  style={{
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems: "center",
                    gap: 10,
                    padding: "10px 12px",
                    cursor: "pointer",
                    background: isVisible ? "#121424" : "#08090f",
                    borderColor: isVisible ? "var(--cyan)" : "var(--border)",
                  }}
                >
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={isVisible}
                    onChange={() => toggleGameVisibility(game)}
                  />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: isVisible ? 700 : 400, color: isVisible ? "var(--text)" : "var(--muted)" }}>
                    {game}
                  </span>
                </label>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn small outline"
                onClick={() => saveHiddenGames([])}
              >
                Show All
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn small outline"
                onClick={() => saveHiddenGames(FILTER_GAMES.filter((g) => g !== "Overwatch"))}
              >
                Overwatch Only
              </button>
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="btn small primary" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>
              Done
            </button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function FolderDialog({ vault, onClose, onDone }: { vault: VaultData; onClose: () => void; onDone: (v: VaultData, name: string) => void }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [err, setErr] = useState("");
  const submit = async () => {
    const n = name.trim();
    const reserved = ["all", "favorites", ...GAMES.map((g) => g.toLowerCase())];
    if (!n) return setErr("Enter a folder name.");
    if (reserved.includes(n.toLowerCase()) || vault.folders.some((f) => f.toLowerCase() === n.toLowerCase()))
      return setErr("That name is already used.");
    try {
      onDone(await api.createFolder(n), n);
    } catch (e) {
      setErr(String(e));
    }
  };
  return (
    <Modal title="New Folder" onClose={onClose} width={420}>
      <label className="label">Folder name</label>
      <input className="input full" autoFocus value={name} placeholder="e.g. Ranked crosshairs" onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && submit()} />
      {err && <div className="error">{err}</div>}
      <div className="modal-foot">
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={submit}>Create Folder</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function RenameDialog({ item, onClose, onDone }: { item: VaultItem; onClose: () => void; onDone: (v: VaultData) => void }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(item.name);
  const [tag, setTag] = useState(item.tag ?? "");
  const [favorite, setFavorite] = useState(!!item.favorite);
  const submit = async () => name.trim() && onDone(await api.updateItem(item.id, { name, tag, favorite }));
  return (
    <Modal title="Rename / Tag overlay" onClose={onClose} width={440}>
      <input className="input full" autoFocus value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && submit()} />
      <label className="label">Element type</label>
      <div className="pill-bar wrap">
        {ELEMENT_TAGS.map((t) => (
          <button key={t.id} type="button" className={`pill${tag === t.id ? " active" : ""}`} onClick={() => setTag(tag === t.id ? "" : t.id)}>{t.label}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <label className="check">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={favorite} onChange={(e) => setFavorite(e.target.checked)} />
        <span>Favorite overlay &#x2605;</span>
      </label>
      <div className="modal-foot">
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={submit}>Save</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function MoveDialog({ vault, item, onClose, onDone }: { vault: VaultData; item: VaultItem; onClose: () => void; onDone: (v: VaultData) => void }) {
  const [folder, setFolder] = useState(item.folder ?? "");
  return (
    <Modal title="Change Folder" onClose={onClose} width={420}>
      <label className="label">Custom folder</label>
      <select className="input full" value={folder} onChange={(e) => setFolder(e.target.value)}>
        <option value="">— None —</option>
        {vault.folders.map((f) => <option key={f}>{f}</option>)}
      </select>
      <div className="modal-foot">
        <button className="btn outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={async () => onDone(await api.updateItem(item.id, { folder }))}>Move</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`src/components/ui.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { clamp } from "../util";

export function Toggle({
  checked,
  onChange,
  disabled,
  large,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (v: boolean) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  large?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      disabled={disabled}
      className={`toggle${large ? " large" : ""}${checked ? " on" : ""}`}
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
    >
      <span />
    </button>
  );
}

/** Numeric input that commits on change but tolerates partially typed values. */
export function NumField({
  value,
  onChange,
  min,
  max,
  suffix,
  width = 76,
}: {
  value: number;
  onChange: (v: number) => void;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  suffix?: string;
  width?: number;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = useState(String(Math.round(value * 10) / 10));
  useEffect(() => setText(String(Math.round(value * 10) / 10)), [value]);
  const commit = (s: string) => {
    const n = parseFloat(s);
    if (Number.isFinite(n)) onChange(clamp(n, min, max));
    else setText(String(value));
  };
  return (
    <span className="numfield" style={{ width }}>
      <input
        value={text}
        inputMode="decimal"
        onChange={(e) => {
          setText(e.target.value);
          const n = parseFloat(e.target.value);
          if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= min && n <= max) onChange(n);
        }}
        onBlur={(e) => commit(e.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && commit(text)}
      />
      {suffix && <em>{suffix}</em>}
    </span>
  );
}

export function SliderRow({
  label,
  value,
  min,
  max,
  suffix,
  onChange,
  extra,
  sliderClassName,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  suffix: string;
  onChange: (v: number) => void;
  extra?: ReactNode;
  sliderClassName?: string;
}) {
  const pct = ((clamp(value, min, max) - min) / (max - min)) * 100;
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <div className="field-head">
        <label>{label}</label>
        <NumField value={value} onChange={onChange} min={min} max={max * 2} suffix={suffix} />
      </div>
      <input
        type="range"
        className={sliderClassName}
        min={min}
        max={max}
        value={clamp(value, min, max)}
        style={{ ["--pct" as string]: `${pct}%` }}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(Number(e.target.value))}
      />
      {extra}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Modal({
  title,
  onClose,
  children,
  width = 520,
}: {
  title: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
  width?: number;
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const h = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", h);
  }, [onClose]);
  return (
    <div className="modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={onClose}>
      <div className="modal card bracket" style={{ width }} onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="modal-head">
          <h3>{title}</h3>
          <button className="icon-btn" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/layers.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ROLES,
  fxOf,
  type Layer,
  type OverlayState,
  type Role,
  type SceneLayer,
  type VaultData,
  type VaultItem,
} from "./types";

export const roleBadge = (r: Role) => ROLES.find((x) => x.id === r)?.badge ?? "CUSTOM";

export function roleFromTag(tag?: string | null): Role {
  switch (tag) {
    case "ult": return "ult";
    case "map": return "map";
    case "reticle": return "crosshair";
    case "hud":
    case "health": return "base";
    default: return "custom";
  }
}

export const uid = () =>
  (crypto.randomUUID?.() ?? Math.random().toString(36).slice(2) + Date.now().toString(36));

export function newLayer(item: VaultItem, at?: { x: number; y: number }): Layer {
  const cal = item.transform;
  return {
    id: uid(),
    vaultItemId: item.id,
    name: item.name,
    role: roleFromTag(item.tag),
    zIndex: 0,
    visible: true,
    locked: false,
    x: at?.x ?? cal?.x ?? 0,
    y: at?.y ?? cal?.y ?? 0,
    scale: cal?.scale ?? 100,
    opacity: cal?.opacity ?? 100,
    hue: cal?.hue ?? 0,
  };
}

/** Stack arrays are top-first; persisted zIndex is higher = in front. */
export const withZ = (layers: Layer[]): Layer[] =>
  layers.map((l, i) => ({ ...l, zIndex: layers.length - 1 - i }));

export const fromPreset = (layers: Layer[]): Layer[] =>
  [...layers].sort((a, b) => b.zIndex - a.zIndex);

/**
 * Visible layers resolved to absolute paths, ordered back-to-front for drawing.
 * `factor` scales the authored canvas to the real monitor (resolution switcher).
 */
export function toScene(layers: Layer[], vault: VaultData, factor = 1): SceneLayer[] {
  const byId = new Map(vault.items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
  const out: SceneLayer[] = [];
  for (const l of [...layers].reverse()) {
    const it = byId.get(l.vaultItemId);
    if (!l.visible || !it) continue;
    out.push({
      path: `${vault.assetsDir}\\${it.file}`,
      width: it.width,
      height: it.height,
      transform: {
        x: l.x * factor,
        y: l.y * factor,
        scale: l.scale * factor,
        opacity: l.opacity,
        hue: l.hue,
        brightness: 100,
        contrast: 100,
      },
      fx: fxOf(l),
    });
  }
  return out;
}

export type EffectiveOverlay = OverlayState;

/** Visible (post-crop) rectangle of a layer in centre-origin canvas pixels. */
export interface Box {
  l: number;
  t: number;
  w: number;
  h: number;
}

export function layerBox(l: Layer, iw: number, ih: number): Box {
  const fx = fxOf(l);
  const w = (iw * l.scale) / 100;
  const h = (ih * l.scale) / 100;
  // crop is defined on the source image; flipping mirrors the whole image about its centre
  const cl = fx.flipX ? fx.cropR : fx.cropL;
  const cr = fx.flipX ? fx.cropL : fx.cropR;
  const ct = fx.flipY ? fx.cropB : fx.cropT;
  const cb = fx.flipY ? fx.cropT : fx.cropB;
  return {
    l: l.x - w / 2 + (w * cl) / 100,
    t: l.y - h / 2 + (h * ct) / 100,
    w: (w * (100 - cl - cr)) / 100,
    h: (h * (100 - ct - cb)) / 100,
  };
}

export interface SnapResult {
  dx: number;
  dy: number;
  /** guide lines in top-left canvas pixels */
  xs: number[];
  ys: number[];
}

function snapAxis(cands: number[], targets: number[], thr: number) {
  let best: { d: number; off: number } | null = null;
  for (const c of cands)
    for (const t of targets) {
      const d = Math.abs(t - c);
      if (d <= thr && (!best || d < best.d)) best = { d, off: t - c };
    }
  return best ? best.off : 0;
}

/**
 * Photoshop-style smart guides: snap a moving box's edges/centre to the canvas
 * edges/centre and to every other visible layer's edges/centre.
 */
export function snapBox(b: Box, W: number, H: number, others: Box[], thr: number, grid = 0): SnapResult {
  const tx = [-W / 2, 0, W / 2];
  const ty = [-H / 2, 0, H / 2];
  for (const o of others) {
    tx.push(o.l, o.l + o.w / 2, o.l + o.w);
    ty.push(o.t, o.t + o.h / 2, o.t + o.h);
  }
  let dx = 0;
  let dy = 0;
  if (grid > 0) {
    dx = Math.round(b.l / grid) * grid - b.l;
    dy = Math.round(b.t / grid) * grid - b.t;
  }
  const ox = snapAxis([b.l, b.l + b.w / 2, b.l + b.w], tx, thr);
  const oy = snapAxis([b.t, b.t + b.h / 2, b.t + b.h], ty, thr);
  if (ox) dx = ox;
  if (oy) dy = oy;
  const nl = b.l + dx;
  const nt = b.t + dy;
  const xs: number[] = [];
  const ys: number[] = [];
  for (const c of [nl, nl + b.w / 2, nl + b.w]) if (tx.some((t) => Math.abs(t - c) < 0.5)) xs.push(c + W / 2);
  for (const c of [nt, nt + b.h / 2, nt + b.h]) if (ty.some((t) => Math.abs(t - c) < 0.5)) ys.push(c + H / 2);
  return { dx, dy, xs, ys };
}
```

`src/overlay.tsx`:

```tsx
import { StrictMode, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import { listen } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
import { convertFileSrc } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { api } from "./api";
import type { OverlayPayload, SceneLayer } from "./types";
import "./styles.css";

const images = new Map<string, Promise<HTMLImageElement>>();

/** Decode each file once; later redraws reuse the decoded bitmap. */
function loadImage(path: string): Promise<HTMLImageElement> {
  let p = images.get(path);
  if (!p) {
    const img = new Image();
    img.src = convertFileSrc(path);
    p = img.decode().then(() => img);
    p.catch(() => images.delete(path));
    images.set(path, p);
  }
  return p;
}

/**
 * The transparent click-through window. Whether it shows one PNG or a whole
 * layer stack, everything is composited onto ONE canvas, and only when the
 * state changes.
 */
function Overlay() {
  const [p, setP] = useState<OverlayPayload | null>(null);
  const [dpr, setDpr] = useState(window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const canvas = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const token = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.overlayGet().then(setP);
    const un = listen<OverlayPayload>("overlay-state", (e) => setP(e.payload));
    const onResize = () => setDpr(window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    window.addEventListener("resize", onResize);
    return () => {
      un.then((f) => f());
      window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const c = canvas.current;
    if (!c || !p) return;
    const my = ++token.current;
    const W = p.monitorW;
    const H = p.monitorH;
    const layers: SceneLayer[] = p.state.scene.length
      ? p.state.scene
      : p.state.path
        ? [{ path: p.state.path, width: p.state.width, height: p.state.height, transform: p.state.transform }]
        : [];

    if (!p.visible || !layers.length) {
      c.width = 1; // release the backbuffer while hidden
      c.height = 1;
      return;
    }
    Promise.all(layers.map((l) => loadImage(l.path).catch(() => null))).then((imgs) => {
      if (my !== token.current) return; // superseded by a newer state
      c.width = W; // also clears
      c.height = H;
      const ctx = c.getContext("2d")!;
      ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = "high";
      // layers arrive back-to-front
      layers.forEach((l, i) => {
        const img = imgs[i];
        if (!img) return;
        const t = l.transform;
        const w = (l.width * t.scale) / 100;
        const h = (l.height * t.scale) / 100;
        const f: string[] = [];
        if (!p.lowRes) {
          if (t.hue) f.push(`hue-rotate(${t.hue}deg)`);
          if (t.brightness !== 100) f.push(`brightness(${t.brightness}%)`);
          if (t.contrast !== 100) f.push(`contrast(${t.contrast}%)`);
        }
        ctx.globalAlpha = t.opacity / 100;
        ctx.filter = f.length ? f.join(" ") : "none";
        const fx = { cropT: 0, cropR: 0, cropB: 0, cropL: 0, flipX: false, flipY: false, blend: "normal", ...(l.fx ?? {}) };
        const iw = img.naturalWidth || l.width;
        const ih = img.naturalHeight || l.height;
        const vw = Math.max(0.01, 1 - (fx.cropL + fx.cropR) / 100);
        const vh = Math.max(0.01, 1 - (fx.cropT + fx.cropB) / 100);
        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = (fx.blend && fx.blend !== "normal" ? fx.blend : "source-over") as GlobalCompositeOperation;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(W / 2 + t.x, H / 2 + t.y);
        ctx.scale(fx.flipX ? -1 : 1, fx.flipY ? -1 : 1);
        ctx.drawImage(
          img,
          (iw * fx.cropL) / 100,
          (ih * fx.cropT) / 100,
          iw * vw,
          ih * vh,
          -w / 2 + (w * fx.cropL) / 100,
          -h / 2 + (h * fx.cropT) / 100,
          w * vw,
          h * vh,
        );
        ctx.restore();
        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
      });
    });
  }, [p]);

  const W = p?.monitorW ?? 1;
  const H = p?.monitorH ?? 1;
  const isChroma = Boolean(
    p?.state.transform?.chromaCycle ||
    p?.state.scene?.some((l) => l.transform?.chromaCycle)
  );
  const chromaDuration = p?.state.transform?.chromaSpeed || 6;
  const shouldAnimate = isChroma && Boolean(p?.visible);

  // Smooth real-time rainbow hue-cycle animation directly on the canvas element
  useEffect(() => {
    const c = canvas.current;
    if (!c) return;
    if (!shouldAnimate) {
      c.style.filter = "";
      return;
    }
    let raf: number;
    const start = performance.now();
    const periodMs = (chromaDuration || 6) * 1000;
    const tick = (now: number) => {
      const deg = (((now - start) % periodMs) / periodMs) * 360;
      c.style.filter = `hue-rotate(${deg.toFixed(1)}deg)`;
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      c.style.filter = "";
    };
  }, [shouldAnimate, chromaDuration]);

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvas}
      style={{
        position: "absolute",
        left: 0,
        top: 0,
        width: W / dpr,
        height: H / dpr,
        pointerEvents: "none",
      }}
    />
  );
}

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <Overlay />
  </StrictMode>,
);

```

`src/types.ts`:

```ts
export const GAMES = [
  "Valorant",
  "Overwatch",
  "Call of Duty",
  "Fortnite",
  "Dead by Daylight",
  "Apex Legends",
  "Marvel Rivals",
  "Other",
] as const;

/** Order of the game pills in the Vault filter bar. */
export const FILTER_GAMES = [
  "Overwatch",
  "Valorant",
  "Call of Duty",
  "Fortnite",
  "Dead by Daylight",
  "Apex Legends",
  "Marvel Rivals",
  "Other",
] as const;

export type Tab = "display" | "layering" | "vault" | "settings";

export interface Transform {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scale: number;
  opacity: number;
  hue: number;
  brightness: number;
  contrast: number;
  chromaCycle?: boolean;
  chromaSpeed?: number;
}

export const DEFAULT_TRANSFORM: Transform = {
  x: 0,
  y: 0,
  scale: 100,
  opacity: 100,
  hue: 0,
  brightness: 100,
  contrast: 100,
  chromaCycle: false,
  chromaSpeed: 6,
};

export interface VaultItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  game: string;
  folder: string | null;
  file: string;
  thumb: string | null;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  createdAt: number;
  transform: Transform | null;
  tag?: string | null;
  favorite?: boolean;
}

export type Role = "base" | "ult" | "map" | "ability" | "crosshair" | "custom";

export const ROLES: { id: Role; label: string; badge: string }[] = [
  { id: "base", label: "Base HUD", badge: "BASE" },
  { id: "ult", label: "Ult Icon", badge: "ULT" },
  { id: "map", label: "Map Overlay", badge: "MAP" },
  { id: "ability", label: "Ability Bar", badge: "ABILITY" },
  { id: "crosshair", label: "Crosshair", badge: "RETICLE" },
  { id: "custom", label: "Custom", badge: "CUSTOM" },
];

export const ELEMENT_TAGS = [
  { id: "ult", label: "Ult Icon" },
  { id: "map", label: "Minimap" },
  { id: "health", label: "Health Bar" },
  { id: "hud", label: "Full HUD" },
  { id: "reticle", label: "Reticle" },
] as const;

/** One modular element in a composite stack (also the persisted preset layer). */
export interface Layer {
  id: string;
  vaultItemId: string;
  name: string;
  role: Role;
  zIndex: number;
  visible: boolean;
  locked: boolean;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scale: number;
  opacity: number;
  hue: number;
  /** non-destructive crop / flip / blend / link group (optional for old presets) */
  fx?: LayerFx;
}

/** Crop values are % of the source image trimmed from each edge. */
export interface LayerFx {
  cropT: number;
  cropR: number;
  cropB: number;
  cropL: number;
  flipX: boolean;
  flipY: boolean;
  blend: string;
  group: string | null;
}

export const DEFAULT_FX: LayerFx = {
  cropT: 0,
  cropR: 0,
  cropB: 0,
  cropL: 0,
  flipX: false,
  flipY: false,
  blend: "normal",
  group: null,
};

export const fxOf = (l: { fx?: LayerFx }): LayerFx => ({ ...DEFAULT_FX, ...(l.fx ?? {}) });

export const BLEND_MODES = [
  { id: "normal", label: "Normal" },
  { id: "screen", label: "Screen (drops black)" },
  { id: "color-dodge", label: "Color Dodge (neon glow)" },
  { id: "overlay", label: "Overlay" },
  { id: "soft-light", label: "Soft Light" },
  { id: "lighten", label: "Lighten" },
  { id: "multiply", label: "Multiply" },
] as const;

export const LINK_GROUPS = ["A", "B", "C", "D"] as const;

export interface CanvasRes {
  w: number;
  h: number;
}

export const RES_GROUPS: { label: string; items: { label: string; w: number; h: number }[] }[] = [
  {
    label: "16:9",
    items: [
      { label: "720p HD", w: 1280, h: 720 },
      { label: "900p", w: 1600, h: 900 },
      { label: "1080p FHD", w: 1920, h: 1080 },
      { label: "1440p QHD (2K)", w: 2560, h: 1440 },
      { label: "2160p UHD (4K)", w: 3840, h: 2160 },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "21:9 Ultrawide",
    items: [
      { label: "UW 720p", w: 1720, h: 720 },
      { label: "UW-FHD", w: 2560, h: 1080 },
      { label: "UW-QHD (2K)", w: 3440, h: 1440 },
      { label: "UW 4K-lite", w: 3840, h: 1600 },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "16:10 / 4:3 / Stretched",
    items: [
      { label: "16:10 WUXGA", w: 1920, h: 1200 },
      { label: "16:10 WQXGA", w: 2560, h: 1600 },
      { label: "4:3 Stretched 1080", w: 1440, h: 1080 },
      { label: "4:3 Stretched 960", w: 1280, h: 960 },
    ],
  },
];

export const ALL_RES = RES_GROUPS.flatMap((g) => g.items);

export interface CompositePreset {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  game: string | null;
  layers: Layer[];
  refW: number;
  refH: number;
  inVault: boolean;
  createdAt: number;
}

export interface SceneLayer {
  path: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  transform: Transform;
  fx?: LayerFx;
}

export interface VaultData {
  items: VaultItem[];
  folders: string[];
  presets: CompositePreset[];
  rootDir: string;
  assetsDir: string;
  thumbsDir: string;
}

export interface Settings {
  hotkeyToggle: string;
  hotkeyNext: string;
  hotkeyPrev: string;
  hideFromCapture: boolean;
  targetMode: "topmost" | "game";
  targetExe: string;
  autoGameSwitch?: boolean;
  autoHideUnfocused?: boolean;
  gameProfiles?: Record<string, string>;
  startWithWindows: boolean;
  closeToTray: boolean;
  hardwareAcceleration: boolean;
  lowResource: boolean;
  layerHotkeys: Record<string, string>;
  checkUpdatesOnStartup?: boolean;
}

export type { UsageStats } from "./usage";

export type UpdateStatus =
  | { status: "idle" }
  | { status: "checking" }
  | { status: "upToDate"; currentVersion: string }
  | { status: "available"; version: string; currentVersion: string; body?: string; date?: string }
  | { status: "downloading"; version: string; progress: number; downloaded: number; total?: number }
  | { status: "downloaded"; version: string }
  | { status: "error"; message: string };

export interface OverlayState {
  enabled: boolean;
  itemId: string | null;
  path: string | null;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  transform: Transform;
  monitor: number;
  /** when non-empty the composite is drawn instead of `path` */
  scene: SceneLayer[];
}

export const DEFAULT_OVERLAY: OverlayState = {
  enabled: false,
  itemId: null,
  path: null,
  width: 0,
  height: 0,
  transform: DEFAULT_TRANSFORM,
  monitor: 0,
  scene: [],
};

export interface OverlayPayload {
  state: OverlayState;
  visible: boolean;
  monitorW: number;
  monitorH: number;
  lowRes: boolean;
}

export interface OverlayStatus {
  enabled: boolean;
  visible: boolean;
  gameRunning: boolean;
}

export interface MonitorInfo {
  index: number;
  name: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scale: number;
  refreshHz: number;
}

export const GAME_EXES: { label: string; exe: string }[] = [

}

export const KNOWN_GAMES: KnownGame[] = [
```

`src/usage.ts`:

```ts
import type { CompositePreset, VaultData, VaultItem } from "./types";

export interface UsageStats {
  totalAppTimeSeconds: number;
  totalSwaps: number;
  totalUploads: number;
  overlayTime: Record<string, number>;
}

export const STORAGE_KEY = "iris_usage_stats";

export const DEFAULT_USAGE: UsageStats = {
  totalAppTimeSeconds: 0,
  totalSwaps: 0,
  totalUploads: 0,
  overlayTime: {},
};

export function loadUsage(): UsageStats {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return { ...DEFAULT_USAGE };
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return {
      totalAppTimeSeconds: Number(parsed.totalAppTimeSeconds) || 0,
      totalSwaps: Number(parsed.totalSwaps) || 0,
      totalUploads: Number(parsed.totalUploads) || 0,
      overlayTime:
        typeof parsed.overlayTime === "object" && parsed.overlayTime !== null
          ? parsed.overlayTime
          : {},
    };
  } catch {
    return { ...DEFAULT_USAGE };
  }
}

export function saveUsage(stats: UsageStats) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(stats));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

export function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
  if (!seconds || seconds <= 0) return "0m";
  const hrs = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = Math.floor(seconds % 60);

  if (hrs >= 24) {
    const days = Math.floor(hrs / 24);
    const remHrs = hrs % 24;
    return `${days}d ${remHrs}h`;
  }
  if (hrs > 0) {
    return `${hrs}h ${mins}m`;
  }
  if (mins > 0) {
    return `${mins}m ${secs}s`;
  }
  return `${secs}s`;
}

export interface FavOverlayInfo {
  name: string;
  timeFormatted: string;
  timeSeconds: number;
  sub: string;
  isStarred: boolean;
  item?: VaultItem | null;
  preset?: CompositePreset | null;
  tag?: string | null;
  isRecentFallback?: boolean;
}

export function computeFavoriteOverlay(
  vault: VaultData | null | undefined,
  usageStats: UsageStats | undefined,
  activeItemId?: string | null
): FavOverlayInfo {
  if (!vault || (!vault.items.length && !vault.presets.length)) {
    return {
      name: "None yet",
      timeFormatted: "0m",
      timeSeconds: 0,
      sub: "No overlays uploaded",
      isStarred: false,
      item: null,
      preset: null,
      tag: null,
      isRecentFallback: false,
    };
  }

  const times = usageStats?.overlayTime ?? {};
  let bestTime = 0;
  let bestName = "";
  let bestSub = "";
  let isStarred = false;
  let bestItem: VaultItem | null = null;
  let bestPreset: CompositePreset | null = null;
  let bestTag: string | null = null;

  // 1. Scan single vault items
  for (const item of vault.items) {
    const t = times[item.id] ?? 0;
    if (t > bestTime) {
      bestTime = t;
      bestName = item.name;
      bestSub = item.game;
      isStarred = !!item.favorite;
      bestItem = item;
      bestPreset = null;
      bestTag = item.tag ?? null;
    }
  }

  // 2. Scan composite stack presets
  for (const p of vault.presets) {
    const t = times[`preset:${p.id}`] ?? times[p.id] ?? 0;
    if (t > bestTime) {
      bestTime = t;
      bestName = `Loadout: ${p.name}`;
      bestSub = p.game || "Composite Stack";
      isStarred = true;
      bestPreset = p;
      bestItem = null;
      bestTag = "stack";
    }
  }

  let isRecentFallback = false;

  // 3. Fallback to starred item, active item, or first item if no item has accumulated time yet
  if (bestTime === 0) {
    isRecentFallback = true;
    const starred = vault.items.find((i) => i.favorite);
    const active = activeItemId ? vault.items.find((i) => i.id === activeItemId) : null;
    const fallback = starred || active || vault.items[0];

    if (fallback) {
      bestName = fallback.name;
      bestSub = fallback.game;
      bestTime = times[fallback.id] ?? 0;
      isStarred = !!fallback.favorite;
      bestItem = fallback;
      bestPreset = null;
      bestTag = fallback.tag ?? null;
    } else if (vault.presets.length > 0) {
      bestName = `Loadout: ${vault.presets[0].name}`;
      bestSub = vault.presets[0].game || "Composite Stack";
      bestTime = times[`preset:${vault.presets[0].id}`] ?? 0;
      isStarred = true;
      bestPreset = vault.presets[0];
      bestItem = null;
      bestTag = "stack";
    }
  }

  return {
    name: (isStarred && !bestName.startsWith("★") ? "★ " : "") + bestName,
    timeFormatted: formatDuration(bestTime),
    timeSeconds: bestTime,
    sub: bestSub,
    isStarred,
    item: bestItem,
    preset: bestPreset,
    tag: bestTag,
    isRecentFallback,
  };
}
```

`src/util.ts`:

```ts
import type { VaultItem } from "./types";
import { FILTER_GAMES } from "./types";

export type SortKey = "new" | "name" | "game";

export function resolutionTag(w: number, h: number): string {
  if (h >= 2160) return "4K";
  if (h >= 1440) return "2K";
  if (h >= 1080) return "1080p";
  return `${w}x${h}`;
}

export function gameClass(game: string): string {
  return "g-" + game.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]+/g, "-");
}

export function filterItems(
  items: VaultItem[],
  filter: string,
  query = "",
  sort: SortKey = "new",
): VaultItem[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const isGame = (FILTER_GAMES as readonly string[]).includes(filter);
  const out = items.filter((i) => {
    if (filter === "Favorites") {
      if (!i.favorite) return false;
    } else if (filter !== "All") {
      if (isGame ? i.game !== filter : i.folder !== filter) return false;
    }
    return !q || i.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || i.game.toLowerCase().includes(q);
  });
  out.sort((a, b) =>
    sort === "name"
      ? a.name.localeCompare(b.name)
      : sort === "game"
        ? a.game.localeCompare(b.game) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name)
        : b.createdAt - a.createdAt,
  );
  return out;
}

export function monitorLabel(m: { index: number; width: number; height: number; refreshHz: number }) {
  return `Display ${m.index + 1}: ${m.width}x${m.height}${m.refreshHz ? ` @ ${m.refreshHz}Hz` : ""}`;
}

export const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));

/** Decode an image, verify it contains real transparency, and build a thumbnail. */
export async function analyzeImage(file: File) {
  const bmp = await createImageBitmap(file);
  const k = Math.min(1, 1024 / Math.max(bmp.width, bmp.height));
  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
  c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bmp.width * k));
  c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bmp.height * k));
  const ctx = c.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true })!;
  ctx.drawImage(bmp, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;
  let hasAlpha = false;
  for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 4) {
    if (d[i] < 250) {
      hasAlpha = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  return { bmp, width: bmp.width, height: bmp.height, hasAlpha };
}

export async function makeThumbnail(bmp: ImageBitmap): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const k = Math.min(1, 480 / bmp.width, 270 / bmp.height);
  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
  c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bmp.width * k));
  c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bmp.height * k));
  c.getContext("2d")!.drawImage(bmp, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  const blob = await new Promise<Blob>((res, rej) =>
    c.toBlob((b) => (b ? res(b) : rej(new Error("thumbnail failed"))), "image/png"),
  );
  return new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());
}
```
